# ASTRA YOGA ENGINE: ARCHITECTURAL RULEBOOK & BREAKER SENSITIVITY MATRIX

This document governs `jyotish/yogas/models.py`, `jyotish/yogas/breakers.py`, and all downstream yoga detection modules. Every engineer and AI auditor must enforce these boundaries to prevent cross-yoga interference.

---

## 1. HEMISPHERE A PURITY LAW
`jyotish/yogas/` resides strictly in **Hemisphere A (Foundational Calculus)**:
1. **Pure Data Only:** Modules must return `YogaInstance` and `YogaBreakerDetail` objects containing floats, ints, lists, and classical Sanskrit terms (*Uccha*, *Nīca*, *Svakṣetra*, *Virūpas*, *Rūpas*, *Kendra*, *Trikoṇa*, *Duṣṭhāna*, *Triṣaḍāya*).
2. **Zero UI Formatting:** Never embed HTML tags, CSS classes, hex color codes, or UI table definitions inside `models.py`, `breakers.py`, or any yoga module.
3. **Dependency Direction:** `jyotish/yogas/` must NEVER import from Hemisphere B (`planetary_evaluation/` or `report/`).

---

## 2. STRICT BOUNDARIES OF `models.py`
`models.py` is a **pure schema and serialization module**:
- **Allowed:**
  - `YogaCategory(str, Enum)` and `YogaStatus(str, Enum)` definitions.
  - `@dataclass` definitions (`YogaBreakerDetail`, `YogaInstance`).
  - Null-safe, clamped `[0.0, 100.0]` `.to_dict()` serialization methods.
- **Forbidden:**
  - Astronomical math, chart dictionary traversal, or house/sign lookups.
  - Yoga detection rules or penalty calculations.

---

## 3. TWO-LAYER ARCHITECTURE OF `breakers.py`

Classical Jyotish is not a uniform checklist where every yoga reacts to the same friction in the same way. Therefore, `breakers.py` is partitioned into **Layer 1 (Universal Calculators)** and **Layer 2 (Opt-In Breaker Auditors)**.

### Layer 1: Universal Calculators (Used by ALL Yogas)
These pure functions extract chart state and perform geometric math without judging whether a placement is "good" or "bad":
- `get_lagna_sign(chart) -> str` (returns `""` if missing; never fabricates `"Aries"`).
- `get_planets_data(chart) -> Dict[str, Any]`
- `get_sign_of_planet(chart, planet) -> str`
- `get_house_of_planet(chart, planet) -> int` (`1..12` whole-sign house, or `0` if unresolved).
- `get_aspect_score(chart, giver, receiver) -> float` (Sphuṭa Dṛṣṭi clamped to `[0.0, 60.0]` Virūpas; `>= 45.0` is Palpable, `30.0–44.9` is Marginal).
- `get_house_rulers(chart) -> Dict[int, List[str]]` and `_first_ruler(house_rulers, house) -> Optional[str]`.
- `audit_neecha_bhanga_condition_3(...)` and `audit_neecha_bhanga(...)` (with `!= "Moon"` tautology guards and Mercury self-lordship deduplication).
- `resolve_yoga_score_and_status(pre_breaker_score, breakers, score_cap=100.0) -> Tuple[float, YogaStatus]`.

### Layer 2: Opt-In Breaker Auditors (Subscribed per Yoga Sensitivity Profile)
These functions calculate penalties for specific structural flaws. **Downstream yoga modules must ONLY call the auditors that match their category's sensitivity profile below:**
1. `audit_trishadaya_interference(yoga_planets, chart, is_supreme_pairing=False, exclude_houses=())`
2. `audit_combustion(yoga_planets, chart, deep_only=False)`
3. `audit_dispositor_vitality(yoga_planets, chart)`
4. `audit_dusthana_placement(yoga_houses, category_name)`
5. `audit_shadbala_muscle(yoga_planets, chart)`

---

## 4. MASTER BREAKER SENSITIVITY MATRIX (ANTI-INTERFERENCE RULES)

Never apply Layer 2 breakers blindly across all yogas. Follow this exact matrix for each module:

| Yoga Category / Module | `audit_trishadaya` (3, 6, 11) | `audit_dusthana` (6, 8, 12) | `audit_combustion` | `audit_dispositor` | `audit_shadbala` | Special / Inverse Breaker Rules |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **1. `MAHAPURUSHA`** (`pancha_mahapurusha.py`) | YES | YES *(Chandra-only in 6/8/12)* | YES | YES *(if exalted)* | YES | Cap pre-breaker score at `100.0`; gate `PURE` on no breaker `>= 20.0`. |
| **2. `NEECHABHANGA`** (`neechabhanga.py`) | NO | Cap score `<= 65.0` in 6/8/12 | YES | NO *(dispositor is rescuer)* | YES | Kendra/Koṇa (`1,4,5,7,9,10`) upgrades to *Nīcabhaṅga Rāja Yoga*; `6,8,12` is Simple Cancellation. |
| **3. `RAJA`** (`raja_yogas.py`) | YES (`is_supreme_pairing=True` for 9+10) | YES | YES | YES | YES | Distinguish True *Rāja Yoga* (9L+10L) from *Śaṅkha Yoga* (other Kendra+Koṇa pairs). |
| **4. `PARIVARTANA`** (`parivartana.py`) | YES *(Mahā only)* | NO *(classified by pair type)* | YES | NO *(mutual hosts)* | YES | 28 *Mahā* (auspicious), 8 *Khala* (3rd house), 30 *Dainya* (6, 8, 12). Resolve dual-lordship via actual occupied houses (`anyonyam`). |
| **5. `VIPARITA`** (`viparita.py`) | NO | **FORBIDDEN** *(requires 6, 8, 12!)* | YES | YES | YES | **Inverse Breaker:** Penalize if a Kendra/Trikoṇa lord conjoins or palpably aspects the Duṣṭhāna lord. |
| **6. `DHANA`** (`dhana_daridrya.py`) | `exclude_houses=(11,)` *(11L is wealth giver!)* | YES | YES | YES | YES | **NEVER penalize the 11th lord** in Dhana yogas; penalize 6/8/12 financial leakage. |
| **7. `DARIDRYA` & `CHANDAL`** (`dhana_daridrya.py`, `chandal_yogas.py`) | NO | **FORBIDDEN** *(affliction yoga)* | NO | NO | NO | Affliction yogas: Benefic aspect/Neecha Bhanga acts as a **mitigation** (moving status to `RESCUED`), never `"Pure & Eminent"`. |
| **8. `LUNAR`** (`lunar_solar_yogas.py`: *Sunaphā, Anaphā, Durudharā, Kemadruma*) | NO | NO | YES *(on 2nd/12th planets)* | YES *(on 2nd/12th planets)* | YES | *Kemadruma* uses its dedicated **4-Tier Bhaṅga Hierarchy** (Lagna Kendra, Moon Kendra, Modality, D9). |
| **9. `SOLAR`** (`lunar_solar_yogas.py`: *Veśi, Vāsi, Ubhayacarī, Budhāditya*) | NO | YES *(for Budhāditya)* | `deep_only=True` (`< 3.0°` only) | YES | YES | Moderate proximity (`3.0°–8.0°`) is astronomically normal for Solar yogas; only deep combustion (`< 3.0°`) penalizes. |
| **10. `KARTARI`** (`kartari.py`) | NO | NO | NO | NO | NO | Check benefic/malefic purity of 2nd and 12th houses from Lagna/Moon/Sun and opposing aspects (`Suśubha`). |
| **11. `CONTEXTUAL`** (`contextual_yogas.py`: *Sāṅkhya, Ādhi, Mahābhāgya*) | NO | **FORBIDDEN** *(Ādhi requires 6, 7, 8!)* | YES *(Ādhi only)* | NO | YES *(Ādhi only)* | **Sāṅkhya Yogas** (*Phaladīpikā* 6.39) stand completely alone—apply **ZERO** breakers to Sāṅkhya. |
| **12. `DISPOSITOR_ROOT`** (`dispositor_root_yogas.py`: *Kāhala, Parvata, Puṣkala*) | YES | YES | YES | YES | YES | Prevent "1st Lord in 1st House" trivial self-dispositor loophole from scoring equal to multi-planet *Parvata*. |
| **13. `CHARACTER`** (`character_growth_yogas.py`: *Kesari, Śakaṭa, Vasumatī, Amala*) | `exclude_houses=(3,6,11)` for *Vasumatī* | YES *(Kesari)* | YES | YES | YES | *Vasumatī* requires Upacayas (`3,6,10,11`); *Śakaṭa* is cancelled when Moon is in Lagna Kendra (`1,4,7,10`). |
| **14. `DEITY`** (`deity_yogas.py`: *Trimūrti, Tridevī, Mālā*) | NO | **FORBIDDEN** on *Aśubha Mālā* (`6,8,12`) & *Śubha Mālā* (`5,6,7`) | YES *(Trimūrti / Tridevī)* | NO | YES *(Trimūrti / Tridevī)* | *Trimūrti* & *Tridevī* already mandate own/exalted/friendly dignity + Kendra/Koṇa placement. |
| **15. `POWER` & `BHAVA`** (`power_yogas.py`, `bhava_yogas.py`) | NO | Built into formation rules | YES *(on positive Bhava lords)* | NO | YES | For the 24 Bhava Yogas, Houses `6, 8, 12` invert positive/negative conditions (*Harṣa, Sarala, Vimala* vs *Asura, Musala*). |

---

## 5. SCORING & STATUS INVARIANTS
1. **Pre-Breaker Score Cap:** Always cap positive confluence scores at `100.0` *before* subtracting breaker penalties (`resolve_yoga_score_and_status`).
2. **Major Breaker Gate:** Any yoga with an active breaker of `penalty >= 20.0` can NEVER receive `YogaStatus.PURE` ("Pure & Eminent"), even if its net score is `>= 75.0`.
3. **Affliction Yoga Semantics:** Negative/affliction combinations (*Kemadruma*, *Śakaṭa*, *Daridrya*, *Chāṇḍāla*, *Pāpa Kartarī*, *Dainya Parivartana*, Converse Bhava Yogas) must use `YogaStatus.STAINED` when active/unmitigated and `YogaStatus.RESCUED` or `YogaStatus.BROKEN` when cancelled—never `YogaStatus.PURE` ("Pure & Eminent").

"""
jyotish/yogas/bhava_yogas.py
The 24 Bhava Yogas — 12 Auspicious (Śubha) and 12 Converse (Aśubha) House Yogas.
Scripture: Phaladeepika Ch. 6, Verses 44–70.
"""

from typing import List, Dict, Any

    YogaInstance, YogaCategory, YogaStatus, YogaBreakerDetail
)

    get_house_of_planet, get_sign_of_planet, get_house_rulers, get_planets_data,
    get_lagna_sign, get_aspect_score, _first_ruler,
    audit_combustion, audit_shadbala_muscle, audit_neecha_bhanga,
    resolve_yoga_score_and_status,
    OWN_SIGNS, EXALTATION_SIGNS, DEBILITATION_SIGNS,
    ASPECT_PALPABLE_THRESHOLD
)

KENDRA_TRIKONA_HOUSES = (1, 4, 5, 7, 9, 10)
TRIKONA_HOUSES = (1, 5, 9)
DUSTHANA_HOUSES = (6, 8, 12)
BENEFICS = ("Jupiter", "Venus", "Mercury")
MALEFICS = ("Saturn", "Mars", "Rahu", "Ketu", "Sun")

# The 24 Bhava definitions from Phaladeepika 6.44-70
# Note on Houses 6, 8, 12 Inversion Paradox (Phaladeepika 6.50, 6.52, 6.56 vs 6.63, 6.65, 6.69):
# - For Houses 1-5, 7, 9-11: Lord in Kendra/Trikona is Auspicious; Lord in Dusthana (6,8,12) is Converse (STAINED/RESCUED).
# - For Houses 8 & 12: Lord in Kendra/Trikona forms Asura (6.52) / Musala (6.56) (Converse/Challenging — STAINED/RESCUED, never PURE!),
#   whereas Lord in Dusthana (6,8,12) forms Sarala (6.65) / Vimala (6.69) (Auspicious Reversal — deduplicated with viparita.py).

def detect_bhava_yogas(chart: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[YogaInstance]:
    """Scans chart for the Bhava Auspicious (Śubha) and Converse (Aśubha) Yogas."""
    detected: List[YogaInstance] = []
    lagna_sign = get_lagna_sign(chart)
    planets_data = get_planets_data(chart)
    house_rulers = get_house_rulers(chart)

    if not lagna_sign or not planets_data:
        return detected

    planet_houses = {p: get_house_of_planet(chart, p) for p in planets_data}
    trikona_lords = {
        _first_ruler(house_rulers, th)
        for th in TRIKONA_HOUSES
        if _first_ruler(house_rulers, th)
    }

    for h_num in range(1, 13):
        lord = _first_ruler(house_rulers, h_num)
        if not lord or lord not in planets_data:
            continue

        lord_h = planet_houses.get(lord, 0)
        if lord_h <= 0:
            continue

        lord_sign = get_sign_of_planet(chart, lord)
        is_exalted = (lord_sign == EXALTATION_SIGNS.get(lord))
        is_own = (lord_sign in OWN_SIGNS.get(lord, []))
        is_debilitated = (lord_sign == DEBILITATION_SIGNS.get(lord))
        is_in_kt = lord_h in KENDRA_TRIKONA_HOUSES
        houses_involved = [h_num] if h_num == lord_h else [h_num, lord_h]

        config = BHAVA_YOGA_CONFIGS.get(h_num, {})

        # 1. Evaluate Auspicious (Śubha) Bhava Yoga (Houses 1-7, 9-11)
        good_cfg = config.get("good")
        if good_cfg:
            if (is_exalted or is_own or is_in_kt) and not is_debilitated and lord_h not in DUSTHANA_HOUSES:
                base_score = 82.0
                if is_exalted or is_own:
                    base_score += 8.0

                breakers: List[YogaBreakerDetail] = []
                breakers.extend(audit_combustion([lord], chart))
                sb_breakers, sb_positives = audit_shadbala_muscle([lord], chart)
                breakers.extend(sb_breakers)

                # For H6 (Astra Yoga), require own/exalted dignity or Trikona co-rulership for PURE
                if h_num == 6 and not (is_exalted or is_own or lord in trikona_lords):
                    breakers.append(YogaBreakerDetail(
                        factor="6th House Martial Friction",
                        culprit_planet=lord,
                        description=f"6th Lord ({lord}) in Kendra/Trikona without own/exalted dignity brings competitive struggle alongside resilience.",
                        penalty=15.0
                    ))

                score, status = resolve_yoga_score_and_status(base_score, breakers)
                score = max(30.0, score)

                detected.append(YogaInstance(
                    id=good_cfg["id"],
                    name=good_cfg["name"],
                    category=YogaCategory.BHAVA,
                    status=status,
                    plausibility_score=round(score, 1),
                    participating_planets=[lord],
                    participating_houses=houses_involved,
                    scripture_ref=f"Phaladeepika 6.{43 + h_num}",
                    archetype=good_cfg["archetype"],
                    manifestation_effects=good_cfg["effects"],
                    positive_factors=[
                        f"Lord of H{h_num} ({lord}) occupies {'exalted' if is_exalted else 'own sign' if is_own else 'supportive Kendra/Trikoṇa'} House {lord_h} ({lord_sign})."
                    ] + sb_positives,
                    breakers=breakers
                ))

        # 2. Evaluate Converse 8th & 12th Kendra/Trikona Yogas: Asura (6.52) & Musala (6.56)
        # Affliction Status Invariant: Asura & Musala are Converse Bhava Yogas (STAINED or RESCUED, never PURE!)
        converse_kendra_cfg = config.get("converse_kendra")
        if converse_kendra_cfg and h_num in (8, 12):
            if (is_exalted or is_own or is_in_kt) and lord_h not in DUSTHANA_HOUSES:
                is_mitigated = (is_exalted or is_own or lord in trikona_lords)
                positive_factors: List[str] = []
                breakers = [
                    YogaBreakerDetail(
                        factor=f"Dusthana H{h_num} Leakage into Kendra/Trikoṇa",
                        culprit_planet=lord,
                        description=f"Lord of Dusthana H{h_num} ({lord}) occupies House {lord_h} ({lord_sign}), injecting H{h_num} volatility or expenditure into angular/trinal pillars.",
                        penalty=15.0 if is_mitigated else 25.0
                    )
                ]
                if is_mitigated:
                    status = YogaStatus.RESCUED
                    score = 72.0
                    positive_factors.append(
                        f"Mitigated (Bhaṅga): {lord} is fortified by {'exaltation' if is_exalted else 'own sign' if is_own else 'Trikoṇa co-rulership'}, channeling H{h_num} intensity constructively."
                    )
                else:
                    status = YogaStatus.STAINED
                    score = 65.0

                detected.append(YogaInstance(
                    id=converse_kendra_cfg["id"],
                    name=converse_kendra_cfg["name"],
                    category=YogaCategory.BHAVA,
                    status=status,
                    plausibility_score=round(score, 1),
                    participating_planets=[lord],
                    participating_houses=houses_involved,
                    scripture_ref=f"Phaladeepika 6.{44 + h_num}",
                    archetype=converse_kendra_cfg["archetype"],
                    manifestation_effects=converse_kendra_cfg["effects"],
                    positive_factors=positive_factors,
                    breakers=breakers
                ))

        # 3. Evaluate Converse (Aśubha) Dusthana Bhava Yogas (for houses 1-5, 7, 9-11)
        # Condition: Lord of house sits in a Dusthana (6, 8, 12)
        bad_cfg = config.get("bad")
        if bad_cfg and h_num not in (6, 8, 12):
            if lord_h in DUSTHANA_HOUSES:
                benefic_aspects = [
                    bp for bp in BENEFICS
                    if bp != lord and get_aspect_score(chart, bp, lord) >= ASPECT_PALPABLE_THRESHOLD
                ]
                is_nb_rescued = False
                if is_debilitated:
                    is_nb_rescued, _, _ = audit_neecha_bhanga(lord, chart)

                is_mitigated = bool(is_exalted or is_own or is_nb_rescued or benefic_aspects)
                positive_factors = []
                if is_exalted or is_own:
                    positive_factors.append(
                        f"Mitigated (Bhaṅga): {lord} occupies {'exalted' if is_exalted else 'own'} sign ({lord_sign}) in House {lord_h}."
                    )
                if is_nb_rescued:
                    positive_factors.append(
                        f"Mitigated (Nīcabhaṅga): {lord}'s debilitation in House {lord_h} is cancelled."
                    )
                if benefic_aspects:
                    positive_factors.append(
                        f"Mitigated (Śubha Dṛṣṭi): {lord} receives palpable benefic aspect from {', '.join(benefic_aspects)}."
                    )

                score = 55.0 if is_mitigated else 65.0
                status = YogaStatus.RESCUED if is_mitigated else YogaStatus.STAINED

                breakers = [
                    YogaBreakerDetail(
                        factor="Dusthana Placement",
                        culprit_planet=lord,
                        description=f"Lord of H{h_num} ({lord}) sits in Dusthana House {lord_h}, creating friction and delays.",
                        penalty=15.0 if is_mitigated else 25.0
                    )
                ]
                detected.append(YogaInstance(
                    id=bad_cfg["id"],
                    name=bad_cfg["name"],
                    category=YogaCategory.BHAVA,
                    status=status,
                    plausibility_score=round(score, 1),
                    participating_planets=[lord],
                    participating_houses=houses_involved,
                    scripture_ref=f"Phaladeepika 6.{57 + h_num}",
                    archetype=bad_cfg["archetype"],
                    manifestation_effects=bad_cfg["effects"],
                    positive_factors=positive_factors,
                    breakers=breakers
                ))

    return detected

"""
jyotish/yogas/breakers.py
The Yoga Breaker (Yoga Bhanga) and Cancellation (Neecha Bhanga) auditing engine.
Implements classical Parashari (BPHS Ch. 34) and Mantreswara (Phaladeepika Ch. 7) rules.
"""

from typing import List, Dict, Any, Tuple, Optional
from jyotish.yogas.models import YogaBreakerDetail, YogaStatus

ZODIAC_SIGNS = [
    "Aries", "Taurus", "Gemini", "Cancer", "Leo", "Virgo",
    "Libra", "Scorpio", "Sagittarius", "Capricorn", "Aquarius", "Pisces"
]

# Natural benefics and malefics
NATURAL_BENEFICS = {"Jupiter", "Venus", "Moon", "Mercury"}
NATURAL_MALEFICS = {"Saturn", "Mars", "Rahu", "Ketu", "Sun"}

# Exaltation & Debilitation Signs
EXALTATION_SIGNS = {
    "Sun": "Aries", "Moon": "Taurus", "Mars": "Capricorn",
    "Mercury": "Virgo", "Jupiter": "Cancer", "Venus": "Pisces", "Saturn": "Libra"
}
PLANET_EXALTED_IN_SIGN = {
    "Aries": "Sun", "Taurus": "Moon", "Cancer": "Jupiter",
    "Virgo": "Mercury", "Libra": "Saturn", "Capricorn": "Mars", "Pisces": "Venus"
}
DEBILITATION_SIGNS = {
    "Sun": "Libra", "Moon": "Scorpio", "Mars": "Cancer",
    "Mercury": "Pisces", "Jupiter": "Capricorn", "Venus": "Virgo", "Saturn": "Aries"
}
OWN_SIGNS = {
    "Sun": ["Leo"], "Moon": ["Cancer"], "Mars": ["Aries", "Scorpio"],
    "Mercury": ["Gemini", "Virgo"], "Jupiter": ["Sagittarius", "Pisces"],
    "Venus": ["Taurus", "Libra"], "Saturn": ["Capricorn", "Aquarius"]
}
SIGN_LORDS = {
    "Aries": "Mars", "Taurus": "Venus", "Gemini": "Mercury", "Cancer": "Moon",
    "Leo": "Sun", "Virgo": "Mercury", "Libra": "Venus", "Scorpio": "Mars",
    "Sagittarius": "Jupiter", "Capricorn": "Saturn", "Aquarius": "Saturn", "Pisces": "Jupiter"
}

# Sphuṭa Dṛṣṭi Aspect Thresholds (0 to 60 Virūpas)
# Per Phaladeepika and Vic DiCara (Lecture 7):
# >= 45 Virūpas (75% power): Palpable, fully verified classical connection
# 30.0 - 44.9 Virūpas (50% - 74% power): Faint / marginal aspect connection
ASPECT_PALPABLE_THRESHOLD = 45.0
ASPECT_MARGINAL_THRESHOLD = 30.0

def get_lagna_sign(chart: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    """Returns the Ascendant (Lagna) sign name, or empty string if missing."""
    if not isinstance(chart, dict):
        return ""
    d1 = (chart.get("vargas") or {}).get("D1") or {}
    sign = (d1.get("lagna") or {}).get("sign") if isinstance(d1, dict) else None
    if not sign:
        asc = chart.get("ascendant") or chart.get("lagna") or {}
        sign = asc.get("sign", "") if isinstance(asc, dict) else (asc if isinstance(asc, str) else "")
    return sign if isinstance(sign, str) else ""

def get_planets_data(chart: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Returns the D1 grahas dictionary."""
    if not isinstance(chart, dict):
        return {}
    vargas = chart.get("vargas") or {}
    d1 = vargas.get("D1") if isinstance(vargas, dict) else {}
    grahas = d1.get("grahas") if isinstance(d1, dict) else None
    if not grahas:
        grahas = chart.get("planets") or {}
    return grahas if isinstance(grahas, dict) else {}

def get_sign_of_planet(chart: Dict[str, Any], planet: str) -> str:
    """Returns the zodiac sign of a planet."""
    p_data = get_planets_data(chart).get(planet) or {}
    if isinstance(p_data, dict):
        return p_data.get("sign", "")
    return ""

def get_house_of_planet(chart: Dict[str, Any], planet: str) -> int:
    """Returns the Whole Sign House (1-12) of a planet relative to Lagna."""
    lagna = get_lagna_sign(chart)
    p_sign = get_sign_of_planet(chart, planet)
    if not lagna or not p_sign or lagna not in ZODIAC_SIGNS or p_sign not in ZODIAC_SIGNS:
        return 0
    l_idx = ZODIAC_SIGNS.index(lagna)
    p_idx = ZODIAC_SIGNS.index(p_sign)
    return (p_idx - l_idx) % 12 + 1

def _extract_virupas(val: Any) -> float:
    """Safely extracts and clamps aspect score to [0.0, 60.0] Virupas."""
    if val is None:
        return 0.0
    if isinstance(val, dict):
        for key in ("raw", "net", "plus", "virupas", "score"):
            if val.get(key) is not None:
                try:
                    return max(0.0, min(60.0, float(val[key])))
                except (ValueError, TypeError):
                    return 0.0
        return 0.0
    try:
        return max(0.0, min(60.0, float(val)))
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return 0.0

def get_aspect_score(chart: Dict[str, Any], giver: str, receiver: str) -> float:
    """Returns Graha Drishti aspect value (0-60 Virupas) from giver to receiver."""
    if not isinstance(chart, dict) or not giver or not receiver or giver == receiver:
        return 0.0

    adv = chart.get("advanced_aspects") or {}
    aspects = adv.get("planets") or {}

    # 1. Direct planet receiver check
    recv_dict = aspects.get(receiver)
    if isinstance(recv_dict, dict) and giver in recv_dict:
        return _extract_virupas(recv_dict[giver])

    # 2. Lagna / Ascendant receiver check across cusps and lagna dictionaries
    if receiver in ("Lagna", "Ascendant", "1"):
        for l_key in ("Lagna", "Ascendant"):
            l_dict = aspects.get(l_key)
            if isinstance(l_dict, dict) and giver in l_dict:
                return _extract_virupas(l_dict[giver])

        lagna_adv = adv.get("lagna")
        if isinstance(lagna_adv, dict) and giver in lagna_adv:
            return _extract_virupas(lagna_adv[giver])

        for cusp_group in ("cusps", "equal_cusps"):
            cusps = adv.get(cusp_group) or {}
            if isinstance(cusps, dict):
                c1 = cusps.get(1) or cusps.get("1") or {}
                if isinstance(c1, dict) and giver in c1:
                    return _extract_virupas(c1[giver])

    return 0.0

def get_house_rulers(chart: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[int, List[str]]:
    """Maps house number (1-12) to ruling planet(s) based on Lagna sign."""
    lagna_sign = get_lagna_sign(chart)
    if lagna_sign not in ZODIAC_SIGNS:
        return {h: [] for h in range(1, 13)}
    
    start_idx = ZODIAC_SIGNS.index(lagna_sign)
    house_rulers = {}
    for h in range(1, 13):
        sign = ZODIAC_SIGNS[(start_idx + h - 1) % 12]
        lord = SIGN_LORDS.get(sign)
        house_rulers[h] = [lord] if lord else []
    return house_rulers

def _first_ruler(house_rulers: Dict[int, List[str]], house: int) -> Optional[str]:
    rulers = house_rulers.get(house) or []
    return rulers[0] if rulers else None


def audit_trishadaya_interference(
    yoga_planets: List[str],
    chart: Dict[str, Any],
    is_supreme_pairing: bool = False,
    exclude_houses: Tuple[int, ...] = ()
) -> List[YogaBreakerDetail]:
    """
    Audits intrusion of the Trishadaya lords (3rd, 6th, and 11th).
    - Deduplicates dual-Trishadaya rulers (e.g., Mars in Gemini, Venus in Sagittarius,
      Jupiter in Libra) and selects the strongest interference across yoga_planets.
    - Use `exclude_houses=(11,)` for Dhana/wealth yogas where the 11th lord is a co-producer.
    """
    breakers = []
    house_rulers = get_house_rulers(chart)
    lagna_lord = _first_ruler(house_rulers, 1)

    lord_11 = _first_ruler(house_rulers, 11)
    lord_6 = _first_ruler(house_rulers, 6)
    lord_3 = _first_ruler(house_rulers, 3)

    saboteurs = [
        (lord_11, 11, 40.0, "11th Lord Intrusion (Primary Saboteur)",
         "Rules Labha (desires/gains). Corrupts higher purpose (Dharma) into vanity, applause-seeking, and social distraction."),
        (lord_6, 6, 25.0, "6th Lord Intrusion",
         "Rules Shatru/Roga. Injects litigation, chronic workplace friction, debts, and competitive animosity into the combination."),
        (lord_3, 3, 15.0, "3rd Lord Intrusion",
         "Rules Sahaja/Bhratri. Dissipates focus into petty hobbies, sibling drama, and restless, unfocused effort.")
    ]

    seen_saboteurs = set()

    for saboteur, house_num, base_penalty, title, desc in saboteurs:
        if house_num in exclude_houses:
            continue
        if not saboteur or saboteur in yoga_planets or saboteur in seen_saboteurs:
            continue
        # Lagnesha exemption: Ascendant lord never destroys the native's yoga
        if saboteur == lagna_lord:
            continue

        penalty = base_penalty
        if is_supreme_pairing and house_num == 3:
            penalty = 7.5

        saboteur_house = get_house_of_planet(chart, saboteur)
        best_breaker: Optional[YogaBreakerDetail] = None

        for yp in yoga_planets:
            yp_house = get_house_of_planet(chart, yp)
            candidate: Optional[YogaBreakerDetail] = None

            # 1. Conjunction in same house (strongest interference)
            if saboteur_house == yp_house and yp_house > 0:
                candidate = YogaBreakerDetail(
                    factor=title,
                    culprit_planet=saboteur,
                    description=f"{saboteur} (rules H{house_num}) conjoins {yp} in House {yp_house}. {desc}",
                    penalty=penalty
                )
            else:
                # 2. Graha Drishti aspect (Palpable >= 45 Virupas, Marginal >= 30 Virupas)
                aspect_score = min(60.0, max(0.0, get_aspect_score(chart, saboteur, yp)))
                if aspect_score >= ASPECT_PALPABLE_THRESHOLD:
                    candidate = YogaBreakerDetail(
                        factor=f"{title} (Palpable Aspect)",
                        culprit_planet=saboteur,
                        description=f"{saboteur} (rules H{house_num}) casts strong {aspect_score:.0f}-Virupa aspect on {yp}. {desc}",
                        penalty=penalty * (aspect_score / 60.0)
                    )
                elif aspect_score >= ASPECT_MARGINAL_THRESHOLD:
                    candidate = YogaBreakerDetail(
                        factor=f"{title} (Marginal Aspect)",
                        culprit_planet=saboteur,
                        description=f"{saboteur} (rules H{house_num}) casts marginal {aspect_score:.0f}-Virupa aspect on {yp}. {desc}",
                        penalty=(penalty * 0.5) * (aspect_score / 60.0)
                    )

            if candidate and (best_breaker is None or candidate.penalty > best_breaker.penalty):
                best_breaker = candidate

        if best_breaker is not None:
            breakers.append(best_breaker)
            seen_saboteurs.add(saboteur)

    return breakers

def audit_combustion(
    yoga_planets: List[str],
    chart: Dict[str, Any],
    deep_only: bool = False
) -> List[YogaBreakerDetail]:
    """
    Audits whether participating planets are combust (< 8° from Sun).
    Deep combustion (< 3°) burns independent manifestation capacity.
    Set `deep_only=True` for Solar combinations (e.g., Budhaditya) where moderate
    proximity (3°–8°) is astronomically expected.
    """
    breakers = []
    comb_status = (
        chart.get("combustion_status")
        if isinstance(chart, dict)
        else getattr(getattr(chart, "baseline", None), "combustion_status", {})
    ) or {}
    planets_data = get_planets_data(chart)

    for p in yoga_planets:
        if p in ("Sun", "Rahu", "Ketu", "Lagna", "Ascendant"):
            continue
        p_entry = planets_data.get(p) or {}
        if not isinstance(p_entry, dict):
            continue

        raw_diff = None
        if isinstance(comb_status, dict) and isinstance(comb_status.get(p), dict):
            raw_diff = comb_status[p].get("sun_separation")
        if raw_diff is None and p_entry.get("sun_distance") is not None:
            raw_diff = p_entry.get("sun_distance")

        if raw_diff is None:
            sun_entry = planets_data.get("Sun") or {}
            sun_lon = sun_entry.get("longitude") if isinstance(sun_entry, dict) else None
            p_lon = p_entry.get("longitude")
            if sun_lon is None or p_lon is None:
                continue
            raw_diff = p_lon - sun_lon

        try:
            diff = abs(float(raw_diff)) % 360.0
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            continue
        if diff > 180.0:
            diff = 360.0 - diff

        if diff < 3.0:
            breakers.append(YogaBreakerDetail(
                factor="Deep Planetary Combustion (Astangata)",
                culprit_planet="Sun",
                description=f"{p} is deeply combust within {diff:.1f}° of the Sun. Its independent rays and tangible manifestation power are burned.",
                penalty=40.0
            ))
        elif diff < 8.0 and not deep_only:
            breakers.append(YogaBreakerDetail(
                factor="Moderate Planetary Combustion (Astangata)",
                culprit_planet="Sun",
                description=f"{p} is combust within {diff:.1f}° of the Sun. May produce internal egoic conflict or diminished visibility.",
                penalty=20.0
            ))

    return breakers

def audit_neecha_bhanga_condition_3(
    debilitated_planet: str,
    debilitation_sign: str,
    chart: Dict[str, Any]
) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
    """
    Evaluates Condition 3 (Phaladipika 7.26) checking both:
    - 3A: Lord of the planet's exaltation sign (tad-uccāṃśa-nātha), excluding self-lordship
    - 3B: Planet that exalts in the debilitation sign, excluding the dispositor already counted in Cond 2
    """
    moon_house = get_house_of_planet(chart, "Moon")
    dispositor = SIGN_LORDS.get(debilitation_sign)
    matched_descriptions = []

    # Condition 3A: Lord of the planet's exaltation sign (tad-uccāṃśa-nātha)
    exalt_sign = EXALTATION_SIGNS.get(debilitated_planet)
    exalt_lord = SIGN_LORDS.get(exalt_sign)
    if exalt_lord:
        lord_house = get_house_of_planet(chart, exalt_lord)
        lord_from_moon = ((lord_house - moon_house) % 12 + 1) if (exalt_lord != "Moon" and lord_house > 0 and moon_house > 0) else 0
        if lord_house in (1, 4, 7, 10):
            matched_descriptions.append(
                f"Lord of exaltation sign ({exalt_lord}) is in Kendra H{lord_house} from Lagna."
            )
        elif lord_from_moon in (1, 4, 7, 10):
            matched_descriptions.append(
                f"Lord of exaltation sign ({exalt_lord}) is in Kendra H{lord_from_moon} from Chandra."
            )

    # Condition 3B: Planet that exalts in this sign (if distinct from dispositor)
    exalted_guest = PLANET_EXALTED_IN_SIGN.get(debilitation_sign)
    if exalted_guest and exalted_guest != debilitated_planet and exalted_guest != dispositor:
        guest_house = get_house_of_planet(chart, exalted_guest)
        guest_from_moon = ((guest_house - moon_house) % 12 + 1) if (exalted_guest != "Moon" and guest_house > 0 and moon_house > 0) else 0
        if guest_house in (1, 4, 7, 10):
            matched_descriptions.append(
                f"Planet exalted in this sign ({exalted_guest}) is in Kendra H{guest_house} from Lagna."
            )
        elif guest_from_moon in (1, 4, 7, 10):
            matched_descriptions.append(
                f"Planet exalted in this sign ({exalted_guest}) is in Kendra H{guest_from_moon} from Chandra."
            )

    if matched_descriptions:
        return True, " ".join(matched_descriptions)
    return False, ""

def audit_neecha_bhanga(planet: str, chart: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[bool, List[str], float]:
    """
    Audits the 6 Classical Cancellation of Debility (Neecha Bhanga) rules
    according to Phaladeepika 7.24-30 and VA Vol 1 Ch. 16.
    Returns: (is_rescued, reasons, redemption_bonus_percentage)
    """
    planets_data = get_planets_data(chart)
    p_info = planets_data.get(planet) or {}
    p_sign = p_info.get("sign", "") if isinstance(p_info, dict) else ""

    if not p_sign or p_sign != DEBILITATION_SIGNS.get(planet):
        return False, [], 0.0

    reasons = []
    bonus = 0.0

    debilitation_sign = p_sign
    dispositor = SIGN_LORDS.get(debilitation_sign)
    moon_house = get_house_of_planet(chart, "Moon")
    p_house = get_house_of_planet(chart, planet)

    # Condition 1: Exalted Co-occupant in same sign
    for other_p, data in planets_data.items():
        if not isinstance(data, dict):
            continue
        if other_p != planet and data.get("sign") == debilitation_sign:
            if data.get("sign") == EXALTATION_SIGNS.get(other_p):
                reasons.append(f"Exalted co-occupant: {other_p} is exalted in {debilitation_sign} sharing the sign with fallen {planet}.")
                bonus += 35.0
                break

    # Condition 2: Dispositor of debilitation sign in Kendra from Lagna (1,4,7,10) or Moon
    if dispositor:
        disp_house = get_house_of_planet(chart, dispositor)
        disp_from_moon = ((disp_house - moon_house) % 12 + 1) if (dispositor != "Moon" and disp_house > 0 and moon_house > 0) else 0
        if disp_house in (1, 4, 7, 10):
            reasons.append(f"Dispositor in Kendra from Lagna: {dispositor} (lord of {debilitation_sign}) occupies Kendra H{disp_house}.")
            bonus += 30.0
        elif disp_from_moon in (1, 4, 7, 10):
            reasons.append(f"Dispositor in Kendra from Moon: {dispositor} is in Kendra H{disp_from_moon} from Chandra.")
            bonus += 30.0

    # Condition 3: Planet that exalts in this sign or lord of exaltation sign is in Kendra from Lagna or Moon
    c3_met, c3_desc = audit_neecha_bhanga_condition_3(planet, debilitation_sign, chart)
    if c3_met:
        reasons.append(f"Condition 3 (Exaltation Alignment): {c3_desc}")
        bonus += 30.0

    # Condition 4: Dispositor of debilitation sign is itself exalted
    if dispositor:
        disp_sign = get_sign_of_planet(chart, dispositor)
        if disp_sign == EXALTATION_SIGNS.get(dispositor):
            reasons.append(f"Exalted dispositor: {dispositor} (ruler of {debilitation_sign}) is exalted in {disp_sign}.")
            bonus += 30.0

    # Condition 5: Dispositor aspects the fallen planet
    if dispositor:
        aspect_score = get_aspect_score(chart, dispositor, planet)
        if aspect_score >= ASPECT_PALPABLE_THRESHOLD:
            reasons.append(f"Dispositor aspect: {dispositor} directly aspects fallen {planet} with palpable {aspect_score:.0f} Virupas (>= 45).")
            bonus += 30.0
        elif aspect_score >= ASPECT_MARGINAL_THRESHOLD:
            reasons.append(f"Dispositor aspect (marginal): {dispositor} aspects fallen {planet} with faint {aspect_score:.0f} Virupas.")
            bonus += 15.0

    # Condition 6: Fallen planet itself is in Kendra from Lagna or Moon
    exalt_sign = EXALTATION_SIGNS.get(planet)
    exalt_lord = SIGN_LORDS.get(exalt_sign)
    if not (c3_met and exalt_lord == planet):
        p_from_moon = ((p_house - moon_house) % 12 + 1) if (planet != "Moon" and p_house > 0 and moon_house > 0) else 0
        disp_house = get_house_of_planet(chart, dispositor) if dispositor else 0
        exalt_lord_house = get_house_of_planet(chart, exalt_lord) if exalt_lord else 0
        conjoined_lord = (
            (dispositor and dispositor != planet and p_house > 0 and p_house == disp_house) or
            (exalt_lord and exalt_lord != planet and p_house > 0 and p_house == exalt_lord_house)
        )
        if p_house in (1, 4, 7, 10):
            pts = 30.0 if (conjoined_lord and bonus < 30.0) else 20.0
            reasons.append(f"Angular fallen planet: {planet} is in Kendra H{p_house} from Lagna, forcing public engagement.")
            bonus += pts
        elif p_from_moon in (1, 4, 7, 10):
            pts = 30.0 if (conjoined_lord and bonus < 30.0) else 15.0
            reasons.append(f"Angular fallen planet from Moon: {planet} is in Kendra H{p_from_moon} from Chandra.")
            bonus += pts

    is_rescued = len(reasons) >= 1 and bonus >= 30.0
    if is_rescued:
        if p_house in (1, 4, 7, 10, 5, 9):
            reasons.append(f"Rāja Yoga Upgrade: {planet} occupies auspicious House {p_house} (Kendra/Koṇa).")
        elif p_house in (6, 8, 12):
            reasons.append(f"Simple Cancellation: {planet} is trapped in Duṣṭhāna House {p_house}; manifests as overcoming deficit rather than worldly command.")
    return is_rescued, reasons, min(bonus, 100.0)

def audit_dispositor_vitality(yoga_planets: List[str], chart: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[YogaBreakerDetail]:
    """
    Audits the condition of the dispositor (host) for the yoga-forming planets.
    Deduplicates shared dispositors so conjoined yoga planets are not penalized twice.
    """
    breakers = []
    seen_dispositors = set()

    for p in yoga_planets:
        sign = get_sign_of_planet(chart, p)
        dispositor = SIGN_LORDS.get(sign)
        if not dispositor or dispositor == p or dispositor in seen_dispositors:
            continue
        seen_dispositors.add(dispositor)

        disp_sign = get_sign_of_planet(chart, dispositor)
        disp_house = get_house_of_planet(chart, dispositor)

        # Dispositor debilitated without Neecha Bhanga
        if disp_sign == DEBILITATION_SIGNS.get(dispositor):
            is_rescued, _, _ = audit_neecha_bhanga(dispositor, chart)
            if not is_rescued:
                breakers.append(YogaBreakerDetail(
                    factor="Debilitated Host Dispositor",
                    culprit_planet=dispositor,
                    description=f"{p}'s dispositor {dispositor} is in fall in {disp_sign} without cancellation. The guest lacks institutional backing.",
                    penalty=25.0
                ))

        # Dispositor trapped in Dusthana (6, 8, 12)
        if disp_house in (6, 8, 12):
            breakers.append(YogaBreakerDetail(
                factor="Host Dispositor Trapped in Dusthana",
                culprit_planet=dispositor,
                description=f"{p}'s dispositor {dispositor} resides in difficult House {disp_house}. Energy leaks into friction or isolation.",
                penalty=15.0
            ))

    return breakers

# Categories that inherently operate in or require Dusthana houses (6, 8, 12)
DUSTHANA_EXEMPT_CATEGORIES = {
    "Viparita Raja (Reversal)",
    "VIPARITA",
    "Daridrya (Poverty)",
    "DARIDRYA",
    "Chāṇḍāla & Doṣa (Afflictions)",
    "CHANDAL",
}

def audit_dusthana_placement(
    yoga_houses: List[int],
    category_name: str
) -> Optional[YogaBreakerDetail]:
    """
    Auspicious worldly yogas (Raja, Dhana, Mahapurusha) placed in 6, 8, 12
    dissipate public authority into internal struggle, health issues, or seclusion.
    Automatically exempts Viparita, Daridrya, and Chandal categories.
    """
    cat_str = category_name.value if hasattr(category_name, "value") else str(category_name)
    if (
        str(category_name) in DUSTHANA_EXEMPT_CATEGORIES
        or cat_str in DUSTHANA_EXEMPT_CATEGORIES
        or getattr(category_name, "name", "") in DUSTHANA_EXEMPT_CATEGORIES
    ):
        return None
    dusthana_count = sum(1 for h in yoga_houses if h in (6, 8, 12))
    if dusthana_count > 0:
        return YogaBreakerDetail(
            factor="Dusthana Trapping (Houses 6, 8, 12)",
            culprit_planet="House Placement",
            description=f"Yoga manifests in Dusthana houses ({[h for h in yoga_houses if h in (6, 8, 12)]}). Results manifest privately, spiritually, or after severe crisis rather than public eminence.",
            penalty=25.0 * dusthana_count
        )
    return None

def audit_shadbala_muscle(yoga_planets: List[str], chart: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[List[YogaBreakerDetail], List[str]]:
    """
    Audits whether planets possess the raw horsepower (Shadbala) to deliver results.
    Standard: >= 1.0 Rupa (>= 100% requirement).
    """
    breakers = []
    positive_notes = []
    shadbala = (chart.get("shadbala") if isinstance(chart, dict) else None) or {}
    if not isinstance(shadbala, dict):
        return breakers, positive_notes

    for p in yoga_planets:
        p_bala = shadbala.get(p) or {}
        if not isinstance(p_bala, dict):
            continue
        try:
            rupas = float(p_bala.get("Rupas") if p_bala.get("Rupas") is not None else 1.0)
            ratio = float(p_bala.get("Ratio_to_Req") if p_bala.get("Ratio_to_Req") is not None else 1.0)
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            continue

        if ratio < 0.85:
            breakers.append(YogaBreakerDetail(
                factor="Low Kinetic Horsepower (Shadbala)",
                culprit_planet=p,
                description=f"{p} has only {rupas:.2f} Rupas ({ratio*100:.0f}% of required strength). Lacks kinetic muscle to deliver tangible promises.",
                penalty=15.0
            ))
        elif ratio >= 1.25:
            positive_notes.append(f"{p} is muscular in Shadbala ({rupas:.2f} Rupas / {ratio*100:.0f}% of requirement).")

    return breakers, positive_notes


def resolve_yoga_score_and_status(
    pre_breaker_score: float,
    breakers: List[YogaBreakerDetail],
    score_cap: float = 100.0
) -> Tuple[float, YogaStatus]:
    """
    Standardized score and status resolver for auspicious yogas:
    1. Caps pre-breaker positive confluence at `score_cap` (default 100.0).
    2. Deducts all breaker penalties and clamps net score to [5.0, 100.0].
    3. Blocks `YogaStatus.PURE` if any major breaker (penalty >= 20.0) is present.
    """
    net_score = min(score_cap, float(pre_breaker_score))
    for b in (breakers or []):
        if b is not None:
            net_score -= float(b.penalty)
    net_score = round(max(5.0, min(score_cap, net_score)), 1)

    has_major_breaker = any(b.penalty >= 20.0 for b in (breakers or []) if b is not None)
    if net_score >= 75.0 and not has_major_breaker:
        status = YogaStatus.PURE
    elif net_score >= 50.0:
        status = YogaStatus.STAINED
    elif net_score >= 30.0:
        status = YogaStatus.RESCUED
    else:
        status = YogaStatus.BROKEN

    return net_score, status

"""
jyotish/yogas/chandal_yogas.py
Classical Chandal and Nodal Affliction / Catalyst Yogas.
Scripture: BPHS Ch. 45 (Avasthas & Graha Yoga), Phaladeepika Ch. 6, Saravali Ch. 31,
and Vic DiCara's Rahu-Ketu Masterclasses.

Evaluates:
1. Guru-Chāṇḍāla Yoga (Jupiter + Rahu): Ideological ambition, unorthodox doctrines, taboo zeal.
2. Guru-Ketu Jñāna Yoga (Jupiter + Ketu): Ascetic contemplation, spiritual discernment, moksha focus.
3. Śrāpita Yoga (Saturn + Rahu): Heavy karmic debt, systemic friction, perseverance crucible.
4. Aṅgāraka Yoga (Mars + Rahu): Volcanic drive, tactical courage, explosive friction.

Architecture Invariant (YOGA_ARCHITECTURE.md Row 7 & Section 5 Rule 3):
- Same-sign longitudinal separation is strictly `abs(deg1 - deg2)` in `[0.0, 30.0)` — NEVER `abs(30.0 - diff)`.
- Affliction combinations in Category `CHANDAL` must use `YogaStatus.STAINED` when active/unmitigated
  and `YogaStatus.RESCUED` when mitigated by exaltation, own sign, Nīcabhaṅga, palpable benefic aspect
  (>= 45.0 Virūpas), or Ketu spiritualization — NEVER `YogaStatus.PURE`!
- NEVER call `audit_dusthana_placement` on `CHANDAL` yogas.
"""

from typing import List, Dict, Any
from jyotish.yogas.models import (
    YogaInstance, YogaCategory, YogaStatus, YogaBreakerDetail
)
from jyotish.yogas.breakers import (
    get_house_of_planet, get_sign_of_planet, get_planets_data, get_aspect_score,
    audit_neecha_bhanga,
    EXALTATION_SIGNS, OWN_SIGNS, DEBILITATION_SIGNS,
    ASPECT_PALPABLE_THRESHOLD, ASPECT_MARGINAL_THRESHOLD
)


def _safe_deg_in_sign(planets_data: Dict[str, Any], planet: str) -> float:
    """Safely extracts degree within sign [0.0, 30.0) without failing on None."""
    p_info = planets_data.get(planet)
    if not isinstance(p_info, dict):
        return 0.0
    deg = p_info.get("degree_0_to_30")
    if deg is not None:
        try:
            return float(deg) % 30.0
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            pass
    lon = p_info.get("longitude")
    if lon is not None:
        try:
            return float(lon) % 30.0
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            pass
    return 0.0


def detect_chandal_yogas(chart: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[YogaInstance]:
    """
    Scans the chart for classical nodal combinations with Jupiter, Saturn, and Mars.
    Distinguishes sharply between Rahu's outward worldly amplification (Chāṇḍāla)
    and Ketu's inward spiritualizing detachment (Jñāna).
    """
    detected: List[YogaInstance] = []
    planets_data = get_planets_data(chart)

    if not planets_data:
        return detected

    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. GURU-CHĀṆḌĀLA YOGA (Jupiter + Rahu Conjunction)
    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    if "Jupiter" in planets_data and "Rahu" in planets_data:
        j_sign = get_sign_of_planet(chart, "Jupiter")
        r_sign = get_sign_of_planet(chart, "Rahu")

        if j_sign and j_sign == r_sign:
            j_house = get_house_of_planet(chart, "Jupiter")
            j_deg = _safe_deg_in_sign(planets_data, "Jupiter")
            r_deg = _safe_deg_in_sign(planets_data, "Rahu")
            # Within the same sign [0°, 30°), separation is strictly abs(j_deg - r_deg)
            diff = abs(j_deg - r_deg)

            breakers: List[YogaBreakerDetail] = []
            positive_factors: List[str] = []

            if diff <= (10.0 / 3.0):  # Exact Navamsha orb (<= 3°20')
                orb_desc = f"Exact / Intimate Orb ({diff:.2f}°): Direct psychological eclipse between wisdom and ambition."
                plausibility = 92.0
            elif diff <= 10.0:
                orb_desc = f"Moderate Orb ({diff:.2f}°): Strong philosophical amplification and unorthodox doctrine."
                plausibility = 82.0
            else:
                orb_desc = f"Wide Sign Conjunction ({diff:.2f}°): Mild unorthodox coloring across common sign field."
                plausibility = 68.0

            is_mitigated = False

            # Dignity check (Exaltation / Own Sign / Debilitation with Neecha Bhanga)
            if j_sign == EXALTATION_SIGNS.get("Jupiter"):
                is_mitigated = True
                positive_factors.append("Jupiter is Exalted in Cancer (Uccha): Converts taboo zeal into revolutionary, compassionate reform.")
            elif j_sign in OWN_SIGNS.get("Jupiter", []):
                is_mitigated = True
                positive_factors.append(f"Jupiter in Domicile ({j_sign}): Immense administrative and institutional organizing power.")
            elif j_sign == DEBILITATION_SIGNS.get("Jupiter"):
                is_nb, nb_reasons, _ = audit_neecha_bhanga("Jupiter", chart)
                if is_nb:
                    is_mitigated = True
                    positive_factors.extend(nb_reasons)
                else:
                    breakers.append(YogaBreakerDetail(
                        factor="Debilitated Dispositor (Nīca Guru)",
                        culprit_planet="Jupiter",
                        description="Jupiter is debilitated in Capricorn without cancellation; worldly opportunism overrides moral restraint.",
                        penalty=25.0
                    ))

            # Benefic aspects (Venus, Moon, Mercury) using Sphuṭa Dṛṣṭi thresholds (45.0 / 30.0 Virūpas)
            for ben in ("Venus", "Moon", "Mercury"):
                asp = get_aspect_score(chart, ben, "Jupiter")
                if asp >= ASPECT_PALPABLE_THRESHOLD:
                    is_mitigated = True
                    positive_factors.append(f"Palpable aspect from natural benefic {ben} ({asp:.1f} Virūpas) softens dogmatic intensity.")
                    plausibility -= 8.0
                elif asp >= ASPECT_MARGINAL_THRESHOLD:
                    positive_factors.append(f"Marginal aspect from natural benefic {ben} ({asp:.1f} Virūpas) tempers ideological extremes.")
                    plausibility -= 4.0

            # Cruel co-conjunctions (Saturn or Mars in same sign -> Vikala / Besieged)
            has_cruel_conj = False
            for mal in ("Saturn", "Mars"):
                if get_sign_of_planet(chart, mal) == j_sign:
                    has_cruel_conj = True
                    breakers.append(YogaBreakerDetail(
                        factor=f"{mal} Co-Conjunction (Vikala Avasthā)",
                        culprit_planet=mal,
                        description=f"{mal} conjoins Jupiter and Rahu in {j_sign}, severely besieging Jupiter's ethical clarity into combative or rigid dogma.",
                        penalty=20.0
                    ))

            # Affliction Status Invariant: RESCUED when mitigated without cruel siege, else STAINED — never PURE
            status = YogaStatus.RESCUED if (is_mitigated and not has_cruel_conj) else YogaStatus.STAINED

            detected.append(YogaInstance(
                id="guru_chandal_yoga",
                name="Guru-Chāṇḍāla Yoga",
                category=YogaCategory.CHANDAL,
                status=status,
                plausibility_score=round(max(20.0, min(100.0, plausibility)), 1),
                participating_planets=["Jupiter", "Rahu"],
                participating_houses=[j_house] if j_house > 0 else [],
                scripture_ref="BPHS Ch. 45, Phaladeepika 6.34, Saravali Ch. 31",
                archetype="The Taboo Zealot & Reformer: Rahu eclipses Jupiter's traditional wisdom, amplifying beliefs into unconventional, dogmatic, or revolutionary crusades.",
                manifestation_effects=[
                    "Constructive: Shatters outmoded dogmas, pioneers non-traditional philosophies, and mobilizes large unconventional networks.",
                    "Strained: Vulnerability to fanaticism, ideological extremism, or using moral rhetoric to justify ruthless worldly ends.",
                    orb_desc
                ],
                positive_factors=positive_factors,
                breakers=breakers
            ))

    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. GURU-KETU JÑĀNA YOGA (Jupiter + Ketu Conjunction)
    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    if "Jupiter" in planets_data and "Ketu" in planets_data:
        j_sign = get_sign_of_planet(chart, "Jupiter")
        k_sign = get_sign_of_planet(chart, "Ketu")

        if j_sign and j_sign == k_sign:
            j_house = get_house_of_planet(chart, "Jupiter")
            j_deg = _safe_deg_in_sign(planets_data, "Jupiter")
            k_deg = _safe_deg_in_sign(planets_data, "Ketu")
            diff = abs(j_deg - k_deg)

            pos_gk = [f"Ketu spiritualizes Jupiter in {j_sign}; promotes introspection and liberation (Mokṣa) over worldly ambition."]
            breakers_gk: List[YogaBreakerDetail] = []
            score_gk = 85.0

            if j_sign == DEBILITATION_SIGNS.get("Jupiter"):
                is_nb, nb_reasons, _ = audit_neecha_bhanga("Jupiter", chart)
                if is_nb:
                    pos_gk.extend(nb_reasons)
                else:
                    breakers_gk.append(YogaBreakerDetail(
                        factor="Unredeemed Debilitation of Jupiter",
                        culprit_planet="Jupiter",
                        description="Jupiter is debilitated in Capricorn without cancellation, dampening philosophical clarity.",
                        penalty=20.0
                    ))
                    score_gk -= 20.0

            # Affliction category invariant: RESCUED (spiritualized nodal inversion) when unafflicted, STAINED when debilitated
            status_gk = YogaStatus.STAINED if breakers_gk else YogaStatus.RESCUED

            detected.append(YogaInstance(
                id="guru_ketu_jnana_yoga",
                name="Guru-Ketu Jñāna Yoga",
                category=YogaCategory.CHANDAL,
                status=status_gk,
                plausibility_score=round(max(20.0, min(100.0, score_gk)), 1),
                participating_planets=["Jupiter", "Ketu"],
                participating_houses=[j_house] if j_house > 0 else [],
                scripture_ref="Phaladeepika 6.35, Vic DiCara Lesson 5 (Rahu-Ketu Masterclass)",
                archetype="The Ascetic Seeker: Ketu internalizes Jupiter's philosophical nature, fostering profound spiritual discernment, detachment from superficial dogmas, and direct mystical perception.",
                manifestation_effects=[
                    "Deep spiritual skepticism toward ritualistic religion; pursuit of direct esoteric truth and liberation (Moksha).",
                    "Exceptional capacity for research, contemplation, and philosophical detachment.",
                    f"Conjoined within {diff:.2f}° in House {j_house} ({j_sign}); favors inner wisdom over outward ambition."
                ],
                positive_factors=pos_gk,
                breakers=breakers_gk
            ))

    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. ŚRĀPITA YOGA (Saturn + Rahu Conjunction)
    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    if "Saturn" in planets_data and "Rahu" in planets_data:
        s_sign = get_sign_of_planet(chart, "Saturn")
        r_sign = get_sign_of_planet(chart, "Rahu")

        if s_sign and s_sign == r_sign:
            s_house = get_house_of_planet(chart, "Saturn")
            s_deg = _safe_deg_in_sign(planets_data, "Saturn")
            r_deg = _safe_deg_in_sign(planets_data, "Rahu")
            diff = abs(s_deg - r_deg)

            breakers = []
            positive_factors = []
            is_mitigated = False
            plausibility = 88.0 if diff <= (10.0 / 3.0) else (82.0 if diff <= 10.0 else 72.0)

            if s_sign == EXALTATION_SIGNS.get("Saturn"):
                is_mitigated = True
                positive_factors.append("Saturn is Exalted in Libra: Forges karmic friction into extraordinary, unbreakable endurance.")
            elif s_sign in OWN_SIGNS.get("Saturn", []):
                is_mitigated = True
                positive_factors.append(f"Saturn in Domicile ({s_sign}): Sturdy structural grounding resists chaotic disruption.")
            elif s_sign == DEBILITATION_SIGNS.get("Saturn"):
                is_nb, nb_reasons, _ = audit_neecha_bhanga("Saturn", chart)
                if is_nb:
                    is_mitigated = True
                    positive_factors.extend(nb_reasons)
                else:
                    breakers.append(YogaBreakerDetail(
                        factor="Debilitated Saturn (Nīca Śani)",
                        culprit_planet="Saturn",
                        description="Saturn is debilitated in Aries without cancellation, intensifying systemic frustration and delays.",
                        penalty=25.0
                    ))

            asp_jup = get_aspect_score(chart, "Jupiter", "Saturn")
            if asp_jup >= ASPECT_PALPABLE_THRESHOLD:
                is_mitigated = True
                positive_factors.append(f"Palpable Jupiter aspect ({asp_jup:.1f} Virūpas) provides moral guidance and mitigates karmic strain.")
                plausibility -= 8.0
            elif asp_jup >= ASPECT_MARGINAL_THRESHOLD:
                positive_factors.append(f"Marginal Jupiter aspect ({asp_jup:.1f} Virūpas) offers philosophical perspective.")
                plausibility -= 4.0

            status = YogaStatus.RESCUED if (is_mitigated and not breakers) else YogaStatus.STAINED

            detected.append(YogaInstance(
                id="shrapit_yoga",
                name="Śrāpita Yoga",
                category=YogaCategory.CHANDAL,
                status=status,
                plausibility_score=round(max(20.0, min(100.0, plausibility)), 1),
                participating_planets=["Saturn", "Rahu"],
                participating_houses=[s_house] if s_house > 0 else [],
                scripture_ref="BPHS Ch. 45, Nadi Shastras",
                archetype="The Karmic Crucible: Rahu amplifies Saturnian burdens, delays, and structural pressure, requiring persistent patience to forge mastery.",
                manifestation_effects=[
                    "Heavy karmic duties and trials in early life; learning through hard labor, resilience, and slow realization.",
                    "Constructive: Bestows monumental patience, realism, and mastery over difficult material structures over time.",
                    f"Conjoined within {diff:.2f}° in House {s_house} ({s_sign})."
                ],
                positive_factors=positive_factors,
                breakers=breakers
            ))

    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. AṄGĀRAKA YOGA (Mars + Rahu Conjunction)
    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    if "Mars" in planets_data and "Rahu" in planets_data:
        m_sign = get_sign_of_planet(chart, "Mars")
        r_sign = get_sign_of_planet(chart, "Rahu")

        if m_sign and m_sign == r_sign:
            m_house = get_house_of_planet(chart, "Mars")
            m_deg = _safe_deg_in_sign(planets_data, "Mars")
            r_deg = _safe_deg_in_sign(planets_data, "Rahu")
            diff = abs(m_deg - r_deg)

            breakers = []
            positive_factors = []
            is_mitigated = False
            plausibility = 88.0 if diff <= (10.0 / 3.0) else (82.0 if diff <= 10.0 else 72.0)

            if m_sign == EXALTATION_SIGNS.get("Mars"):
                is_mitigated = True
                positive_factors.append("Mars is Exalted in Capricorn: Extreme tactical discipline harnesses raw volcanic energy constructively.")
            elif m_sign in OWN_SIGNS.get("Mars", []):
                is_mitigated = True
                positive_factors.append(f"Mars in Domicile ({m_sign}): Strong martial self-command channels competitive heat.")
            elif m_sign == DEBILITATION_SIGNS.get("Mars"):
                is_nb, nb_reasons, _ = audit_neecha_bhanga("Mars", chart)
                if is_nb:
                    is_mitigated = True
                    positive_factors.extend(nb_reasons)
                else:
                    breakers.append(YogaBreakerDetail(
                        factor="Debilitated Mars (Nīca Kuja)",
                        culprit_planet="Mars",
                        description="Mars is debilitated in Cancer without cancellation, producing volatile emotional reactivity.",
                        penalty=25.0
                    ))

            if m_house in (3, 6, 10, 11):
                positive_factors.append(f"Placed in Upachaya House {m_house}: Martial fire aggressively crushes obstacles and competition.")

            asp_jup = get_aspect_score(chart, "Jupiter", "Mars")
            if asp_jup >= ASPECT_PALPABLE_THRESHOLD:
                is_mitigated = True
                positive_factors.append(f"Palpable Jupiter aspect ({asp_jup:.1f} Virūpas) cools martial aggression into principled courage.")
                plausibility -= 8.0
            elif asp_jup >= ASPECT_MARGINAL_THRESHOLD:
                positive_factors.append(f"Marginal Jupiter aspect ({asp_jup:.1f} Virūpas) moderates impulsiveness.")
                plausibility -= 4.0

            status = YogaStatus.RESCUED if (is_mitigated and not breakers) else YogaStatus.STAINED

            detected.append(YogaInstance(
                id="angaraka_yoga",
                name="Aṅgāraka Yoga",
                category=YogaCategory.CHANDAL,
                status=status,
                plausibility_score=round(max(20.0, min(100.0, plausibility)), 1),
                participating_planets=["Mars", "Rahu"],
                participating_houses=[m_house] if m_house > 0 else [],
                scripture_ref="Saravali Ch. 31, BPHS Ch. 45",
                archetype="The Volcanic Catalyst: Rahu injects high-octane fuel into Mars's tactical fire, producing relentless courage or volatile aggression.",
                manifestation_effects=[
                    "Explosive energy drive, fearlessness, decisive physical action, and tactical prowess.",
                    "Strained: Risk of impulsive temper, burn-out, or disputes if not channeled toward a disciplined craft.",
                    f"Conjoined within {diff:.2f}° in House {m_house} ({m_sign})."
                ],
                positive_factors=positive_factors,
                breakers=breakers
            ))

    return detected

"""
jyotish/yogas/character_growth_yogas.py
Character & Growth Yogas (Gaja Kesarī, Śakaṭa, Vasumatī, Amalā).
Scripture: Phaladeepika Ch. 6.13–17, BPHS Ch. 36–37.
"""

from typing import List, Dict, Any
from jyotish.yogas.models import (
    YogaInstance, YogaCategory, YogaStatus, YogaBreakerDetail
)
from jyotish.yogas.breakers import (
    get_house_of_planet, get_sign_of_planet, get_planets_data, get_lagna_sign,
    audit_trishadaya_interference, audit_combustion, audit_dusthana_placement,
    audit_dispositor_vitality, audit_shadbala_muscle, audit_neecha_bhanga,
    resolve_yoga_score_and_status,
    OWN_SIGNS, EXALTATION_SIGNS, DEBILITATION_SIGNS
)

BENEFICS = ("Jupiter", "Venus", "Mercury")
MAJOR_BENEFICS = ("Jupiter", "Venus")
MALEFICS = ("Sun", "Mars", "Saturn", "Rahu", "Ketu")
UPACHAYA_HOUSES = (3, 6, 10, 11)
KENDRA_HOUSES = (1, 4, 7, 10)


def detect_character_and_growth_yogas(chart: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[YogaInstance]:
    """Scans chart for Gaja Kesarī, Śakaṭa, Vasumatī, and Amalā Yogas."""
    detected: List[YogaInstance] = []
    lagna_sign = get_lagna_sign(chart)
    planets_data = get_planets_data(chart)
    if not lagna_sign or not planets_data:
        return detected

    planet_houses = {p: get_house_of_planet(chart, p) for p in planets_data}
    moon_house = planet_houses.get("Moon", 0)
    jup_house = planet_houses.get("Jupiter", 0)

    # =========================================================================
    # 1. GAJA KESARĪ YOGA (The Lion & Elephant Majesty — Phaladeepika 6.13 / 6.17)
    # =========================================================================
    # Jupiter in a Kendra (1, 4, 7, 10) from the Moon.
    if moon_house > 0 and jup_house > 0:
        jup_from_moon = ((jup_house - moon_house) % 12) + 1
        if jup_from_moon in KENDRA_HOUSES:
            score_gk = 82.0
            pos_factors_gk = [
                f"Jupiter (H{jup_house}) occupies Kendra position ({jup_from_moon}th house) from the Moon (H{moon_house})."
            ]
            breakers_gk: List[YogaBreakerDetail] = []

            # Lagna Kendra confluence bonus
            if jup_house in KENDRA_HOUSES and moon_house in KENDRA_HOUSES:
                score_gk += 8.0
                pos_factors_gk.append(
                    f"Both Jupiter (H{jup_house}) and Moon (H{moon_house}) also occupy Kendras from the Ascendant."
                )
            elif jup_house in KENDRA_HOUSES:
                score_gk += 5.0
                pos_factors_gk.append(f"Jupiter is additionally anchored in Lagna Kendra House {jup_house}.")

            # Planetary dignity check for Jupiter and Moon
            for gk_p in ("Jupiter", "Moon"):
                p_sign = get_sign_of_planet(chart, gk_p)
                if p_sign == EXALTATION_SIGNS.get(gk_p) or p_sign in OWN_SIGNS.get(gk_p, []):
                    score_gk += 5.0
                    pos_factors_gk.append(f"{gk_p} is fortified in dignity in {p_sign}.")
                elif p_sign == DEBILITATION_SIGNS.get(gk_p):
                    is_rescued, _, _ = audit_neecha_bhanga(gk_p, chart)
                    if not is_rescued:
                        breakers_gk.append(YogaBreakerDetail(
                            factor="Unrescued Planetary Debilitation",
                            culprit_planet=gk_p,
                            description=f"{gk_p} is debilitated in {p_sign} without Neecha Bhanga, dampening Gaja Kesarī majesty.",
                            penalty=25.0
                        ))

            gk_houses = list(dict.fromkeys([moon_house, jup_house]))
            dust_brk_gk = audit_dusthana_placement(gk_houses, YogaCategory.CHARACTER.value)
            if dust_brk_gk:
                breakers_gk.append(dust_brk_gk)
            breakers_gk.extend(audit_trishadaya_interference(["Moon", "Jupiter"], chart))
            breakers_gk.extend(audit_combustion(["Jupiter"], chart))
            breakers_gk.extend(audit_dispositor_vitality(["Moon", "Jupiter"], chart))
            shadbala_brk_gk, shadbala_pos_gk = audit_shadbala_muscle(["Moon", "Jupiter"], chart)
            breakers_gk.extend(shadbala_brk_gk)
            pos_factors_gk.extend(shadbala_pos_gk)

            final_score_gk, status_gk = resolve_yoga_score_and_status(score_gk, breakers_gk)

            detected.append(YogaInstance(
                id="gaja_kesari_yoga",
                name="Gaja Kesarī Yoga",
                category=YogaCategory.CHARACTER,
                status=status_gk,
                plausibility_score=final_score_gk,
                participating_planets=["Moon", "Jupiter"],
                participating_houses=gk_houses,
                scripture_ref="Phaladeepika 6.13 & 6.17",
                archetype="Lion-Like Majesty: Jupiter in an angle from the Moon fuses wisdom with emotional intelligence, conferring lasting reputation.",
                manifestation_effects=[
                    "Formidable personal dignity, sharp intellect, and self-reliant authority.",
                    "Enduring reputation, noble alliances, and victory over opposition like a lion before elephants."
                ],
                positive_factors=pos_factors_gk,
                breakers=breakers_gk
            ))

    # =========================================================================
    # 2. ŚAKAṬA YOGA (The Heavy Cart & Setbacks — Phaladeepika 6.13 / 6.14)
    # =========================================================================
    # Moon in 6th, 8th, or 12th from Jupiter.
    # Cancellation (Śakaṭa Bhaṅga): Moon in Kendra from Lagna, Jupiter in Kendra from Lagna,
    # or Moon in own/exalted sign.
    if moon_house > 0 and jup_house > 0:
        rel_from_jup = ((moon_house - jup_house) % 12) + 1
        if rel_from_jup in (6, 8, 12):
            moon_in_lagna_kendra = moon_house in KENDRA_HOUSES
            jup_in_lagna_kendra = jup_house in KENDRA_HOUSES
            moon_sign_cur = get_sign_of_planet(chart, "Moon")
            moon_dignified = (
                moon_sign_cur == EXALTATION_SIGNS.get("Moon")
                or moon_sign_cur in OWN_SIGNS.get("Moon", [])
            )

            if moon_in_lagna_kendra or jup_in_lagna_kendra or moon_dignified:
                rescue_reasons = []
                if moon_in_lagna_kendra:
                    rescue_reasons.append(
                        f"Moon in Kendra House {moon_house} from Lagna completely rescues the {rel_from_jup}th-house dusthana tension from Jupiter."
                    )
                if jup_in_lagna_kendra:
                    rescue_reasons.append(
                        f"Jupiter in Kendra House {jup_house} from Lagna provides angular protection (Śakaṭa Bhaṅga)."
                    )
                if moon_dignified:
                    rescue_reasons.append(
                        f"Moon is fortified in {moon_sign_cur}, stabilizing lunar fortunes against oscillation."
                    )
                rescued_score = 72.0 if moon_in_lagna_kendra else 68.0

                detected.append(YogaInstance(
                    id="sakata_yoga_rescued",
                    name="Śakaṭa Yoga (Rescued / Bhaṅga)",
                    category=YogaCategory.CHARACTER,
                    status=YogaStatus.RESCUED,
                    plausibility_score=rescued_score,
                    participating_planets=["Moon", "Jupiter"],
                    participating_houses=list(dict.fromkeys([moon_house, jup_house])),
                    scripture_ref="Phaladeepika 6.14",
                    archetype="Neutralized Cart Friction: Moon sits in a dusthana from Jupiter but is anchored by Lagna Kendra/dignity, averting chronic loss.",
                    manifestation_effects=[
                        "Initial fluctuations between sudden success and temporary setbacks resolve into steady worldly balance.",
                        "Angular/dignified protection preserves reputation and maintains overall career stability."
                    ],
                    positive_factors=rescue_reasons,
                    breakers=[]
                ))
            else:
                breakers_sk = [
                    YogaBreakerDetail(
                        factor="Adverse Lunar-Jupiter Axis",
                        culprit_planet="Moon",
                        description=f"Moon is placed in House {rel_from_jup} (Dusthana) from Jupiter without Kendra anchor from Lagna.",
                        penalty=30.0
                    )
                ]
                detected.append(YogaInstance(
                    id="sakata_yoga",
                    name="Śakaṭa Yoga (The Heavy Cart)",
                    category=YogaCategory.CHARACTER,
                    status=YogaStatus.STAINED,
                    plausibility_score=60.0,
                    participating_planets=["Moon", "Jupiter"],
                    participating_houses=list(dict.fromkeys([moon_house, jup_house])),
                    scripture_ref="Phaladeepika 6.14",
                    archetype="The Rolling Cart: Fortunes oscillate like a heavy cartwheel; periods of progress alternate with exhausting setbacks.",
                    manifestation_effects=[
                        "Cycles of rapid financial/professional gain followed by unexpected downturns or fatigue.",
                        "Feels like pulling a heavy cart alone; requires emotional resilience and steadfast perseverance."
                    ],
                    positive_factors=[
                        f"Moon in House {moon_house}, Jupiter in House {jup_house} ({rel_from_jup}th axis)."
                    ],
                    breakers=breakers_sk
                ))

    # =========================================================================
    # 3. VASUMATĪ YOGA (Immense Self-Generating Wealth — Phaladeepika 6.15 / 6.16)
    # =========================================================================
    # Natural benefics (Jupiter, Venus, Mercury) in Upachayas (3, 6, 10, 11)
    # from Lagna OR from Moon. Requires at least 2 benefics (never a single isolated benefic!).
    benefics_in_upachaya_lagna = [
        p for p in BENEFICS
        if planet_houses.get(p, 0) in UPACHAYA_HOUSES
    ]

    benefics_in_upachaya_moon = []
    if moon_house > 0:
        benefics_in_upachaya_moon = [
            p for p in BENEFICS
            if planet_houses.get(p, 0) > 0
            and ((planet_houses[p] - moon_house) % 12 + 1) in UPACHAYA_HOUSES
        ]

    count_lagna = len(benefics_in_upachaya_lagna)
    count_moon = len(benefics_in_upachaya_moon)

    if count_lagna >= 2 or count_moon >= 2:
        is_dual_upachaya = (count_lagna >= 2 and count_moon >= 2)

        if count_lagna >= count_moon:
            best_count = count_lagna
            best_planets = benefics_in_upachaya_lagna
            base_ref = "Ascendant (Lagna)"
            rel_house_desc = ", ".join(f"{p} in H{planet_houses[p]}" for p in best_planets)
        else:
            best_count = count_moon
            best_planets = benefics_in_upachaya_moon
            base_ref = "Moon (Chandra)"
            rel_house_desc = ", ".join(
                f"{p} in H{planet_houses[p]} ({((planet_houses[p] - moon_house) % 12) + 1}th from Moon)"
                for p in best_planets
            )

        houses_inv = list(dict.fromkeys([planet_houses[p] for p in best_planets]))

        if count_lagna == 3 and count_moon == 3:
            tier_name = "Uttama Dual-Reference (Supreme Riches)"
            score_v = 95.0
            effect_desc = "Grants inexhaustible, self-generating wealth anchored from both Ascendant and Moon; commands abundant independent assets."
        elif best_count == 3:
            tier_name = "Uttama (Peerless Riches)"
            score_v = 90.0 + (4.0 if is_dual_upachaya else 0.0)
            effect_desc = "Grants inexhaustible, self-generating wealth; the native commands abundant independent income without financial want."
        else:
            has_both_major = all(mb in best_planets for mb in MAJOR_BENEFICS)
            tier_name = "Madhyama (Substantial Wealth)"
            score_v = (82.0 if has_both_major else 76.0) + (4.0 if is_dual_upachaya else 0.0)
            effect_desc = "Grants strong financial stability, comfortable real assets (Vasu), and enduring commercial independence."

        pos_factors_v = [
            f"{best_count} natural benefics ({', '.join(best_planets)}) occupy Upachaya houses from {base_ref}: {rel_house_desc}."
        ]
        if is_dual_upachaya:
            pos_factors_v.append(
                f"Dual Upachaya alignment: {count_lagna} benefic(s) in Upachaya from Lagna and {count_moon} from Moon."
            )

        # Anti-Interference Rule (YOGA_ARCHITECTURE.md Row 13):
        # Exclude Upachaya houses (3, 6, 11) from Trishadaya penalties and NEVER penalize H6 in Vasumatī!
        breakers_v: List[YogaBreakerDetail] = []
        breakers_v.extend(audit_trishadaya_interference(best_planets, chart, exclude_houses=(3, 6, 11)))
        breakers_v.extend(audit_combustion(best_planets, chart))
        breakers_v.extend(audit_dispositor_vitality(best_planets, chart))
        shadbala_brk_v, shadbala_pos_v = audit_shadbala_muscle(best_planets, chart)
        breakers_v.extend(shadbala_brk_v)
        pos_factors_v.extend(shadbala_pos_v)

        final_score_v, status_v = resolve_yoga_score_and_status(score_v, breakers_v)

        detected.append(YogaInstance(
            id=f"vasumati_yoga_{best_count}",
            name=f"Vasumatī Yoga — {tier_name}",
            category=YogaCategory.CHARACTER,
            status=status_v,
            plausibility_score=final_score_v,
            participating_planets=best_planets,
            participating_houses=houses_inv,
            scripture_ref="Phaladeepika 6.16",
            archetype=f"Growth-House Fortification: {best_count} natural benefics occupy Upachayas from {base_ref}, driving steady accumulation of wealth.",
            manifestation_effects=[
                effect_desc,
                f"Continuous asset generation represented by {', '.join(best_planets)} in growth houses (Upachayas)."
            ],
            positive_factors=pos_factors_v,
            breakers=breakers_v
        ))

    # =========================================================================
    # 4. AMALĀ YOGA (The Spotless Reputation — Phaladeepika 6.15 / 6.17)
    # =========================================================================
    # Natural benefic(s) occupying the 10th house from Lagna OR Moon.
    h10_lagna = 10
    h10_moon = ((moon_house + 8) % 12) + 1 if moon_house > 0 else 0

    for base_name, target_h in [("Lagna", h10_lagna), ("Moon", h10_moon)]:
        if target_h <= 0:
            continue
        benefics_in_10 = [p for p in BENEFICS if planet_houses.get(p) == target_h]
        malefics_in_10 = [p for p in MALEFICS if planet_houses.get(p) == target_h]

        if benefics_in_10:
            score_am = 85.0
            pos_factors_am = [
                f"Natural benefic(s) ({', '.join(benefics_in_10)}) occupy the 10th house (House {target_h}) from {base_name}."
            ]
            if base_name == "Lagna" and h10_lagna == h10_moon:
                score_am += 5.0
                pos_factors_am.append("House 10 is simultaneously the 10th house from both Lagna and Moon.")

            breakers_am: List[YogaBreakerDetail] = []
            if malefics_in_10:
                breakers_am.append(YogaBreakerDetail(
                    factor="Malefic Corruption in 10th House",
                    culprit_planet=", ".join(malefics_in_10),
                    description=f"Natural malefic(s) ({', '.join(malefics_in_10)}) share the 10th house, tarnishing spotless public standing.",
                    penalty=35.0
                ))

            dust_brk_am = audit_dusthana_placement([target_h], YogaCategory.CHARACTER.value)
            if dust_brk_am:
                breakers_am.append(dust_brk_am)
            breakers_am.extend(audit_combustion(benefics_in_10, chart))
            breakers_am.extend(audit_dispositor_vitality(benefics_in_10, chart))
            shadbala_brk_am, shadbala_pos_am = audit_shadbala_muscle(benefics_in_10, chart)
            breakers_am.extend(shadbala_brk_am)
            pos_factors_am.extend(shadbala_pos_am)

            final_score_am, status_am = resolve_yoga_score_and_status(score_am, breakers_am)

            detected.append(YogaInstance(
                id=f"amala_yoga_{base_name.lower()}",
                name=f"Amalā Yoga (from {base_name})",
                category=YogaCategory.CHARACTER,
                status=status_am,
                plausibility_score=final_score_am,
                participating_planets=benefics_in_10,
                participating_houses=[target_h],
                scripture_ref="Phaladeepika 6.17",
                archetype=f"The Spotless Legacy: Benefic ({', '.join(benefics_in_10)}) in the 10th house from {base_name}, bestowing civic virtue and lasting honor.",
                manifestation_effects=[
                    "Spotless public reputation, respected career standing, and unblemished character.",
                    "Lasting societal honor, philanthropic achievements, and moral leadership."
                ],
                positive_factors=pos_factors_am,
                breakers=breakers_am
            ))
            if base_name == "Lagna" and h10_lagna == h10_moon:
                break

    return detected
