"""
jyotish/yogas/dhana_daridrya.py
Dhana (Wealth) and Daridrya (Poverty/Depletion) Yogas.
Scripture: BPHS Ch. 41 (Vishesha Dhana Yoga), Ch. 42 (Daridrya Yoga), Phaladeepika Ch. 6.
"""

from typing import List, Dict, Any, Tuple, Set
from jyotish.yogas.models import (
    YogaInstance, YogaCategory, YogaStatus, YogaBreakerDetail
)
from jyotish.yogas.breakers import (
    get_lagna_sign, get_house_of_planet, get_sign_of_planet, get_house_rulers,
    get_planets_data, get_aspect_score, _first_ruler,
    audit_trishadaya_interference, audit_combustion, audit_dispositor_vitality,
    audit_dusthana_placement, audit_shadbala_muscle, audit_neecha_bhanga,
    resolve_yoga_score_and_status,
    EXALTATION_SIGNS, OWN_SIGNS, DEBILITATION_SIGNS, SIGN_LORDS,
    ASPECT_PALPABLE_THRESHOLD, ASPECT_MARGINAL_THRESHOLD
)

DHANA_HOUSES = (1, 2, 5, 9, 11)
KENDRA_TRIKONA_HOUSES = (1, 4, 5, 7, 9, 10)
DUSTHANA_HOUSES = (6, 8, 12)
BENEFICS = ("Jupiter", "Venus", "Mercury")


def detect_dhana_and_daridrya_yogas(chart: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[YogaInstance]:
    """Scans chart for wealth (Dhana) and poverty/depletion (Daridrya) yogas."""
    detected: List[YogaInstance] = []
    lagna_sign = get_lagna_sign(chart)
    planets_data = get_planets_data(chart)
    if not lagna_sign or not planets_data:
        return detected

    house_rulers = get_house_rulers(chart)
    lord_1 = _first_ruler(house_rulers, 1)
    lord_2 = _first_ruler(house_rulers, 2)
    lord_5 = _first_ruler(house_rulers, 5)
    lord_9 = _first_ruler(house_rulers, 9)
    lord_11 = _first_ruler(house_rulers, 11)

    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. LAKṢMĪ YOGA (9th Lord + Venus fortified in Kendras/Trikoṇas + Lagneśa check)
    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    if lord_9 and lord_9 in planets_data and "Venus" in planets_data:
        l9_house = get_house_of_planet(chart, lord_9)
        l9_sign = get_sign_of_planet(chart, lord_9)
        v_house = get_house_of_planet(chart, "Venus")
        v_sign = get_sign_of_planet(chart, "Venus")

        is_l9_strong = (
            l9_house in KENDRA_TRIKONA_HOUSES and
            (l9_sign == EXALTATION_SIGNS.get(lord_9) or l9_sign in OWN_SIGNS.get(lord_9, []))
        )
        is_v_strong = (
            v_house in KENDRA_TRIKONA_HOUSES and
            (v_sign == EXALTATION_SIGNS.get("Venus") or v_sign in OWN_SIGNS.get("Venus", []))
        )

        if is_l9_strong and is_v_strong:
            score = 95.0
            participants = list(dict.fromkeys([lord_9, "Venus"]))
            houses_inv = list(dict.fromkeys([l9_house, v_house]))
            pos_factors = [
                f"9th Lord ({lord_9}) is fortified in House {l9_house} ({l9_sign}).",
                f"Venus is fortified in House {v_house} ({v_sign})."
            ]
            breakers: List[YogaBreakerDetail] = []

            # Phaladeepika 6.28 & BPHS 36.27-28: Check Ascendant Lord (Lagnesha) strength
            if lord_1 and lord_1 in planets_data:
                l1_house = get_house_of_planet(chart, lord_1)
                l1_sign = get_sign_of_planet(chart, lord_1)
                l1_dignified = (
                    l1_sign == EXALTATION_SIGNS.get(lord_1) or
                    l1_sign in OWN_SIGNS.get(lord_1, [])
                )
                l1_deb = (l1_sign == DEBILITATION_SIGNS.get(lord_1))
                l1_rescued = audit_neecha_bhanga(lord_1, chart)[0] if l1_deb else False

                if l1_dignified or l1_house in KENDRA_TRIKONA_HOUSES:
                    score += 3.0
                    pos_factors.append(f"Ascendant Lord ({lord_1}) supports from House {l1_house} ({l1_sign}).")
                elif (l1_deb and not l1_rescued) or (l1_house in DUSTHANA_HOUSES and not l1_dignified):
                    breakers.append(YogaBreakerDetail(
                        factor="Weakened Ascendant Lord (Lagneśa)",
                        culprit_planet=lord_1,
                        description=f"1st Lord ({lord_1}) is in House {l1_house} ({l1_sign}), reducing the vessel's capacity to hold Lakṣmī Yoga's full splendor.",
                        penalty=20.0
                    ))

            # Category 6 (DHANA) Layer 2 Breakers: NEVER penalize 11th lord!
            breakers.extend(audit_trishadaya_interference(participants, chart, exclude_houses=(11,)))
            breakers.extend(audit_combustion(participants, chart))
            breakers.extend(audit_dispositor_vitality(participants, chart))
            sb_brk, sb_pos = audit_shadbala_muscle(participants, chart)
            breakers.extend(sb_brk)
            pos_factors.extend(sb_pos)

            final_score, status = resolve_yoga_score_and_status(score, breakers)

            detected.append(YogaInstance(
                id="lakshmi_yoga",
                name="Lakṣmī Yoga",
                category=YogaCategory.DHANA,
                status=status,
                plausibility_score=final_score,
                participating_planets=participants,
                participating_houses=houses_inv,
                scripture_ref="Phaladeepika 6.24 & 6.28, BPHS 36.27-28",
                archetype="The Golden Grace: Bestows boundless wealth, aesthetic charm, virtuous livelihood, and noble character.",
                manifestation_effects=[
                    "Immense wealth, royal vehicles, widespread social goodwill, and ethical integrity.",
                    "Enjoyment of all life comforts without loss of moral principles."
                ],
                positive_factors=pos_factors,
                breakers=breakers
            ))

    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # 2A. CORE MULTI-PLANET DHANA YOGAS (All 10 Classical BPHS Ch. 41 Pairs)
    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    dhana_pairs: List[Tuple[str, str, str, float]] = [
        (lord_2, lord_11, "Treasury (H2) & Profit (H11) Primary Wealth Alliance", 88.0),
        (lord_1, lord_2, "Self (H1) & Wealth (H2) Union", 85.0),
        (lord_1, lord_11, "Self (H1) & Gains (H11) Union", 85.0),
        (lord_5, lord_9, "Supreme Trinal Merit (H5 & H9) Fusion", 88.0),
        (lord_2, lord_5, "Treasury (H2) & Creative Merit (H5) Alliance", 86.0),
        (lord_2, lord_9, "Wealth (H2) & Divine Fortune (H9) Alliance", 88.0),
        (lord_5, lord_11, "Intellect (H5) & Large Gains (H11) Polarity", 86.0),
        (lord_9, lord_11, "Divine Fortune (H9) & Gains (H11) Alliance", 88.0),
        (lord_1, lord_5, "Self (H1) & Merit (H5) Wealth-Grace Union", 84.0),
        (lord_1, lord_9, "Self (H1) & Fortune (H9) Wealth-Grace Union", 85.0),
    ]

    evaluated_dhana: Set[Tuple[str, str]] = set()
    for p1, p2, desc, base_score in dhana_pairs:
        if not p1 or not p2 or p1 == p2 or p1 not in planets_data or p2 not in planets_data:
            continue
        key = tuple(sorted([p1, p2]))
        if key in evaluated_dhana:
            continue

        h1 = get_house_of_planet(chart, p1)
        h2 = get_house_of_planet(chart, p2)
        if h1 <= 0 or h2 <= 0:
            continue

        s1 = get_sign_of_planet(chart, p1)
        s2 = get_sign_of_planet(chart, p2)
        is_conj = (h1 == h2) or (bool(s1) and s1 == s2)
        is_exchange = (SIGN_LORDS.get(s1) == p2 and SIGN_LORDS.get(s2) == p1)

        aspect_val = max(get_aspect_score(chart, p1, p2), get_aspect_score(chart, p2, p1))
        is_aspect = (aspect_val >= ASPECT_PALPABLE_THRESHOLD)
        is_marginal_aspect = (not is_conj) and (not is_exchange) and (not is_aspect) and (aspect_val >= ASPECT_MARGINAL_THRESHOLD)

        if not (is_conj or is_exchange or is_aspect or is_marginal_aspect):
            continue

        evaluated_dhana.add(key)
        score = base_score
        pos: List[str] = []
        breakers: List[YogaBreakerDetail] = []

        if is_conj:
            pos.append(f"{desc}: {p1} and {p2} unite in House {h1} ({s1}).")
        elif is_exchange:
            pos.append(f"{desc}: {p1} (H{h1}) and {p2} (H{h2}) exchange signs (Parivartana).")
            score += 4.0
        else:
            pos.append(f"{desc}: {p1} and {p2} connect via aspect ({aspect_val:.0f} Virūpas).")

        # Dignity bonus / unredeemed debility check
        for dp in (p1, p2):
            dp_sign = get_sign_of_planet(chart, dp)
            if dp_sign == EXALTATION_SIGNS.get(dp) or dp_sign in OWN_SIGNS.get(dp, []):
                score += 4.0
                pos.append(f"{dp} is fortified in dignity in {dp_sign}.")
            elif dp_sign == DEBILITATION_SIGNS.get(dp):
                is_rescued, nb_reasons, _ = audit_neecha_bhanga(dp, chart)
                if is_rescued:
                    pos.extend(nb_reasons)
                else:
                    breakers.append(YogaBreakerDetail(
                        factor="Unredeemed Debilitation",
                        culprit_planet=dp,
                        description=f"Wealth lord {dp} is debilitated in {dp_sign} without cancellation.",
                        penalty=25.0
                    ))

        if is_marginal_aspect:
            breakers.append(YogaBreakerDetail(
                factor="Marginal Aspect Connection",
                culprit_planet=f"{p1}/{p2}",
                description=f"Aspect strength ({aspect_val:.0f} Virūpas) is below the palpable 45.0 Virūpa threshold.",
                penalty=25.0
            ))

        houses_inv = list(dict.fromkeys([h1, h2]))
        dust_brk = audit_dusthana_placement(houses_inv, YogaCategory.DHANA.value)
        if dust_brk is not None:
            breakers.append(dust_brk)

        # Category 6 (DHANA) Layer 2 Breakers: NEVER penalize the 11th lord!
        breakers.extend(audit_trishadaya_interference([p1, p2], chart, exclude_houses=(11,)))
        breakers.extend(audit_combustion([p1, p2], chart))
        if not is_exchange:
            breakers.extend(audit_dispositor_vitality([p1, p2], chart))
        sb_brk, sb_pos = audit_shadbala_muscle([p1, p2], chart)
        breakers.extend(sb_brk)
        pos.extend(sb_pos)

        final_score, status = resolve_yoga_score_and_status(score, breakers)

        detected.append(YogaInstance(
            id=f"dhana_yoga_{p1.lower()}_{p2.lower()}",
            name=f"Dhana Yoga ({p1} + {p2})",
            category=YogaCategory.DHANA,
            status=status,
            plausibility_score=final_score,
            participating_planets=[p1, p2],
            participating_houses=houses_inv,
            scripture_ref="BPHS 41.1-15",
            archetype="Resource Flow: Systematic connection between wealth, earnings, intellect, and fortune.",
            manifestation_effects=[
                "Substantial capacity to generate, retain, and expand financial assets.",
                "High earnings during the conjoined or operational Daśā periods."
            ],
            positive_factors=pos,
            breakers=breakers
        ))

    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # 2B. SINGLE-PLANET MULTI-DHANA LORDSHIP (BPHS 41.2–12)
    # (e.g., Taurus Mercury H2+H5, Leo Mercury H2+H11, Virgo Venus H2+H9,
    #  Scorpio Jupiter H2+H5, Aquarius Jupiter H2+H11, Pisces Mars H2+H9)
    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    planet_dhana_houses: Dict[str, List[int]] = {}
    for dh in (2, 5, 9, 11):
        ruler = _first_ruler(house_rulers, dh)
        if ruler:
            planet_dhana_houses.setdefault(ruler, []).append(dh)

    for p_dhana, owned_dh in planet_dhana_houses.items():
        if len(owned_dh) >= 2 and (2 in owned_dh or 11 in owned_dh) and p_dhana in planets_data:
            p_h = get_house_of_planet(chart, p_dhana)
            p_s = get_sign_of_planet(chart, p_dhana)
            is_dignified = (p_s == EXALTATION_SIGNS.get(p_dhana) or p_s in OWN_SIGNS.get(p_dhana, []))
            is_deb = (p_s == DEBILITATION_SIGNS.get(p_dhana))

            if p_h in (1, 2, 4, 5, 7, 9, 10, 11) and not is_deb:
                score = 86.0 + (6.0 if is_dignified else 0.0)
                owned_str = " & ".join(f"H{h}" for h in owned_dh)
                pos = [
                    f"Single-Planet Dual Dhana Kāraka: {p_dhana} simultaneously rules wealth houses {owned_str} for {lagna_sign} Lagna and occupies House {p_h} ({p_s})."
                ]
                if is_dignified:
                    pos.append(f"{p_dhana} is fortified in dignity in {p_s}.")

                breakers = []
                breakers.extend(audit_trishadaya_interference([p_dhana], chart, exclude_houses=(11,)))
                breakers.extend(audit_combustion([p_dhana], chart))
                breakers.extend(audit_dispositor_vitality([p_dhana], chart))
                sb_brk, sb_pos = audit_shadbala_muscle([p_dhana], chart)
                breakers.extend(sb_brk)
                pos.extend(sb_pos)

                final_score, status = resolve_yoga_score_and_status(score, breakers)

                detected.append(YogaInstance(
                    id=f"single_dhana_yoga_{p_dhana.lower()}",
                    name=f"Viśeṣa Dhana Kāraka Yoga ({p_dhana} — Rules {owned_str})",
                    category=YogaCategory.DHANA,
                    status=status,
                    plausibility_score=final_score,
                    participating_planets=[p_dhana],
                    participating_houses=list(dict.fromkeys([p_h] + owned_dh)),
                    scripture_ref="BPHS 41.2-12",
                    archetype=f"Unified Wealth Conduit: {p_dhana} unites the lordships of {owned_str} within a single planet in House {p_h}.",
                    manifestation_effects=[
                        f"Direct, self-contained wealth generation during {p_dhana}'s operational periods.",
                        "Links liquid savings with investment or career gains without requiring an external partner."
                    ],
                    positive_factors=pos,
                    breakers=breakers
                ))

    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. DĀRIDRYA YOGAS (POVERTY & LEAKAGE - BPHS Ch. 42)
    # Affliction Status Invariant (YOGA_ARCHITECTURE.md Row 7):
    # - NEVER call audit_dusthana_placement (already a Dusthana affliction yoga!)
    # - Use YogaStatus.STAINED when unmitigated, YogaStatus.RESCUED when mitigated — NEVER PURE!
    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    for p_lord, h_num in [(lord_2, 2), (lord_11, 11)]:
        if not p_lord or p_lord not in planets_data:
            continue
        h_pos = get_house_of_planet(chart, p_lord)
        if h_pos in DUSTHANA_HOUSES:
            sign_pos = get_sign_of_planet(chart, p_lord)
            is_exalted = (sign_pos == EXALTATION_SIGNS.get(p_lord))
            is_own = (sign_pos in OWN_SIGNS.get(p_lord, []))
            is_deb = (sign_pos == DEBILITATION_SIGNS.get(p_lord))
            is_nb_rescued = audit_neecha_bhanga(p_lord, chart)[0] if is_deb else False

            benefic_aspects = [
                bp for bp in BENEFICS
                if bp != p_lord and get_aspect_score(chart, bp, p_lord) >= ASPECT_PALPABLE_THRESHOLD
            ]

            is_mitigated = bool(is_exalted or is_own or is_nb_rescued or benefic_aspects)
            pos_factors: List[str] = []
            if is_exalted or is_own:
                pos_factors.append(
                    f"Dāridrya Bhaṅga (Dignity Rescue): {p_lord} occupies {'exalted' if is_exalted else 'own'} sign ({sign_pos}) in House {h_pos}, protecting assets from depletion."
                )
            if is_nb_rescued:
                pos_factors.append(
                    f"Dāridrya Bhaṅga (Nīcabhaṅga): Debility of {p_lord} in House {h_pos} is cancelled."
                )
            if benefic_aspects:
                pos_factors.append(
                    f"Dāridrya Bhaṅga (Śubha Dṛṣṭi): {p_lord} receives palpable benefic aspect from {', '.join(benefic_aspects)}."
                )

            if is_mitigated:
                status = YogaStatus.RESCUED
                score = 52.0
                penalty_val = 15.0
            else:
                status = YogaStatus.STAINED
                score = 72.0 if is_deb else 62.0
                penalty_val = 30.0

            detected.append(YogaInstance(
                id=f"daridrya_h{h_num}_{p_lord.lower()}",
                name=f"Dāridrya Yoga (H{h_num} Lord {p_lord} in H{h_pos})" + (" (Mitigated)" if is_mitigated else ""),
                category=YogaCategory.DARIDRYA,
                status=status,
                plausibility_score=score,
                participating_planets=[p_lord],
                participating_houses=[h_pos],
                scripture_ref="BPHS 42.1-5",
                archetype="Financial Leakage: Ruler of earnings or treasury sits in a Dusthana of loss, debt, or crisis.",
                manifestation_effects=[
                    "Tendency for wealth to leak into sudden expenses, medical bills, legal battles, or bad debts."
                    if not is_mitigated else
                    "Initial financial volatility or high overhead in House " + str(h_pos) + " is cushioned and stabilized by planetary dignity/benefic support.",
                    "Requires strict conscious budgeting and avoiding speculative gambles."
                ],
                positive_factors=pos_factors,
                breakers=[YogaBreakerDetail(
                    factor=f"Lord of H{h_num} in Dusthana H{h_pos}",
                    culprit_planet=p_lord,
                    description=f"{p_lord} (ruler of House {h_num}) is relegated to House {h_pos}, causing financial drains.",
                    penalty=penalty_val
                )]
            ))

    return detected


================================================================================
FILE: dispositor_root_yogas.py

"""
jyotish/yogas/dispositor_root_yogas.py
Dispositor Root Yogas: Kāhala Yoga (Variants A & B), Parvata Yoga (Authentic, Self-Dispositor & BPHS),
and Puṣkala Yoga.
Scripture: Phaladeepika 6.18, 6.33–36, BPHS Ch. 36.
"""

from typing import List, Dict, Any
from jyotish.yogas.models import (
    YogaInstance, YogaCategory, YogaStatus, YogaBreakerDetail
)
from jyotish.yogas.breakers import (
    get_house_of_planet, get_sign_of_planet, get_house_rulers, get_planets_data,
    get_lagna_sign, get_aspect_score, _first_ruler,
    audit_trishadaya_interference, audit_combustion, audit_dusthana_placement,
    audit_dispositor_vitality, audit_shadbala_muscle, resolve_yoga_score_and_status,
    audit_neecha_bhanga,
    SIGN_LORDS, OWN_SIGNS, EXALTATION_SIGNS, DEBILITATION_SIGNS, ZODIAC_SIGNS,
    ASPECT_PALPABLE_THRESHOLD, ASPECT_MARGINAL_THRESHOLD
)
from jyotish.relationships.relationships import (
    get_natural_relationship, get_temporary_relationship, get_compound_relationship
)

KENDRA_TRIKONA_HOUSES = (1, 4, 5, 7, 9, 10)
KENDRA_HOUSES = (1, 4, 7, 10)
BENEFICS = ("Jupiter", "Venus", "Mercury")
MALEFICS = ("Sun", "Mars", "Saturn", "Rahu", "Ketu")


def detect_puskala_yoga(chart: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[YogaInstance]:
    """
    Detects Puṣkala Yoga (Phaladeepika 6.18):
    1. Lord of Moon sign (Candra-rāśīśa) and Lord of Ascendant (Lagneśa) conjoin in a Kendra
       in a friendly (or own/exalted) sign.
    2. A strong/auspicious planet palpably aspects the Ascendant (>= 45.0 Virūpas).
    3. The Ascendant is occupied or fortified by a strong/auspicious planet.
    """
    detected: List[YogaInstance] = []
    lagna_sign = get_lagna_sign(chart)
    planets_data = get_planets_data(chart)
    if not lagna_sign or not planets_data:
        return detected

    house_rulers = get_house_rulers(chart)
    lagna_lord = _first_ruler(house_rulers, 1)
    moon_sign = get_sign_of_planet(chart, "Moon")
    moon_lord = SIGN_LORDS.get(moon_sign)

    if not lagna_lord or not moon_lord or lagna_lord not in planets_data or moon_lord not in planets_data:
        return detected

    planet_houses = {p: get_house_of_planet(chart, p) for p in planets_data}
    ll_house = planet_houses.get(lagna_lord, 0)
    ml_house = planet_houses.get(moon_lord, 0)
    moon_house = planet_houses.get("Moon", 0)

    # Guard against single-planet self-conjunction tautology when lagna_lord == moon_lord
    # (unless the Moon itself is conjoined with lagna_lord in that Kendra)
    if lagna_lord == moon_lord and moon_house != ll_house:
        return detected

    if ll_house != ml_house or ll_house not in KENDRA_HOUSES:
        return detected

    shared_sign = get_sign_of_planet(chart, lagna_lord)
    sign_host = SIGN_LORDS.get(shared_sign)
    if not shared_sign or not sign_host:
        return detected

    # Reject if either lord is debilitated in shared_sign without Neecha Bhanga
    for lord_p in dict.fromkeys([lagna_lord, moon_lord]):
        if shared_sign == DEBILITATION_SIGNS.get(lord_p):
            is_rescued, _, _ = audit_neecha_bhanga(lord_p, chart)
            if not is_rescued:
                return detected

    # Check if shared_sign is a friendly, own, or exaltation sign
    is_friendly_host = False
    if (
        sign_host in (lagna_lord, moon_lord)
        or shared_sign == EXALTATION_SIGNS.get(lagna_lord)
        or shared_sign == EXALTATION_SIGNS.get(moon_lord)
    ):
        is_friendly_host = True
    else:
        host_actual_sign = get_sign_of_planet(chart, sign_host)
        nat_rel_ll = get_natural_relationship(lagna_lord, sign_host)
        nat_rel_ml = get_natural_relationship(moon_lord, sign_host)
        if shared_sign in ZODIAC_SIGNS and host_actual_sign in ZODIAC_SIGNS:
            ll_idx = ZODIAC_SIGNS.index(shared_sign)
            host_idx = ZODIAC_SIGNS.index(host_actual_sign)
            temp_rel = get_temporary_relationship(ll_idx, host_idx)
            comp_ll = get_compound_relationship(nat_rel_ll, temp_rel)
            comp_ml = get_compound_relationship(nat_rel_ml, temp_rel)
            is_friendly_host = (
                comp_ll in ("Friend", "Great Friend")
                or comp_ml in ("Friend", "Great Friend")
                or nat_rel_ll == "Friend"
                or nat_rel_ml == "Friend"
            )
        else:
            is_friendly_host = (nat_rel_ll == "Friend" or nat_rel_ml == "Friend")

    if not is_friendly_host:
        return detected

    # Pillar 2: Check aspect on Lagna (palpable >= 45.0 Virūpas from a strong planet, or marginal >= 30.0)
    palpable_aspectors: List[str] = []
    marginal_aspectors: List[str] = []
    for p in planets_data:
        if p in ("Rahu", "Ketu"):
            continue
        asp = get_aspect_score(chart, p, "Lagna")
        if asp >= ASPECT_PALPABLE_THRESHOLD:
            palpable_aspectors.append(p)
        elif asp >= ASPECT_MARGINAL_THRESHOLD:
            marginal_aspectors.append(p)

    # Pillar 3: Check if Lagna (House 1) is occupied by a strong/benefic planet
    h1_occupants = [
        p for p, h in planet_houses.items()
        if h == 1 and (
            p in BENEFICS
            or p in (lagna_lord, moon_lord)
            or get_sign_of_planet(chart, p) == EXALTATION_SIGNS.get(p)
            or get_sign_of_planet(chart, p) in OWN_SIGNS.get(p, [])
        )
    ]

    score = 85.0
    yoga_planets = list(dict.fromkeys(
        [lagna_lord, moon_lord] + (["Moon"] if lagna_lord == moon_lord else [])
    ))
    pos_factors = [
        f"Ascendant Lord ({lagna_lord}) and Moon Dispositor ({moon_lord}) unite in Kendra House {ll_house} ({shared_sign}).",
        f"Hosted favorably in {shared_sign} (ruled by {sign_host})."
    ]
    breakers: List[YogaBreakerDetail] = []

    if palpable_aspectors:
        score += 5.0
        pos_factors.append(
            f"Ascendant receives a palpable aspect (>= 45 Virūpas) from {', '.join(palpable_aspectors)}."
        )
    elif marginal_aspectors:
        breakers.append(YogaBreakerDetail(
            factor="Marginal Aspect on Lagna",
            culprit_planet="Lagna",
            description=f"Ascendant receives only a marginal aspect (30–44.9 Virūpas) from {', '.join(marginal_aspectors)}.",
            penalty=10.0
        ))
    else:
        breakers.append(YogaBreakerDetail(
            factor="Missing Palpable Aspect on Lagna",
            culprit_planet="Lagna",
            description="The Ascendant does not receive a palpable aspect from a strong planet.",
            penalty=15.0
        ))

    if h1_occupants:
        score += 5.0
        pos_factors.append(
            f"Ascendant (House 1) is directly fortified by {', '.join(h1_occupants)}."
        )

    # Layer 2 Breakers (Category 12: DISPOSITOR_ROOT)
    dust_brk = audit_dusthana_placement([ll_house], YogaCategory.DISPOSITOR_ROOT.value)
    if dust_brk:
        breakers.append(dust_brk)
    breakers.extend(audit_trishadaya_interference(yoga_planets, chart))
    breakers.extend(audit_combustion(yoga_planets, chart))
    breakers.extend(audit_dispositor_vitality(yoga_planets, chart))
    shadbala_brk, shadbala_pos = audit_shadbala_muscle(yoga_planets, chart)
    breakers.extend(shadbala_brk)
    pos_factors.extend(shadbala_pos)

    final_score, status = resolve_yoga_score_and_status(score, breakers)

    detected.append(YogaInstance(
        id="puskala_yoga",
        name="Puṣkala Yoga",
        category=YogaCategory.DISPOSITOR_ROOT,
        status=status,
        plausibility_score=final_score,
        participating_planets=yoga_planets,
        participating_houses=list(dict.fromkeys([ll_house, 1])),
        scripture_ref="Phaladeepika 6.18",
        archetype="Abundant Splendor: Ascendant Lord and Moon Dispositor combine in an auspicious angular seat, bestowing magnetic prestige and wealth.",
        manifestation_effects=[
            "Overflowing material wealth, sweet and persuasive speech, and high social renown.",
            "Honored by government or corporate superiors; broad civic influence."
        ],
        positive_factors=pos_factors,
        breakers=breakers
    ))
    return detected


def detect_dispositor_root_yogas(chart: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[YogaInstance]:
    """Scans chart for Parvata, Kāhala, and Puṣkala dispositor-root yogas."""
    detected: List[YogaInstance] = []
    lagna_sign = get_lagna_sign(chart)
    planets_data = get_planets_data(chart)
    house_rulers = get_house_rulers(chart)

    if not lagna_sign or not planets_data:
        return detected

    lagna_lord = _first_ruler(house_rulers, 1)
    if not lagna_lord or lagna_lord not in planets_data:
        return detected

    planet_houses = {p: get_house_of_planet(chart, p) for p in planets_data}
    ll_house = planet_houses.get(lagna_lord, 0)
    ll_sign = get_sign_of_planet(chart, lagna_lord)
    ll_dispositor = SIGN_LORDS.get(ll_sign)

    ll_is_exalted = (ll_sign == EXALTATION_SIGNS.get(lagna_lord))
    ll_is_own = (ll_sign in OWN_SIGNS.get(lagna_lord, []))
    ll_in_kt = ll_house in KENDRA_TRIKONA_HOUSES

    # =========================================================================
    # 1. PARVATA YOGA (Mountainous / Enduring Nobility — Phaladeepika 6.36 & BPHS 36.11–12)
    # =========================================================================
    authentic_parvata_formed = False

    if (ll_is_exalted or ll_is_own) and ll_in_kt and ll_dispositor:
        is_self_dispositor = (ll_dispositor == lagna_lord)

        if is_self_dispositor:
            # Self-Dispositor Variant: 1st lord in own sign in Kendra/Trikona (e.g. H1)
            # Pre-breaker base 100.0 minus 30.0 Single-Tier Loophole penalty = 70.0 net (STAINED) when unafflicted.
            pre_score = 100.0
            breakers_self: List[YogaBreakerDetail] = [
                YogaBreakerDetail(
                    factor="Single-Tier Self-Dispositor Loophole",
                    culprit_planet=lagna_lord,
                    description=f"{lagna_lord} hosts itself in House {ll_house}. Lacks an external multi-tier dispositor root chain.",
                    penalty=30.0
                )
            ]
            breakers_self.extend(audit_trishadaya_interference([lagna_lord], chart))
            breakers_self.extend(audit_combustion([lagna_lord], chart))
            shadbala_brk_s, shadbala_pos_s = audit_shadbala_muscle([lagna_lord], chart)
            breakers_self.extend(shadbala_brk_s)

            final_score_s, status_s = resolve_yoga_score_and_status(pre_score, breakers_self)
            pos_factors_s = [
                f"Ascendant Lord ({lagna_lord}) resides in House {ll_house} in its own sign ({ll_sign})."
            ] + shadbala_pos_s

            detected.append(YogaInstance(
                id="parvata_yoga_self_dispositor",
                name="Parvata Yoga (Self-Dispositor Variant)",
                category=YogaCategory.DISPOSITOR_ROOT,
                status=status_s,
                plausibility_score=final_score_s,
                participating_planets=[lagna_lord],
                participating_houses=[ll_house],
                scripture_ref="Phaladeepika 6.36 & Vic DiCara Lecture 1",
                archetype="Self-Hosted Mountain: Ascendant Lord sits in its own sign in an angle, providing strong self-reliance (~70% weight).",
                manifestation_effects=[
                    "High personal autonomy, dignified bearing, and reliable self-generated fortune.",
                    "Represents strong single-tier self-anchoring rather than multi-generational dispositor tree depth."
                ],
                positive_factors=pos_factors_s,
                breakers=breakers_self
            ))
        elif ll_dispositor in planets_data:
            # Multi-tier Authentic Parvata Yoga: External dispositor chain
            disp_house = planet_houses.get(ll_dispositor, 0)
            disp_sign = get_sign_of_planet(chart, ll_dispositor)
            disp_is_exalted = (disp_sign == EXALTATION_SIGNS.get(ll_dispositor))
            disp_is_own = (disp_sign in OWN_SIGNS.get(ll_dispositor, []))
            disp_in_kt = disp_house in KENDRA_TRIKONA_HOUSES

            if (disp_is_exalted or disp_is_own) and disp_in_kt:
                authentic_parvata_formed = True
                score_p = 95.0
                yoga_planets_p = [lagna_lord, ll_dispositor]
                yoga_houses_p = list(dict.fromkeys([ll_house, disp_house]))

                breakers_p: List[YogaBreakerDetail] = []
                dust_brk_p = audit_dusthana_placement(yoga_houses_p, YogaCategory.DISPOSITOR_ROOT.value)
                if dust_brk_p:
                    breakers_p.append(dust_brk_p)
                breakers_p.extend(audit_trishadaya_interference(yoga_planets_p, chart))
                breakers_p.extend(audit_combustion(yoga_planets_p, chart))
                breakers_p.extend(audit_dispositor_vitality(yoga_planets_p, chart))
                shadbala_brk_p, shadbala_pos_p = audit_shadbala_muscle(yoga_planets_p, chart)
                breakers_p.extend(shadbala_brk_p)

                final_score_p, status_p = resolve_yoga_score_and_status(score_p, breakers_p)
                pos_factors_p = [
                    f"Ascendant Lord ({lagna_lord}) is {'exalted' if ll_is_exalted else 'in own sign'} in supportive House {ll_house} ({ll_sign}).",
                    f"Host dispositor ({ll_dispositor}) is {'exalted' if disp_is_exalted else 'in own sign'} in supportive House {disp_house} ({disp_sign})."
                ] + shadbala_pos_p

                detected.append(YogaInstance(
                    id="parvata_yoga_authentic",
                    name="Parvata Yoga (Authentic Multi-Tier Root)",
                    category=YogaCategory.DISPOSITOR_ROOT,
                    status=status_p,
                    plausibility_score=final_score_p,
                    participating_planets=yoga_planets_p,
                    participating_houses=yoga_houses_p,
                    scripture_ref="Phaladeepika 6.36 & Vic DiCara Lecture 1",
                    archetype="The Mountain of Enduring Nobility: Both Lagna Lord and its host dispositor blaze in high dignity in Kendras/Trikoṇas, guaranteeing permanent status.",
                    manifestation_effects=[
                        "Unshakeable societal stature, illustrious renown, and generational prosperity.",
                        "Enduring moral character; like a mountain, cannot be shaken by passing political or social storms."
                    ],
                    positive_factors=pos_factors_p,
                    breakers=breakers_p
                ))

    # BPHS Parvata Variant (BPHS 36.11–12): Benefics in Kendras, with H6 and H8 empty or occupied only by benefics
    kendra_benefics = [p for p in BENEFICS if planet_houses.get(p, 0) in KENDRA_HOUSES]
    h6_8_malefics = [p for p in MALEFICS if planet_houses.get(p, 0) in (6, 8)]
    h6_8_all = [p for p, h in planet_houses.items() if h in (6, 8)]
    if len(kendra_benefics) >= 2 and not h6_8_malefics and not authentic_parvata_formed:
        score_bp = 85.0
        kb_houses = list(dict.fromkeys([planet_houses[p] for p in kendra_benefics]))
        breakers_bp: List[YogaBreakerDetail] = []
        breakers_bp.extend(audit_trishadaya_interference(kendra_benefics, chart))
        breakers_bp.extend(audit_combustion(kendra_benefics, chart))
        breakers_bp.extend(audit_dispositor_vitality(kendra_benefics, chart))
        shadbala_brk_bp, shadbala_pos_bp = audit_shadbala_muscle(kendra_benefics, chart)
        breakers_bp.extend(shadbala_brk_bp)

        final_score_bp, status_bp = resolve_yoga_score_and_status(score_bp, breakers_bp)
        h6_8_desc = (
            "Houses 6 and 8 are completely unoccupied."
            if not h6_8_all
            else f"Houses 6 and 8 contain only natural benefics ({', '.join(h6_8_all)}) with zero malefics."
        )
        detected.append(YogaInstance(
            id="parvata_yoga_bphs",
            name="Parvata Yoga (BPHS Kendra-Benefic Variant)",
            category=YogaCategory.DISPOSITOR_ROOT,
            status=status_bp,
            plausibility_score=final_score_bp,
            participating_planets=kendra_benefics,
            participating_houses=kb_houses,
            scripture_ref="BPHS 36.11-12",
            archetype="The Sanctuary Mountain: Natural benefics fortify the Kendras while Houses 6 and 8 remain pure of malefic agitation.",
            manifestation_effects=[
                "Enduring civic peace, charitable leadership, and lasting material prosperity.",
                "Protected from chronic enmity or sudden structural collapse."
            ],
            positive_factors=[
                f"Natural benefics ({', '.join(kendra_benefics)}) occupy Kendra houses ({', '.join(str(h) for h in kb_houses)}).",
                h6_8_desc
            ] + shadbala_pos_bp,
            breakers=breakers_bp
        ))

    # =========================================================================
    # 2. KĀHALA YOGA (Dual Scriptural Traditions — Phaladeepika 6.35 & BPHS 36)
    # =========================================================================

    # Variant A: 4th & 10th lords (or 4th & 9th / Jupiter per BPHS) in mutual Kendras
    # Strictly requires two distinct planets (p1 != p2) to prevent Dual-Lagna self-Kendra tautology!
    lord_4 = _first_ruler(house_rulers, 4)
    lord_9 = _first_ruler(house_rulers, 9)
    lord_10 = _first_ruler(house_rulers, 10)

    kahala_a_candidates = [
        (lord_4, lord_10, "4th Lord", "10th Lord"),
        (lord_4, lord_9, "4th Lord", "9th Lord"),
        (lord_4, "Jupiter", "4th Lord", "Jupiter"),
    ]

    for p1, p2, label1, label2 in kahala_a_candidates:
        if not p1 or not p2 or p1 == p2 or p1 not in planets_data or p2 not in planets_data:
            continue
        h1_pos = planet_houses.get(p1, 0)
        h2_pos = planet_houses.get(p2, 0)
        if h1_pos <= 0 or h2_pos <= 0:
            continue

        diff_pos = ((h2_pos - h1_pos) % 12) + 1
        if diff_pos in KENDRA_HOUSES:
            score_a = 85.0
            if ll_is_exalted or ll_is_own:
                score_a += 5.0

            yoga_planets_a = list(dict.fromkeys([p1, p2, lagna_lord]))
            yoga_houses_a = list(dict.fromkeys([h1_pos, h2_pos, ll_house]))

            breakers_a: List[YogaBreakerDetail] = []
            if ll_house in (6, 8, 12):
                breakers_a.append(YogaBreakerDetail(
                    factor="Lagnesha in Dusthana",
                    culprit_planet=lagna_lord,
                    description=f"Ascendant Lord ({lagna_lord}) sits in Dusthana House {ll_house}, weakening the foundational root of the {label1}/{label2} alliance.",
                    penalty=25.0
                ))

            dust_brk_a = audit_dusthana_placement([h1_pos, h2_pos], YogaCategory.DISPOSITOR_ROOT.value)
            if dust_brk_a:
                breakers_a.append(dust_brk_a)
            breakers_a.extend(audit_trishadaya_interference([p1, p2], chart))
            breakers_a.extend(audit_combustion(yoga_planets_a, chart))
            breakers_a.extend(audit_dispositor_vitality([p1, p2], chart))
            shadbala_brk_a, shadbala_pos_a = audit_shadbala_muscle(yoga_planets_a, chart)
            breakers_a.extend(shadbala_brk_a)

            final_score_a, status_a = resolve_yoga_score_and_status(score_a, breakers_a)
            pos_factors_a = [
                f"{label1} ({p1} in H{h1_pos}) and {label2} ({p2} in H{h2_pos}) occupy mutual Kendra ({diff_pos}th from each other).",
                f"Ascendant Lord is {lagna_lord} in House {ll_house}."
            ] + shadbala_pos_a

            detected.append(YogaInstance(
                id="kahala_yoga_variant_a",
                name="Kāhala Yoga (Variant A: Mutual Kendra Alliance)",
                category=YogaCategory.DISPOSITOR_ROOT,
                status=status_a,
                plausibility_score=final_score_a,
                participating_planets=yoga_planets_a,
                participating_houses=yoga_houses_a,
                scripture_ref="Phaladeepika 6.35 & BPHS Ch. 36 (Tradition A)",
                archetype=f"The Resounding Drum: {label1} and {label2} occupy mutual angles, generating dynamic organizational rhythm.",
                manifestation_effects=[
                    "Dynamic executive stamina, unwavering resolve, and organizational leadership.",
                    "Ability to lead teams and mobilize resources toward prominent civic or corporate success."
                ],
                positive_factors=pos_factors_a,
                breakers=breakers_a
            ))
            break

    # Variant B: Dispositor Root Chain (Phaladeepika 6.35)
    # 1. Requires external dispositor (ll_dispositor != lagna_lord)
    # 2. Avoids redundant double-emission when Authentic Parvata is already formed
    # 3. Supports both 1-step (1L -> disp1) and 2-step (1L -> disp1 -> disp2) dispositor roots
    if ll_dispositor and ll_dispositor != lagna_lord and ll_dispositor in planets_data and not authentic_parvata_formed:
        disp_house = planet_houses.get(ll_dispositor, 0)
        disp_sign = get_sign_of_planet(chart, ll_dispositor)
        disp_is_exalted = (disp_sign == EXALTATION_SIGNS.get(ll_dispositor))
        disp_is_own = (disp_sign in OWN_SIGNS.get(ll_dispositor, []))
        disp_in_kt = disp_house in KENDRA_TRIKONA_HOUSES

        root_planet = None
        root_house = 0
        root_sign = ""
        root_is_exalted = False
        yoga_planets_b: List[str] = []
        yoga_houses_b: List[int] = []
        chain_desc = ""

        if (disp_is_exalted or disp_is_own) and disp_in_kt:
            root_planet = ll_dispositor
            root_house = disp_house
            root_sign = disp_sign
            root_is_exalted = disp_is_exalted
            yoga_planets_b = list(dict.fromkeys([lagna_lord, ll_dispositor]))
            yoga_houses_b = list(dict.fromkeys([ll_house, disp_house]))
            chain_desc = f"Ascendant Lord ({lagna_lord}) hosted in {ll_sign} by {ll_dispositor}."
        else:
            # Check 2-step root: Dispositor of the 1st Lord's dispositor (1L -> disp1 -> disp2)
            disp2 = SIGN_LORDS.get(disp_sign)
            if disp2 and disp2 not in (lagna_lord, ll_dispositor) and disp2 in planets_data:
                disp2_house = planet_houses.get(disp2, 0)
                disp2_sign = get_sign_of_planet(chart, disp2)
                disp2_is_exalted = (disp2_sign == EXALTATION_SIGNS.get(disp2))
                disp2_is_own = (disp2_sign in OWN_SIGNS.get(disp2, []))
                disp2_in_kt = disp2_house in KENDRA_TRIKONA_HOUSES
                if (disp2_is_exalted or disp2_is_own) and disp2_in_kt:
                    root_planet = disp2
                    root_house = disp2_house
                    root_sign = disp2_sign
                    root_is_exalted = disp2_is_exalted
                    yoga_planets_b = list(dict.fromkeys([lagna_lord, ll_dispositor, disp2]))
                    yoga_houses_b = list(dict.fromkeys([ll_house, disp_house, disp2_house]))
                    chain_desc = (
                        f"Two-step dispositor chain: Ascendant Lord ({lagna_lord} in {ll_sign}) -> "
                        f"Dispositor ({ll_dispositor} in {disp_sign}) -> Root Dispositor ({disp2})."
                    )

        if root_planet:
            score_b = 90.0
            breakers_b: List[YogaBreakerDetail] = []
            dust_brk_b = audit_dusthana_placement(yoga_houses_b, YogaCategory.DISPOSITOR_ROOT.value)
            if dust_brk_b:
                breakers_b.append(dust_brk_b)
            breakers_b.extend(audit_trishadaya_interference(yoga_planets_b, chart))
            breakers_b.extend(audit_combustion(yoga_planets_b, chart))
            breakers_b.extend(audit_dispositor_vitality([lagna_lord], chart))
            shadbala_brk_b, shadbala_pos_b = audit_shadbala_muscle(yoga_planets_b, chart)
            breakers_b.extend(shadbala_brk_b)

            final_score_b, status_b = resolve_yoga_score_and_status(score_b, breakers_b)
            pos_factors_b = [
                chain_desc,
                f"Root dispositor ({root_planet}) is {'exalted' if root_is_exalted else 'in own sign'} in supportive House {root_house} ({root_sign})."
            ] + shadbala_pos_b

            detected.append(YogaInstance(
                id="kahala_yoga_variant_b",
                name="Kāhala Yoga (Variant B: Dispositor Root Chain)",
                category=YogaCategory.DISPOSITOR_ROOT,
                status=status_b,
                plausibility_score=final_score_b,
                participating_planets=yoga_planets_b,
                participating_houses=yoga_houses_b,
                scripture_ref="Phaladeepika 6.35 (Tradition B & Vic DiCara Lecture 1)",
                archetype="Strong Dispositor Roots: The root dispositor of the Ascendant Lord blazes with dignity in an angle or trine, infusing the personality with unshakeable fortitude.",
                manifestation_effects=[
                    "Deep inner confidence, steady growth (Vṛddhi), optimism (Prasanna), and unyielding courage.",
                    "The native stands firm against setbacks because their underlying dispositor root is deeply anchored."
                ],
                positive_factors=pos_factors_b,
                breakers=breakers_b
            ))

    # =========================================================================
    # 3. PUṢKALA YOGA (Abundant Splendor — Phaladeepika 6.18)
    # =========================================================================
    detected.extend(detect_puskala_yoga(chart))

    return detected

"""
jyotish/yogas/evaluator.py
Master Yoga Evaluator and Orchestration Engine.
Integrates all sub-modules, performs global deduplication, and computes chart-wide metrics.
"""

from typing import List, Dict, Any, Set
from jyotish.yogas.models import YogaInstance, YogaCategory, YogaStatus
from jyotish.yogas.breakers import (
    get_house_rulers, get_house_of_planet, get_lagna_sign, get_planets_data, _first_ruler
)
from jyotish.yogas.pancha_mahapurusha import detect_pancha_mahapurusha_yogas
from jyotish.yogas.raja_yogas import detect_raja_yogas
from jyotish.yogas.dhana_daridrya import detect_dhana_and_daridrya_yogas
from jyotish.yogas.lunar_solar_yogas import detect_lunar_and_solar_yogas
from jyotish.yogas.parivartana import detect_parivartana_yogas
from jyotish.yogas.viparita import detect_viparita_raja_yogas
from jyotish.yogas.kartari import detect_kartari_yogas
from jyotish.yogas.chandal_yogas import detect_chandal_yogas
from jyotish.yogas.character_growth_yogas import detect_character_and_growth_yogas
from jyotish.yogas.dispositor_root_yogas import detect_dispositor_root_yogas
from jyotish.yogas.deity_yogas import detect_deity_yogas
from jyotish.yogas.bhava_yogas import detect_bhava_yogas
from jyotish.yogas.power_yogas import detect_chapter7_power_yogas
from jyotish.yogas.contextual_yogas import detect_contextual_yogas
from jyotish.yogas.neechabhanga import detect_neechabhanga_yogas

# Cross-module deduplication groups: maps variant IDs to a single canonical concept
CANONICAL_DUPLICATE_GROUPS = {
    # Kemadruma variants
    "kemadruma_yoga": "KEMADRUMA",
    "kemadruma_bhanga_yoga": "KEMADRUMA",
    # Mahabhagya variants
    "mahabhagya_yoga": "MAHABHAGYA",
    "mahabhagya_male_yoga": "MAHABHAGYA",
    "mahabhagya_female_yoga": "MAHABHAGYA",
    # Sun-Moon Quadrant Scope variants
    "adhama_yoga": "SUN_MOON_QUADRANT",
    "madhya_yoga": "SUN_MOON_QUADRANT",
    "sama_yoga": "SUN_MOON_QUADRANT",
    "varishtha_yoga": "SUN_MOON_QUADRANT",
    "kendra_scope_yoga": "SUN_MOON_QUADRANT",
    "panaphara_scope_yoga": "SUN_MOON_QUADRANT",
    "apoklima_scope_yoga": "SUN_MOON_QUADRANT",
    # Kartari variants
    "shubha_kartari_lagna": "KARTARI_LAGNA_SHUBHA",
    "subha_kartari_lagna": "KARTARI_LAGNA_SHUBHA",
    "shubhakartari_lagna_yoga": "KARTARI_LAGNA_SHUBHA",
    "shubha_kartari_lagna_(ascendant)": "KARTARI_LAGNA_SHUBHA",
    "papa_kartari_lagna": "KARTARI_LAGNA_PAPA",
    "papakartari_lagna_yoga": "KARTARI_LAGNA_PAPA",
    "papa_kartari_lagna_(ascendant)": "KARTARI_LAGNA_PAPA",
    "shubha_kartari_moon": "KARTARI_MOON_SHUBHA",
    "papa_kartari_moon": "KARTARI_MOON_PAPA",
    # Solar Flanking variants
    "vesi_yoga": "SOLAR_VESI",
    "subha_vesi_yoga": "SOLAR_VESI",
    "papa_vesi_yoga": "SOLAR_VESI",
    "vosi_yoga": "SOLAR_VOSI",
    "vasi_yoga": "SOLAR_VOSI",
    "subha_vosi_yoga": "SOLAR_VOSI",
    "papa_vosi_yoga": "SOLAR_VOSI",
    "ubhayacari_yoga": "SOLAR_UBHAYACARI",
    "ubhayachari_yoga": "SOLAR_UBHAYACARI",
    "subha_ubhayacari_yoga": "SOLAR_UBHAYACARI",
    "papa_ubhayacari_yoga": "SOLAR_UBHAYACARI",
    "misra_ubhayacari_yoga": "SOLAR_UBHAYACARI",
    # Lakshmi Yoga cross-module overlap (dhana_daridrya.py vs deity_yogas.py)
    "lakshmi_yoga": "LAKSHMI_YOGA",
    "tridevi_lakshmi_yoga": "LAKSHMI_YOGA",
    "deity_lakshmi_yoga": "LAKSHMI_YOGA",
    # Viparita vs Bhava 6/8/12 Reversal overlap (viparita.py vs bhava_yogas.py)
    "harsha_viparita_yoga": "VIPARITA_HARSHA",
    "harsha_yoga": "VIPARITA_HARSHA",
    "sarala_viparita_yoga": "VIPARITA_SARALA",
    "sarala_yoga": "VIPARITA_SARALA",
    "vimala_viparita_yoga": "VIPARITA_VIMALA",
    "vimala_yoga": "VIPARITA_VIMALA",
    # Dharma-Karmadhipati Exchange (raja_yogas.py vs parivartana.py vs power_yogas.py)
    "power_yoga_dharma_karma_exchange": "DHARMA_KARMA_EXCHANGE",
}


def detect_all_yogas(chart: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Master entry point for Classical Yoga Detection in Astra.
    Scans the chart across all classical categories, audits Yoga Breakers,
    deduplicates overlapping definitions, and returns a structured, categorized payload.
    Supports either ChartPipeline or standard chart dict.
    """
    if hasattr(chart, "to_dict") and not isinstance(chart, dict):
        chart = chart.to_dict()
    if not isinstance(chart, dict):
        chart = {}

    categories = [cat.value for cat in YogaCategory]

    # Guard against empty chart payloads or missing Lagna/planets data
    if not get_lagna_sign(chart) or not get_planets_data(chart):
        return {
            "total_count": 0,
            "summary": {
                "pure": 0,
                "stained": 0,
                "rescued": 0,
                "broken": 0
            },
            "yogas": [],
            "categories": categories
        }

    raw_yogas: List[YogaInstance] = []

    # 1. Pancha Mahapurusha Yogas
    raw_yogas.extend(detect_pancha_mahapurusha_yogas(chart))

    # 2. Raja Yogas (Single-planet & Multi-planet)
    raw_yogas.extend(detect_raja_yogas(chart))

    # 3. Dhana & Daridrya Yogas
    raw_yogas.extend(detect_dhana_and_daridrya_yogas(chart))

    # 4. Lunar and Solar Yogas (Flanking, Sun-Moon quadrants, Candradhi, Lagnadhi)
    raw_yogas.extend(detect_lunar_and_solar_yogas(chart))

    # 5. Parivartana Yogas (Maha, Khala, Dainya)
    raw_yogas.extend(detect_parivartana_yogas(chart))

    # 6. Viparita Raja Yogas (Harsha, Sarala, Vimala)
    raw_yogas.extend(detect_viparita_raja_yogas(chart))

    # 7. Kartari Yogas (Universal Hemming)
    raw_yogas.extend(detect_kartari_yogas(chart))

    # 8. Chandal & Nodal Affliction Yogas
    raw_yogas.extend(detect_chandal_yogas(chart))

    # 9. Character & Growth Yogas (Vasumati, Amala, Puskala, Sakata)
    raw_yogas.extend(detect_character_and_growth_yogas(chart))

    # 10. Dispositor Root Yogas (Kahala Variants A/B, Parvata Authentic & Self-Dispositor)
    raw_yogas.extend(detect_dispositor_root_yogas(chart))

    # 11. Cosmic Deity Yogas (Trimurti, Tridevi, Mala)
    raw_yogas.extend(detect_deity_yogas(chart))

    # 12. 12 Bhava Good & Converse Yogas
    raw_yogas.extend(detect_bhava_yogas(chart))

    # 13. Chapter 7 Royal Power Yogas (Multi-Kendra, Digbala, Specific Power Yogas)
    raw_yogas.extend(detect_chapter7_power_yogas(chart))

    # 14. Contextual & Macro Setup Yogas (Sankhya, Solitary Bhavas, Dual-Lagna)
    raw_yogas.extend(detect_contextual_yogas(chart))

    # 15. Neechabhanga Yogas (Universal Debilitation Reversal & Cancellation)
    raw_yogas.extend(detect_neechabhanga_yogas(chart))

    # Dynamic Duplicate Grouping: 9th–10th Lord Parivartana Cross-Module Deduplication
    canonical_groups = dict(CANONICAL_DUPLICATE_GROUPS)
    house_rulers = get_house_rulers(chart)
    lord_9 = _first_ruler(house_rulers, 9)
    lord_10 = _first_ruler(house_rulers, 10)
    if lord_9 and lord_10 and lord_9 != lord_10:
        h9_pos = get_house_of_planet(chart, lord_9)
        h10_pos = get_house_of_planet(chart, lord_10)
        if h9_pos == 10 and h10_pos == 9:
            p1 = lord_9.lower()
            p2 = lord_10.lower()
            for prefix in (
                "maha_parivartana",
                "dharma_karma_raja_yoga",
                "dharma_karmadhipati_yoga",
                "dharma_karmadhipati_raja_yoga",
                "raja_yoga",
            ):
                canonical_groups[f"{prefix}_{p1}_{p2}"] = "DHARMA_KARMA_EXCHANGE"
                canonical_groups[f"{prefix}_{p2}_{p1}"] = "DHARMA_KARMA_EXCHANGE"

    # Global Deduplication (retaining first-seen canonical group after prioritizing more complete/higher-plausibility instances)
    seen_ids: Set[str] = set()
    seen_canonical_groups: Set[str] = set()
    deduped_yogas: List[YogaInstance] = []

    for yoga in raw_yogas:
        if not isinstance(yoga, YogaInstance):
            continue
        if yoga.id in seen_ids:
            continue

        group_key = canonical_groups.get(yoga.id)
        if group_key:
            if group_key in seen_canonical_groups:
                continue
            seen_canonical_groups.add(group_key)

        seen_ids.add(yoga.id)
        deduped_yogas.append(yoga)

    # Deterministic sort by plausibility score descending, then ID ascending
    deduped_yogas.sort(key=lambda y: (-float(y.plausibility_score or 0.0), str(y.id or "")))

    pure_count = sum(1 for y in deduped_yogas if y.status == YogaStatus.PURE)
    stained_count = sum(1 for y in deduped_yogas if y.status == YogaStatus.STAINED)
    rescued_count = sum(1 for y in deduped_yogas if y.status == YogaStatus.RESCUED)
    broken_count = sum(1 for y in deduped_yogas if y.status == YogaStatus.BROKEN)

    return {
        "total_count": len(deduped_yogas),
        "summary": {
            "pure": pure_count,
            "stained": stained_count,
            "rescued": rescued_count,
            "broken": broken_count
        },
        "yogas": [y.to_dict() for y in deduped_yogas],
        "categories": categories
    }

FILE: kartari.py

"""
jyotish/yogas/kartari.py
Kartarī Yogas (The Universal Hemming Principle — Hemisphere A: Pure Foundational Calculus).

Scripture: Phaladeepika Ch. 6, Verses 7 & 11-13; BPHS Ch. 34.
Architecture: Strictly adheres to YOGA_ARCHITECTURE.md Category 10 (KARTARI):
- Zero generic Layer 2 breakers (no Trishadaya, Dusthana, Combustion, Dispositor, or Shadbala).
- Verifies strict benefic/malefic purity of BOTH the 2nd AND 12th houses around Lagna (and Moon).
- Evaluates opposing/direct aspects onto the hemmed focal point (Suśubha enhancement vs. benefic rescue mitigation).
- Enforces Affliction Status Invariant: Pāpa Kartarī is STAINED when unmitigated and RESCUED when mitigated by a strong benefic aspect/occupant — NEVER PURE.
"""

from typing import List, Dict, Any, Set
from jyotish.yogas.models import (
    YogaInstance, YogaCategory, YogaStatus, YogaBreakerDetail
)
from jyotish.yogas.breakers import (
    get_lagna_sign, get_house_of_planet, get_planets_data, get_aspect_score,
    NATURAL_BENEFICS, NATURAL_MALEFICS, ZODIAC_SIGNS,
    ASPECT_PALPABLE_THRESHOLD
)

VALID_KARTARI_GRAHAS: Set[str] = set(NATURAL_BENEFICS) | set(NATURAL_MALEFICS)


def detect_kartari_yogas(chart: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[YogaInstance]:
    """
    Scans for Śubha Kartarī (protective benefic hemming) and Pāpa Kartarī (restrictive malefic scissors)
    around Lagna and Moon, including Suśubha aspect reinforcement and benefic aspect mitigation.
    """
    detected: List[YogaInstance] = []
    planets_data = get_planets_data(chart)
    planet_houses = {p: get_house_of_planet(chart, p) for p in planets_data}

    lagna_sign = get_lagna_sign(chart)
    moon_house = planet_houses.get("Moon", 0)

    targets = []
    if lagna_sign and lagna_sign in ZODIAC_SIGNS:
        targets.append(("Lagna (Ascendant)", 1, "Ascendant / Physical Vessel", "Lagna"))
    if moon_house > 0:
        targets.append(("Moon (Chandra)", moon_house, "Emotional Mind / Manas", "Moon"))

    for target_name, target_house, domain, target_key in targets:
        h2 = (target_house % 12) + 1
        h12 = ((target_house - 2) % 12) + 1

        # Filter strictly to recognized classical grahas + nodes in H2 and H12
        planets_h2 = [
            p for p in planets_data
            if p in VALID_KARTARI_GRAHAS and planet_houses.get(p) == h2 and p != target_key
        ]
        planets_h12 = [
            p for p in planets_data
            if p in VALID_KARTARI_GRAHAS and planet_houses.get(p) == h12 and p != target_key
        ]

        if not (planets_h2 and planets_h12):
            continue

        h2_benefics = [p for p in planets_h2 if p in NATURAL_BENEFICS]
        h12_benefics = [p for p in planets_h12 if p in NATURAL_BENEFICS]
        h2_malefics = [p for p in planets_h2 if p in NATURAL_MALEFICS]
        h12_malefics = [p for p in planets_h12 if p in NATURAL_MALEFICS]

        # Check occupants and palpable aspects (Suśubha / Mitigation) onto the hemmed focal point
        focal_occupants = [
            p for p in planets_data
            if p in VALID_KARTARI_GRAHAS and planet_houses.get(p) == target_house and p != target_key
        ]
        benefic_protectors = [p for p in focal_occupants if p in NATURAL_BENEFICS]
        malefic_afflictors = [p for p in focal_occupants if p in NATURAL_MALEFICS]

        for p in planets_data:
            if p not in VALID_KARTARI_GRAHAS or p == target_key or p in focal_occupants:
                continue
            if get_aspect_score(chart, p, target_key) >= ASPECT_PALPABLE_THRESHOLD:
                if p in NATURAL_BENEFICS and p not in benefic_protectors:
                    benefic_protectors.append(p)
                elif p in NATURAL_MALEFICS and p not in malefic_afflictors:
                    malefic_afflictors.append(p)

        # 1. Śubha Kartarī: BOTH H2 and H12 occupied exclusively by natural benefics (zero malefics)
        if h2_benefics and h12_benefics and not h2_malefics and not h12_malefics:
            flanking_benefics = h12_benefics + h2_benefics
            score = 90.0
            pos = [
                f"Natural benefics exclusively flank {target_name} in H{h12} ({', '.join(h12_benefics)}) and H{h2} ({', '.join(h2_benefics)})."
            ]
            breakers: List[YogaBreakerDetail] = []

            is_sushubha = bool(benefic_protectors and not malefic_afflictors)
            if is_sushubha:
                score = 95.0
                pos.append(
                    f"Suśubha Confluence: {target_name} is additionally fortified by benefic influence from {', '.join(benefic_protectors)}."
                )

            if malefic_afflictors:
                breakers.append(YogaBreakerDetail(
                    factor="Malefic Intrusion on Hemmed Focal Point",
                    culprit_planet=", ".join(malefic_afflictors),
                    description=(
                        f"Although flanked by benefics, {target_name} is occupied or palpably aspected by malefic(s) ({', '.join(malefic_afflictors)})."
                    ),
                    penalty=20.0
                ))
                score -= 20.0

            status = YogaStatus.PURE if (score >= 75.0 and not breakers) else YogaStatus.STAINED
            yoga_label = "Suśubha Kartarī Yoga" if is_sushubha else "Śubha Kartarī Yoga"

            detected.append(YogaInstance(
                id=f"shubha_kartari_{target_name.split()[0].lower()}",
                name=f"{yoga_label} ({target_name})",
                category=YogaCategory.KARTARI,
                status=status,
                plausibility_score=round(score, 1),
                participating_planets=flanking_benefics,
                participating_houses=[h12, target_house, h2],
                scripture_ref="Phaladeepika 6.7, 6.11",
                archetype=f"The Protective Shield: {target_name} is cushioned on both sides by natural benefics, granting ease and continuous support.",
                manifestation_effects=[
                    f"Surrounds the {domain} with psychological safety, resource support, and physical vitality.",
                    "Quick recovery from life setbacks and natural protection from hostility."
                ],
                positive_factors=pos,
                breakers=breakers
            ))

        # 2. Pāpa Kartarī: BOTH H2 and H12 occupied exclusively by natural malefics (zero benefics)
        elif h2_malefics and h12_malefics and not h2_benefics and not h12_benefics:
            flanking_malefics = h12_malefics + h2_malefics
            pos = []
            breakers = [YogaBreakerDetail(
                factor="Malefic Scissors (Pāpa Kartarī)",
                culprit_planet=", ".join(flanking_malefics),
                description=f"Natural malefics flank H{h12} ({', '.join(h12_malefics)}) and H{h2} ({', '.join(h2_malefics)}), compressing {target_name}.",
                penalty=35.0
            )]

            # Benefic occupant or palpable aspect on the hemmed house rescues the native (never PURE)
            is_rescued = bool(benefic_protectors)
            if is_rescued:
                pos.append(
                    f"Mitigated by benefic protection ({', '.join(benefic_protectors)}) occupying or palpably aspecting {target_name}."
                )
                status = YogaStatus.RESCUED
                score = 55.0
            else:
                status = YogaStatus.STAINED
                score = 75.0

            detected.append(YogaInstance(
                id=f"papa_kartari_{target_name.split()[0].lower()}",
                name=f"Pāpa Kartarī Yoga ({target_name})" + (" (Mitigated)" if is_rescued else ""),
                category=YogaCategory.KARTARI,
                status=status,
                plausibility_score=round(score, 1),
                participating_planets=flanking_malefics,
                participating_houses=[h12, target_house, h2],
                scripture_ref="Phaladeepika 6.7, 6.12-13",
                archetype=f"The Scissor Squeeze: {target_name} is hemmed on both sides by malefics, producing pressure and restriction.",
                manifestation_effects=[
                    f"Creates tension, obstacles, and a feeling of being boxed in regarding the {domain}.",
                    "Demands conscious emotional discipline, boundary-setting, and perseverance."
                ],
                positive_factors=pos,
                breakers=breakers
            ))

    return detected

FILE: lunar_solar_yogas.py
TYPE: Python Source | LINES: 518 | SIZE: 25.1 KB
