"""
jyotish/yogas/lunar_solar_yogas.py
Lunar (Chandra) and Solar (Ravi) Yogas (Hemisphere A: Pure Foundational Calculus).

Scripture: Phaladeepika Ch. 6 (Verses 5-13), BPHS Ch. 37-38.
Architecture: Strictly adheres to YOGA_ARCHITECTURE.md Categories 8 (LUNAR) & 9 (SOLAR).

Evaluates:
1. Lunar Flanking & Isolation Yogas:
   - Sunaphā (2nd from Moon), Anaphā (12th from Moon), Durudharā (both 2nd & 12th from Moon)
     * Strictly restricted to the 5 starry planets (Mars, Mercury, Jupiter, Venus, Saturn) — Sun, Rahu, Ketu excluded.
     * Audits combustion, dispositor vitality, and Shadbala on flanking planets.
   - Kemadruma Yoga & 4-Tier Bhaṅga Hierarchy:
     * Tier 1: Physical planets in Kendra (1, 4, 7, 10) from Moon.
     * Tier 2: Moon in Kendra (1, 4, 7, 10) from Lagna.
     * Tier 3: Sign Modality / Quadruplicity (Chatuṣṭaya) alignment between Lagna and Moon.
     * Tier 4: Physical planets in Kendra from Moon in D9 Navāṃśa.
     * Affliction status invariant: STAINED when active, RESCUED when cancelled — NEVER PURE.
   - Candra-Maṅgala Yoga (Moon + Mars conjunction or aspect).
2. Solar Flanking & Illumination Yogas:
   - Budhāditya Yoga (Sun + Mercury; uses audit_combustion(..., deep_only=True) so only < 3.0° penalizes).
   - Veśi (2nd from Sun), Vāsi/Vośi (12th from Sun), Ubhayacarī (both 2nd & 12th from Sun)
     * Strictly restricted to the 5 starry planets (Mars, Mercury, Jupiter, Venus, Saturn) — Moon, Rahu, Ketu excluded.
     * Audits deep combustion (< 3.0°), dispositor vitality, and Shadbala.
"""

from typing import List, Dict, Any, Tuple
from jyotish.yogas.models import (
    YogaInstance, YogaCategory, YogaStatus, YogaBreakerDetail
)
from jyotish.yogas.breakers import (
    get_lagna_sign, get_house_of_planet, get_sign_of_planet, get_planets_data,
    get_aspect_score, resolve_yoga_score_and_status,
    NATURAL_BENEFICS, NATURAL_MALEFICS, ZODIAC_SIGNS,
    audit_trishadaya_interference, audit_combustion, audit_dispositor_vitality,
    audit_dusthana_placement, audit_shadbala_muscle,
    ASPECT_PALPABLE_THRESHOLD, ASPECT_MARGINAL_THRESHOLD
)

# Strictly the 5 non-luminary physical planets (Sun, Moon, Rahu, Ketu excluded)
FIVE_STARRY_GRAHAS: Tuple[str, ...] = ("Mars", "Mercury", "Jupiter", "Venus", "Saturn")


def _audit_lunar_flankers(
    flankers: List[str],
    chart: Dict[str, Any],
    base_score: float,
) -> Tuple[float, YogaStatus, List[str], List[YogaBreakerDetail]]:
    """
    Applies Category 8 (LUNAR) Layer 2 breakers to 2nd/12th lunar flanking planets:
    - Benefic vs. Malefic flavor modulation
    - audit_combustion(flankers, chart)
    - audit_dispositor_vitality(flankers, chart)
    - audit_shadbala_muscle(flankers, chart)
    """
    benefics = [p for p in flankers if p in NATURAL_BENEFICS]
    malefics = [p for p in flankers if p in NATURAL_MALEFICS]

    score = base_score
    pos: List[str] = []
    breakers: List[YogaBreakerDetail] = []

    if benefics:
        pos.append(f"Natural benefics flanking ({', '.join(benefics)}) bestow refinement, generosity, and smooth prosperity.")
        score += 8.0
    if malefics:
        breakers.append(YogaBreakerDetail(
            factor="Malefics Flanking Moon",
            culprit_planet=", ".join(malefics),
            description=f"Natural malefics ({', '.join(malefics)}) in 2nd/12th from Moon add hard-edged grit and emotional friction.",
            penalty=15.0 if benefics else 20.0
        ))

    breakers.extend(audit_combustion(flankers, chart))
    breakers.extend(audit_dispositor_vitality(flankers, chart))
    sb_breakers, sb_pos = audit_shadbala_muscle(flankers, chart)
    breakers.extend(sb_breakers)
    pos.extend(sb_pos)

    final_score, status = resolve_yoga_score_and_status(score, breakers)
    return final_score, status, pos, breakers


def _audit_solar_flankers(
    flankers: List[str],
    chart: Dict[str, Any],
    base_score: float,
) -> Tuple[str, float, YogaStatus, List[str], List[YogaBreakerDetail]]:
    """
    Applies Category 9 (SOLAR) Layer 2 breakers to 2nd/12th solar flanking planets:
    - Distinguishes Śubha (benefic), Pāpa (malefic -> STAINED), and Miśra (mixed) flavors (Phaladeepika 6.8-13).
    - audit_combustion(flankers, chart, deep_only=True) (< 3.0° only).
    - audit_dispositor_vitality(flankers, chart).
    - audit_shadbala_muscle(flankers, chart).
    """
    benefics = [p for p in flankers if p in NATURAL_BENEFICS]
    malefics = [p for p in flankers if p in NATURAL_MALEFICS]

    score = base_score
    pos: List[str] = []
    breakers: List[YogaBreakerDetail] = []

    if benefics and not malefics:
        prefix = "Śubha-"
        pos.append(f"Pure natural benefics ({', '.join(benefics)}) flanking the Sun bestow eloquence, nobility, and royal favor.")
        score += 5.0
    elif malefics and not benefics:
        prefix = "Pāpa-"
        breakers.append(YogaBreakerDetail(
            factor="Pāpa Solar Flanking (Malefic Neighbors)",
            culprit_planet=", ".join(malefics),
            description=f"Malefics ({', '.join(malefics)}) flanking the Sun create harshness, dampened splendor, or competitive friction (Phaladeepika 6.12).",
            penalty=20.0
        ))
    else:
        prefix = "Miśra-"
        pos.append(f"Mixed benefics ({', '.join(benefics)}) and malefics ({', '.join(malefics)}) flanking the Sun grant versatile executive grit.")
        breakers.append(YogaBreakerDetail(
            factor="Mixed Malefic Solar Flanking",
            culprit_planet=", ".join(malefics),
            description=f"Co-flanking malefics ({', '.join(malefics)}) introduce competitive strain alongside benefic support.",
            penalty=10.0
        ))

    # Category 9 Rule: deep_only=True (< 3.0° only) so normal solar proximity (3.0°–8.0°) is NOT penalized
    breakers.extend(audit_combustion(flankers, chart, deep_only=True))
    breakers.extend(audit_dispositor_vitality(flankers, chart))
    sb_breakers, sb_pos = audit_shadbala_muscle(flankers, chart)
    breakers.extend(sb_breakers)
    pos.extend(sb_pos)

    final_score, status = resolve_yoga_score_and_status(score, breakers)
    if prefix == "Pāpa-" and status == YogaStatus.PURE:
        status = YogaStatus.STAINED

    return prefix, final_score, status, pos, breakers


def detect_lunar_and_solar_yogas(chart: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[YogaInstance]:
    """Scans chart for classical Lunar (Category 8) and Solar (Category 9) yogas."""
    detected: List[YogaInstance] = []
    planets_data = get_planets_data(chart)
    planet_houses = {p: get_house_of_planet(chart, p) for p in planets_data}

    moon_house = planet_houses.get("Moon", 0)
    sun_house = planet_houses.get("Sun", 0)

    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. LUNAR YOGAS (CHANDRA YOGAS — Category 8)
    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    if moon_house > 0:
        h2_moon = (moon_house % 12) + 1
        h12_moon = ((moon_house - 2) % 12) + 1

        # Strictly whitelist the 5 starry planets (Sun, Rahu, Ketu, Moon excluded)
        planets_in_h2 = [
            p for p in FIVE_STARRY_GRAHAS
            if p in planets_data and planet_houses.get(p) == h2_moon
        ]
        planets_in_h12 = [
            p for p in FIVE_STARRY_GRAHAS
            if p in planets_data and planet_houses.get(p) == h12_moon
        ]

        # A1. Durudharā Yoga (Both 2nd and 12th from Moon occupied)
        if planets_in_h2 and planets_in_h12:
            flankers = planets_in_h2 + planets_in_h12
            final_score, status, pos, breakers = _audit_lunar_flankers(flankers, chart, 85.0)
            pos.insert(0, f"Planets flank Moon in 2nd ({', '.join(planets_in_h2)}) and 12th ({', '.join(planets_in_h12)}).")

            detected.append(YogaInstance(
                id="durudhara_yoga",
                name="Durudharā Yoga",
                category=YogaCategory.LUNAR,
                status=status,
                plausibility_score=final_score,
                participating_planets=["Moon"] + flankers,
                participating_houses=sorted(list({moon_house, h2_moon, h12_moon})),
                scripture_ref="Phaladeepika 6.5-7, BPHS 37.1-2",
                archetype="The Emotionally Anchored Mind: Flanked by resources (2nd) and philosophical perspective (12th).",
                manifestation_effects=[
                    "Endows material comfort, reliable helpers (Sadbhṛtya), generous disposition, and verbal eloquence.",
                    "Enjoyment of vehicles, landed property, and consistent resource acquisition."
                ],
                positive_factors=pos,
                breakers=breakers
            ))

        # A2. Sunaphā Yoga (Only 2nd from Moon occupied)
        elif planets_in_h2:
            final_score, status, pos, breakers = _audit_lunar_flankers(planets_in_h2, chart, 80.0)
            pos.insert(0, f"Planets in 2nd from Moon: {', '.join(planets_in_h2)}.")

            detected.append(YogaInstance(
                id="sunapha_yoga",
                name="Sunaphā Yoga",
                category=YogaCategory.LUNAR,
                status=status,
                plausibility_score=final_score,
                participating_planets=["Moon"] + planets_in_h2,
                participating_houses=sorted(list({moon_house, h2_moon})),
                scripture_ref="Phaladeepika 6.5, BPHS 37.1",
                archetype="Resourceful Mind: Planets in the 2nd from the Moon provide self-made wealth and steady earnings.",
                manifestation_effects=[
                    "Self-made prosperity (Svabhujārjita-vitta), intelligent speech, and comfortable livelihood."
                ],
                positive_factors=pos,
                breakers=breakers
            ))

        # A3. Anaphā Yoga (Only 12th from Moon occupied)
        elif planets_in_h12:
            final_score, status, pos, breakers = _audit_lunar_flankers(planets_in_h12, chart, 80.0)
            pos.insert(0, f"Planets in 12th from Moon: {', '.join(planets_in_h12)}.")

            detected.append(YogaInstance(
                id="anapha_yoga",
                name="Anaphā Yoga",
                category=YogaCategory.LUNAR,
                status=status,
                plausibility_score=final_score,
                participating_planets=["Moon"] + planets_in_h12,
                participating_houses=sorted(list({moon_house, h12_moon})),
                scripture_ref="Phaladeepika 6.6, BPHS 37.1",
                archetype="Refined & Carefree Mind: Planets in the 12th from the Moon bestow dignity, health, and inner poise.",
                manifestation_effects=[
                    "Refined conduct (Śīlavān), physical vitality, sensory enjoyment, and moral contentment."
                ],
                positive_factors=pos,
                breakers=breakers
            ))

        # A4. Kemadruma Yoga & 4-Tier Bhaṅga Hierarchy (Neither 2nd nor 12th occupied)
        else:
            lagna_sign_cur = get_lagna_sign(chart)
            moon_sign_cur = get_sign_of_planet(chart, "Moon")

            CARDINAL_SIGNS = {"Aries", "Cancer", "Libra", "Capricorn"}
            FIXED_SIGNS = {"Taurus", "Leo", "Scorpio", "Aquarius"}
            DUAL_SIGNS = {"Gemini", "Virgo", "Sagittarius", "Pisces"}

            # Tier 1: Physical planets (Mars, Mercury, Jupiter, Venus, Saturn) in Kendra from Moon
            kendra_planets_moon = [
                p for p in FIVE_STARRY_GRAHAS
                if p in planets_data
                and planet_houses.get(p, 0) > 0
                and ((planet_houses[p] - moon_house) % 12 + 1) in (1, 4, 7, 10)
            ]

            # Tier 2: Moon itself occupying Kendra (1, 4, 7, 10) from Lagna
            moon_in_lagna_kendra = moon_house in (1, 4, 7, 10)

            # Tier 3: Sign Modality / Quadruplicity (Chatuṣṭaya) alignment between Lagna and Moon
            same_modality = bool(lagna_sign_cur and moon_sign_cur) and (
                (lagna_sign_cur in CARDINAL_SIGNS and moon_sign_cur in CARDINAL_SIGNS) or
                (lagna_sign_cur in FIXED_SIGNS and moon_sign_cur in FIXED_SIGNS) or
                (lagna_sign_cur in DUAL_SIGNS and moon_sign_cur in DUAL_SIGNS)
            )

            # Tier 4: Classical physical planets in Kendra from Moon in Navāṃśa (D9)
            d9_block = (chart.get("vargas") or {}).get("D9") or chart.get("D9") or chart.get("d9") or {}
            d9_grahas = d9_block.get("grahas") or d9_block.get("planets") or {}
            moon_d9_sign = (d9_grahas.get("Moon") or {}).get("sign", "")
            d9_kendra_planets: List[str] = []
            if moon_d9_sign in ZODIAC_SIGNS:
                m_d9_idx = ZODIAC_SIGNS.index(moon_d9_sign)
                for p in FIVE_STARRY_GRAHAS:
                    p_d9_sign = (d9_grahas.get(p) or {}).get("sign", "")
                    if p_d9_sign in ZODIAC_SIGNS:
                        diff_d9 = ((ZODIAC_SIGNS.index(p_d9_sign) - m_d9_idx) % 12) + 1
                        if diff_d9 in (1, 4, 7, 10):
                            d9_kendra_planets.append(p)

            cancellation_reasons: List[str] = []
            if kendra_planets_moon:
                cancellation_reasons.append(
                    f"Tier 1 Lunar Kendra: Rescued by physical planets in Kendra from Chandra ({', '.join(kendra_planets_moon)})."
                )
            if moon_in_lagna_kendra:
                cancellation_reasons.append(
                    f"Tier 2 Lagna Kendra: Rescued by Moon itself occupying Kendra House {moon_house} from Lagna."
                )
            if same_modality:
                cancellation_reasons.append(
                    f"Tier 3 Modality Alignment: Rescued by Ascendant ({lagna_sign_cur}) and Moon ({moon_sign_cur}) sharing the same quadruplicity (Chatuṣṭaya)."
                )
            if d9_kendra_planets:
                cancellation_reasons.append(
                    f"Tier 4 Navāṃśa Kendra: Rescued by planets in Kendra from Moon in D9 ({', '.join(d9_kendra_planets)})."
                )

            is_cancelled = bool(cancellation_reasons)
            # Affliction Yoga Invariant: STAINED when active/unmitigated; RESCUED when cancelled — NEVER PURE!
            status = YogaStatus.RESCUED if is_cancelled else YogaStatus.STAINED
            score = max(40.0, 75.0 - 10.0 * len(cancellation_reasons)) if is_cancelled else 80.0

            participating_planets: List[str] = ["Moon"]
            for p in kendra_planets_moon + d9_kendra_planets:
                if p not in participating_planets:
                    participating_planets.append(p)

            participating_houses = sorted(list(
                {moon_house} | {planet_houses[p] for p in kendra_planets_moon if planet_houses.get(p, 0) > 0}
            ))

            breakers = [YogaBreakerDetail(
                factor="Moon Isolation (No 2nd/12th Flanking Planets)",
                culprit_planet="Moon",
                description="No physical planets reside in 2nd or 12th from the Moon, creating subjective feelings of emotional solitude.",
                penalty=25.0 if is_cancelled else 50.0
            )]

            detected.append(YogaInstance(
                id="kemadruma_yoga",
                name="Kemadruma Yoga" + (" (Rescued / Bhaṅga)" if is_cancelled else ""),
                category=YogaCategory.LUNAR,
                status=status,
                plausibility_score=round(score, 1),
                participating_planets=participating_planets,
                participating_houses=participating_houses,
                scripture_ref="Phaladeepika 6.7, BPHS 37.11-16",
                archetype="The Solitary Mind: Moon sits without immediate 2nd/12th flanking planets.",
                manifestation_effects=[
                    "Feelings of psychological isolation, melancholy, or having to fend for oneself without external cushioning."
                    if not is_cancelled else
                    "Initial emotional solitude is rescued and transformed into deep self-reliance through angular Kendra support."
                ],
                positive_factors=cancellation_reasons,
                breakers=breakers
            ))

        # B. Candra-Maṅgala Yoga (Moon + Mars)
        mars_house = planet_houses.get("Mars", 0)
        if mars_house > 0:
            is_cm_conj = (mars_house == moon_house)
            aspect_mm = max(
                get_aspect_score(chart, "Mars", "Moon"),
                get_aspect_score(chart, "Moon", "Mars")
            )
            is_cm_aspect = aspect_mm >= ASPECT_PALPABLE_THRESHOLD
            is_cm_marginal = (not is_cm_conj) and (not is_cm_aspect) and (aspect_mm >= ASPECT_MARGINAL_THRESHOLD)

            if is_cm_conj or is_cm_aspect or is_cm_marginal:
                score = 80.0
                pos = [
                    f"Moon and Mars combine (House {moon_house})"
                    if is_cm_conj else
                    f"Moon and Mars link via {aspect_mm:.1f} Virūpa aspect."
                ]
                breakers = []
                if is_cm_marginal:
                    breakers.append(YogaBreakerDetail(
                        factor="Marginal Aspect Connection",
                        culprit_planet="Mars",
                        description=f"Aspect strength ({aspect_mm:.1f} Virūpas) is below the palpable 45.0 Virūpa threshold.",
                        penalty=25.0
                    ))
                breakers.extend(audit_trishadaya_interference(["Moon", "Mars"], chart))
                breakers.extend(audit_combustion(["Mars"], chart))
                breakers.extend(audit_dispositor_vitality(["Moon", "Mars"], chart))
                sb_breakers, sb_pos = audit_shadbala_muscle(["Moon", "Mars"], chart)
                breakers.extend(sb_breakers)
                pos.extend(sb_pos)

                final_score, status = resolve_yoga_score_and_status(score, breakers)

                detected.append(YogaInstance(
                    id="chandra_mangala_yoga",
                    name="Candra-Maṅgala Yoga",
                    category=YogaCategory.LUNAR,
                    status=status,
                    plausibility_score=final_score,
                    participating_planets=["Moon", "Mars"],
                    participating_houses=sorted(list({moon_house, mars_house})),
                    scripture_ref="BPHS 37.8, Phaladeepika 6.20",
                    archetype="Energetic Enterprise & Vigor: Unites the feeling mind with Martian drive, fostering commercial hustle.",
                    manifestation_effects=[
                        "Strong commercial acumen, financial enterprise, and capacity to generate liquid assets.",
                        "Dynamic emotional drive, though can produce occasional impatience or emotional heat."
                    ],
                    positive_factors=pos,
                    breakers=breakers
                ))

    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. SOLAR YOGAS (RAVI YOGAS — Category 9)
    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    if sun_house > 0:
        # A. Budhāditya Yoga (Sun + Mercury)
        merc_house = get_house_of_planet(chart, "Mercury")
        if merc_house == sun_house and "Mercury" in planets_data:
            score = 85.0
            pos = [f"Sun and Mercury conjoined in House {sun_house}."]
            breakers = []

            sun_lon = (planets_data.get("Sun") or {}).get("longitude")
            merc_lon = (planets_data.get("Mercury") or {}).get("longitude")
            if sun_lon is not None and merc_lon is not None:
                try:
                    dist = abs(float(merc_lon) - float(sun_lon)) % 360.0
                    if dist > 180.0:
                        dist = 360.0 - dist
                    pos[0] = f"Sun and Mercury conjoined in House {sun_house} (Separation: {dist:.1f}°)."
                    if 3.0 <= dist <= 14.0:
                        pos.append("Optimal illumination orb (3.0°–14.0°): Mercury absorbs solar brilliance without deep incineration.")
                        score += 5.0
                except (TypeError, ValueError):
                    pass

            # Category 9 Rule: audit_combustion(..., deep_only=True) (< 3.0° only)
            breakers.extend(audit_combustion(["Mercury"], chart, deep_only=True))
            dust_brk = audit_dusthana_placement([sun_house], "Budhaditya")
            if dust_brk:
                breakers.append(dust_brk)
            breakers.extend(audit_dispositor_vitality(["Sun", "Mercury"], chart))
            sb_breakers, sb_pos = audit_shadbala_muscle(["Sun", "Mercury"], chart)
            breakers.extend(sb_breakers)
            pos.extend(sb_pos)

            final_score, status = resolve_yoga_score_and_status(score, breakers)

            detected.append(YogaInstance(
                id="budhaditya_yoga",
                name="Budhāditya Yoga",
                category=YogaCategory.SOLAR,
                status=status,
                plausibility_score=final_score,
                participating_planets=["Sun", "Mercury"],
                participating_houses=[sun_house],
                scripture_ref="BPHS 38.1, Phaladeepika 6.8",
                archetype="The Solar Intellect: The Sun's soul-force illuminates Mercury's analytical brilliance.",
                manifestation_effects=[
                    "High intelligence, administrative tact, verbal eloquence, and scholarly distinction.",
                    "Respected in scientific, governmental, or educational circles."
                ],
                positive_factors=pos,
                breakers=breakers
            ))

        # B. Veśi, Vāsi (Vośi), and Ubhayacarī Yogas
        h2_sun = (sun_house % 12) + 1
        h12_sun = ((sun_house - 2) % 12) + 1

        # Strictly whitelist the 5 starry planets (Moon, Rahu, Ketu, Sun excluded)
        planets_in_h2_s = [
            p for p in FIVE_STARRY_GRAHAS
            if p in planets_data and planet_houses.get(p) == h2_sun
        ]
        planets_in_h12_s = [
            p for p in FIVE_STARRY_GRAHAS
            if p in planets_data and planet_houses.get(p) == h12_sun
        ]

        if planets_in_h2_s and planets_in_h12_s:
            flankers_s = planets_in_h2_s + planets_in_h12_s
            prefix, final_score, status, pos, breakers = _audit_solar_flankers(flankers_s, chart, 85.0)
            pos.insert(0, f"Flanked in 2nd ({', '.join(planets_in_h2_s)}) and 12th ({', '.join(planets_in_h12_s)}) from Sun.")

            detected.append(YogaInstance(
                id="ubhayacari_yoga",
                name=f"{prefix}Ubhayacarī Yoga",
                category=YogaCategory.SOLAR,
                status=status,
                plausibility_score=final_score,
                participating_planets=["Sun"] + flankers_s,
                participating_houses=sorted(list({h12_sun, sun_house, h2_sun})),
                scripture_ref="Phaladeepika 6.10, BPHS 38.4",
                archetype="Balanced Solar Aura: Planets flank both sides of the Sun, conferring executive poise and royal presence.",
                manifestation_effects=[
                    "Balanced personality, broad organizational capability, articulateness, and public stature."
                ],
                positive_factors=pos,
                breakers=breakers
            ))

        elif planets_in_h2_s:
            prefix, final_score, status, pos, breakers = _audit_solar_flankers(planets_in_h2_s, chart, 80.0)
            pos.insert(0, f"Planets in 2nd from Sun: {', '.join(planets_in_h2_s)}.")

            detected.append(YogaInstance(
                id="vesi_yoga",
                name=f"{prefix}Veśi Yoga",
                category=YogaCategory.SOLAR,
                status=status,
                plausibility_score=final_score,
                participating_planets=["Sun"] + planets_in_h2_s,
                participating_houses=sorted(list({sun_house, h2_sun})),
                scripture_ref="Phaladeepika 6.8, BPHS 38.2",
                archetype="Articulate Solar Expression: Planets in 2nd from Sun channel solar authority into speech and tangible resources.",
                manifestation_effects=[
                    "Steady memory, articulate communication, and tangible financial execution."
                ],
                positive_factors=pos,
                breakers=breakers
            ))

        elif planets_in_h12_s:
            prefix, final_score, status, pos, breakers = _audit_solar_flankers(planets_in_h12_s, chart, 80.0)
            pos.insert(0, f"Planets in 12th from Sun: {', '.join(planets_in_h12_s)}.")

            detected.append(YogaInstance(
                id="vosi_yoga",
                name=f"{prefix}Vāsi (Vośi) Yoga",
                category=YogaCategory.SOLAR,
                status=status,
                plausibility_score=final_score,
                participating_planets=["Sun"] + planets_in_h12_s,
                participating_houses=sorted(list({h12_sun, sun_house})),
                scripture_ref="Phaladeepika 6.9, BPHS 38.3",
                archetype="Charitable Solar Vision: Planets in 12th from Sun bestow philosophical foresight and strategic wisdom.",
                manifestation_effects=[
                    "Charitable nature, contemplative learning, and broad reputation."
                ],
                positive_factors=pos,
                breakers=breakers
            ))

    return detected

"""
jyotish/yogas/models.py
Data models and enumerations for Classical Yoga Detection and Yoga Breaker analysis.
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass, field, asdict
from enum import Enum
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional

class YogaCategory(str, Enum):
    MAHAPURUSHA = "Pancha Mahapurusha"
    RAJA = "Raja Yoga"
    DHANA = "Dhana (Wealth)"
    DARIDRYA = "Daridrya (Poverty)"
    LUNAR = "Chandra (Lunar)"
    SOLAR = "Ravi (Solar)"
    PARIVARTANA = "Parivartana (Exchange)"
    VIPARITA = "Viparita Raja (Reversal)"
    KARTARI = "Kartari (Hemming)"
    CHANDAL = "Chāṇḍāla & Doṣa (Afflictions)"
    CONTEXTUAL = "Contextual & Macro Yogas"
    CHARACTER = "Character & Growth"
    DISPOSITOR_ROOT = "Dispositor Root Yogas"
    DEITY = "Deity (Trimūrti & Tridevī)"
    BHAVA = "12 Bhava Yogas"
    POWER = "Chapter 7 Power Yogas"
    NEECHABHANGA = "Nīcabhaṅga (Debility Cancellation)"

class YogaStatus(str, Enum):
    PURE = "Pure & Eminent"
    STAINED = "Stained / Challenged"
    RESCUED = "Rescued (Neecha Bhanga / Reversal)"
    BROKEN = "Broken (Yoga Bhanga)"

@dataclass
class YogaBreakerDetail:
    factor: str             # e.g. "11th Lord Intrusion", "Combustion", "Debilitated Dispositor"
    culprit_planet: str     # Planet responsible, or "House" / "System"
    description: str        # Plain-English intuitive explanation
    penalty: float          # Subtracted percentage (e.g. 40.0)

    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return asdict(self)

@dataclass
class YogaInstance:
    id: str                         # Machine ID, e.g. "ruchaka_mars"
    name: str                       # Display name, e.g. "Ruchaka Yoga"
    category: YogaCategory          # Broad category
    status: YogaStatus              # Pure, Stained, Rescued, Broken
    plausibility_score: float       # 0.0 to 100.0%
    participating_planets: List[str]# Grahas involved
    participating_houses: List[int] # Bhavas involved (1-12)
    scripture_ref: str              # e.g. "BPHS 75.1-4, Phaladeepika 6.1"
    archetype: str                  # Intuitive 1-2 sentence core meaning
    manifestation_effects: List[str]# Key psychological and worldly outcomes
    positive_factors: List[str]     # Supportive features (dignity, Kendra, benefic aspects)
    breakers: List[YogaBreakerDetail] = field(default_factory=list) # Disruptive factors

    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        serialized_breakers = []
        for b in (self.breakers or []):
            if b is None:
                continue
            if hasattr(b, "to_dict") and callable(b.to_dict):
                serialized_breakers.append(b.to_dict())
            elif isinstance(b, dict):
                serialized_breakers.append(dict(b))

        return {
            "id": self.id,
            "name": self.name,
            "category": self.category.value if isinstance(self.category, Enum) else str(self.category or ""),
            "status": self.status.value if isinstance(self.status, Enum) else str(self.status or ""),
            "plausibility_score": max(0.0, min(100.0, float(self.plausibility_score or 0.0))),
            "participating_planets": list(self.participating_planets or []),
            "participating_houses": list(self.participating_houses or []),
            "scripture_ref": self.scripture_ref or "",
            "archetype": self.archetype or "",
            "manifestation_effects": list(self.manifestation_effects or []),
            "positive_factors": list(self.positive_factors or []),
            "breakers": serialized_breakers,
        }

"""
jyotish/yogas/neechabhanga.py
Universal Debilitation Reversal Engine (Nīcabhaṅga Rāja Yoga & Simple Cancellation).
Evaluates debilitated planets across all classical conditions from Phaladeepika Ch. 7 (Verses 24–30)
and Brihat Parashara Hora Shastra (BPHS Ch. 39).
"""

from typing import List, Dict, Any, Tuple
from jyotish.yogas.models import (
    YogaInstance, YogaCategory, YogaStatus, YogaBreakerDetail
)
from jyotish.yogas.breakers import (
    get_house_of_planet, get_sign_of_planet, get_planets_data,
    get_lagna_sign, get_aspect_score,
    audit_combustion, audit_shadbala_muscle,
    DEBILITATION_SIGNS, EXALTATION_SIGNS, SIGN_LORDS, PLANET_EXALTED_IN_SIGN,
    ASPECT_PALPABLE_THRESHOLD, ASPECT_MARGINAL_THRESHOLD
)

CLASSICAL_PLANETS = ("Sun", "Moon", "Mars", "Mercury", "Jupiter", "Venus", "Saturn")


def audit_neecha_bhanga_details(
    planet: str,
    chart: Dict[str, Any]
) -> Tuple[bool, List[str], float, Dict[str, Any]]:
    """
    Audits the Classical Cancellation of Debility (Nīcabhaṅga) conditions
    according to Phaladeepika 7.24-30:
    1. Dispositor of debilitation sign in Kendra (1, 4, 7, 10) from Lagna or Moon (+30%).
    2. Exaltation lord (tad-uccāṃśa-nātha) or planet exalted in debilitation sign in Kendra (+30%).
    3. Cumulative Dual Lord Prominence: Both #1 and #2 met by distinct planets (+20% bonus).
    4. Dispositor directly aspects the debilitated planet (>= 45 Virūpas: +30%, >= 30 Virūpas: +15%)
       or dispositor is itself exalted (+30%).
    5. Exalted co-occupant sharing the sign (+35%).
    6. Fallen planet in Kendra from Lagna or Moon alongside distinct dispositor/exaltation lord (+30%),
       or angular on its own when not already counted via self-exaltation lordship (+20% Lagna / +15% Moon).

    Returns:
        (is_rescued, reasons, cumulative_score, metadata)
    """
    planets_data = get_planets_data(chart)
    p_info = planets_data.get(planet) or {}
    p_sign = p_info.get("sign", "") if isinstance(p_info, dict) else ""

    if not p_sign or p_sign != DEBILITATION_SIGNS.get(planet):
        return False, [], 0.0, {}

    debilitation_sign = p_sign
    dispositor = SIGN_LORDS.get(debilitation_sign)
    exaltation_sign = EXALTATION_SIGNS.get(planet)
    exaltation_lord = SIGN_LORDS.get(exaltation_sign)
    exalted_in_deb_sign = PLANET_EXALTED_IN_SIGN.get(debilitation_sign)

    moon_house = get_house_of_planet(chart, "Moon")
    p_house = get_house_of_planet(chart, planet)
    disp_house = get_house_of_planet(chart, dispositor) if dispositor else 0
    exalt_lord_house = get_house_of_planet(chart, exaltation_lord) if exaltation_lord else 0
    exalted_guest_house = get_house_of_planet(chart, exalted_in_deb_sign) if exalted_in_deb_sign else 0

    reasons: List[str] = []
    bonus = 0.0
    conditions_met: Dict[str, bool] = {}
    rescuing_planets: List[str] = []

    def _add_rescuer(p_name: str) -> None:
        if p_name and p_name != planet and p_name not in rescuing_planets:
            rescuing_planets.append(p_name)

    # Condition 1: Dispositor of debilitation sign in Kendra from Lagna (1,4,7,10) or Moon (+30%)
    # Guard: Moon is trivially H1 from itself; never count dispositor == "Moon" as Kendra from Moon.
    c1_met = False
    if dispositor and disp_house > 0:
        disp_from_moon = (
            ((disp_house - moon_house) % 12 + 1)
            if (dispositor != "Moon" and moon_house > 0)
            else 0
        )
        if disp_house in (1, 4, 7, 10):
            c1_met = True
            _add_rescuer(dispositor)
            reasons.append(f"Condition 1 (Dispositor in Lagna Kendra): {dispositor} (lord of {debilitation_sign}) occupies Kendra H{disp_house} (+30%).")
            bonus += 30.0
        elif disp_from_moon in (1, 4, 7, 10):
            c1_met = True
            _add_rescuer(dispositor)
            reasons.append(f"Condition 1 (Dispositor in Chandra Kendra): {dispositor} (lord of {debilitation_sign}) is in Kendra H{disp_from_moon} from Moon (+30%).")
            bonus += 30.0
    conditions_met["c1"] = c1_met

    # Condition 2: Exaltation lord in Kendra from Lagna or Moon (+30%)
    # 2A: Lord of the planet's exaltation sign (tad-uccāṃśa-nātha)
    # 2B: Planet that exalts in the debilitation sign (if distinct from dispositor and planet)
    c2_met = False
    c2_via_self = False
    c2_distinct = False
    if exaltation_lord and exalt_lord_house > 0:
        el_from_moon = (
            ((exalt_lord_house - moon_house) % 12 + 1)
            if (exaltation_lord != "Moon" and moon_house > 0)
            else 0
        )
        if exalt_lord_house in (1, 4, 7, 10):
            c2_met = True
            c2_via_self = (exaltation_lord == planet)
            c2_distinct = (exaltation_lord != planet and exaltation_lord != dispositor)
            _add_rescuer(exaltation_lord)
            reasons.append(f"Condition 2 (Exaltation Lord in Kendra): {exaltation_lord} (rules exaltation sign {exaltation_sign}) occupies Kendra H{exalt_lord_house} from Lagna (+30%).")
            bonus += 30.0
        elif el_from_moon in (1, 4, 7, 10):
            c2_met = True
            c2_via_self = (exaltation_lord == planet)
            c2_distinct = (exaltation_lord != planet and exaltation_lord != dispositor)
            _add_rescuer(exaltation_lord)
            reasons.append(f"Condition 2 (Exaltation Lord in Kendra): {exaltation_lord} occupies Kendra H{el_from_moon} from Moon (+30%).")
            bonus += 30.0

    # Evaluate 2B if 2A was not met by a distinct planet, and exalted_in_deb_sign is not already co-occupying debilitation_sign (Cond 5)
    if (not c2_distinct) and exalted_in_deb_sign and exalted_in_deb_sign != dispositor and exalted_in_deb_sign != planet and exalted_guest_house > 0 and exalted_guest_house != p_house:
        eg_from_moon = (
            ((exalted_guest_house - moon_house) % 12 + 1)
            if (exalted_in_deb_sign != "Moon" and moon_house > 0)
            else 0
        )
        if exalted_guest_house in (1, 4, 7, 10):
            c2_distinct = True
            _add_rescuer(exalted_in_deb_sign)
            reasons.append(f"Condition 2 (Planet Exalted in Sign in Kendra): {exalted_in_deb_sign} occupies Kendra H{exalted_guest_house} from Lagna (+30%).")
            if not c2_met:
                bonus += 30.0
            c2_met = True
        elif eg_from_moon in (1, 4, 7, 10):
            c2_distinct = True
            _add_rescuer(exalted_in_deb_sign)
            reasons.append(f"Condition 2 (Planet Exalted in Sign in Kendra): {exalted_in_deb_sign} occupies Kendra H{eg_from_moon} from Moon (+30%).")
            if not c2_met:
                bonus += 30.0
            c2_met = True
    conditions_met["c2"] = c2_met

    # Condition 3: Cumulative Dual Lord Prominence (both #1 and #2 met by distinct planets -> extra +20%)
    c3_met = c1_met and c2_distinct
    if c3_met:
        reasons.append("Condition 3 (Dual Lord Prominence): Both dispositor and exaltation lord occupy Kendras (+20% cumulative prestige bonus).")
        bonus += 20.0
    conditions_met["c3"] = c3_met

    # Condition 4: Dispositor directly aspects the debilitated planet or is itself exalted
    c4_met = False
    if dispositor:
        asp_disp = get_aspect_score(chart, dispositor, planet)
        if asp_disp >= ASPECT_PALPABLE_THRESHOLD:
            c4_met = True
            _add_rescuer(dispositor)
            reasons.append(f"Condition 4 (Direct Aspect by Dispositor): {dispositor} aspects fallen {planet} with palpable {asp_disp:.0f} Virūpas (+30%).")
            bonus += 30.0
        elif asp_disp >= ASPECT_MARGINAL_THRESHOLD:
            c4_met = True
            _add_rescuer(dispositor)
            reasons.append(f"Condition 4 (Direct Aspect by Dispositor, Marginal): {dispositor} aspects fallen {planet} with {asp_disp:.0f} Virūpas (+15%).")
            bonus += 15.0

        disp_sign = get_sign_of_planet(chart, dispositor)
        if disp_sign and disp_sign == EXALTATION_SIGNS.get(dispositor):
            c4_met = True
            _add_rescuer(dispositor)
            reasons.append(f"Condition 4B (Exalted Dispositor): {dispositor} (ruler of {debilitation_sign}) is itself exalted in {disp_sign} (+30%).")
            bonus += 30.0
    conditions_met["c4"] = c4_met

    # Condition 5: Exalted co-occupant sharing the sign (+35%)
    c5_met = False
    for other_p, data in planets_data.items():
        if not isinstance(data, dict):
            continue
        if other_p != planet and data.get("sign") == debilitation_sign:
            if data.get("sign") == EXALTATION_SIGNS.get(other_p):
                c5_met = True
                _add_rescuer(other_p)
                reasons.append(f"Condition 5 (Exalted Co-occupant): {other_p} is exalted in {debilitation_sign} alongside fallen {planet} (+35%).")
                bonus += 35.0
                break
    conditions_met["c5"] = c5_met

    # Condition 6: Fallen planet in Kendra from Lagna or Moon
    c6_met = False
    p_from_moon = (
        ((p_house - moon_house) % 12 + 1)
        if (planet != "Moon" and p_house > 0 and moon_house > 0)
        else 0
    )
    p_kendra_lagna = p_house in (1, 4, 7, 10)
    p_kendra_moon = p_from_moon in (1, 4, 7, 10)
    p_is_kendra = p_kendra_lagna or p_kendra_moon

    # Check if conjoined with dispositor or exaltation lord not already counted in C1/C2/C5
    uncredited_co_lord = None
    if dispositor and dispositor != planet and disp_house > 0 and p_house == disp_house and not c1_met:
        uncredited_co_lord = dispositor
    elif exaltation_lord and exaltation_lord != planet and exalt_lord_house > 0 and p_house == exalt_lord_house and not c2_met:
        uncredited_co_lord = exaltation_lord

    kendra_label = f"Kendra H{p_house} from Lagna" if p_kendra_lagna else f"Kendra H{p_from_moon} from Chandra (H{p_house})"

    if p_is_kendra and uncredited_co_lord:
        c6_met = True
        _add_rescuer(uncredited_co_lord)
        reasons.append(f"Condition 6 (Fallen Planet Angular with Lord): Fallen {planet} is in {kendra_label} conjoined with {uncredited_co_lord} (+30%).")
        bonus += 30.0
    elif p_is_kendra and not c2_via_self:
        c6_met = True
        pts = 20.0 if p_kendra_lagna else 15.0
        reasons.append(f"Condition 6 (Angular Fallen Planet): Fallen {planet} occupies {kendra_label}, forcing active public engagement (+{pts:.0f}%).")
        bonus += pts
    conditions_met["c6"] = c6_met

    is_rescued = len(reasons) >= 1 and bonus >= 30.0

    metadata = {
        "conditions_met": conditions_met,
        "dispositor": dispositor,
        "exaltation_lord": exaltation_lord,
        "rescuing_planets": rescuing_planets,
        "p_house": p_house,
        "debilitation_sign": debilitation_sign,
    }

    return is_rescued, reasons, min(100.0, bonus), metadata


def audit_neecha_bhanga(planet: str, chart: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[bool, List[str], float]:
    """
    Standard interface for checking if a planet's debility is cancelled.
    Compatible drop-in replacement for existing breaker calls.
    """
    is_rescued, reasons, bonus, _ = audit_neecha_bhanga_details(planet, chart)
    return is_rescued, reasons, bonus


def detect_neechabhanga_yogas(chart: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[YogaInstance]:
    """
    Scans the chart for any debilitated planets and produces distinct YogaInstance records:
    - Kendra/Koṇa (1, 4, 5, 7, 9, 10): Nīcabhaṅga Rāja Yoga (Royal eminence forged through adversity).
    - Duṣṭhāna (6, 8, 12): Nīca Bhaṅga Cancellation (Deficit overcome without worldly command).
    - Other Houses (2, 3, 11): Nīca Bhaṅga Cancellation (Overcoming vulnerability with personal effort).
    """
    detected: List[YogaInstance] = []

    for planet in CLASSICAL_PLANETS:
        is_rescued, reasons, score, meta = audit_neecha_bhanga_details(planet, chart)
        if not is_rescued:
            continue

        p_house = meta.get("p_house", get_house_of_planet(chart, planet))
        deb_sign = meta.get("debilitation_sign", get_sign_of_planet(chart, planet))
        rescuing_planets = meta.get("rescuing_planets") or []
        dispositor = meta.get("dispositor")

        participating_planets = [planet]
        for rp in rescuing_planets:
            if rp not in participating_planets:
                participating_planets.append(rp)
        if len(participating_planets) == 1 and dispositor and dispositor != planet:
            participating_planets.append(dispositor)

        participating_houses = [p_house] if p_house > 0 else []
        for rp in participating_planets[1:]:
            rp_h = get_house_of_planet(chart, rp)
            if rp_h > 0 and rp_h not in participating_houses:
                participating_houses.append(rp_h)

        # Audit secondary breakers on the fallen planet (combustion, Shadbala deficit)
        breakers: List[YogaBreakerDetail] = []
        breakers.extend(audit_combustion([planet], chart))
        shad_breakers, shad_pos = audit_shadbala_muscle([planet], chart)
        breakers.extend(shad_breakers)

        positive_factors = list(reasons) + shad_pos
        net_score = score
        for b in breakers:
            net_score -= b.penalty
        net_score = max(5.0, min(100.0, net_score))

        # Output Classification
        if p_house in (1, 4, 5, 7, 9, 10):
            if net_score >= 75.0 and not breakers:
                status = YogaStatus.PURE
            elif net_score >= 30.0:
                status = YogaStatus.RESCUED
            else:
                status = YogaStatus.BROKEN

            detected.append(YogaInstance(
                id=f"neechabhanga_raja_yoga_{planet.lower()}",
                name=f"Nīcabhaṅga Rāja Yoga ({planet})",
                category=YogaCategory.NEECHABHANGA,
                status=status,
                plausibility_score=round(net_score, 1),
                participating_planets=participating_planets,
                participating_houses=participating_houses,
                scripture_ref="Phaladeepika 7.24–30, BPHS 39.1-15",
                archetype=f"Adversity Transmuted to Sovereignty: Fallen {planet} in {deb_sign} is fully redeemed in auspicious House {p_house}, turning profound vulnerability into royal resilience.",
                manifestation_effects=[
                    f"Converts early limitations or structural deficits of {planet} into unshakeable personal tenacity and worldly distinction.",
                    f"Bestows sovereign authority, respect, and executive eminence, culminating during {planet}'s Daśā periods."
                ],
                positive_factors=positive_factors,
                breakers=breakers
            ))
        elif p_house in (6, 8, 12):
            capped_score = min(65.0, net_score)
            status = YogaStatus.RESCUED if capped_score >= 30.0 else YogaStatus.BROKEN
            detected.append(YogaInstance(
                id=f"neecha_bhanga_simple_{planet.lower()}",
                name=f"Nīca Bhaṅga Cancellation ({planet})",
                category=YogaCategory.NEECHABHANGA,
                status=status,
                plausibility_score=round(capped_score, 1),
                participating_planets=participating_planets,
                participating_houses=participating_houses,
                scripture_ref="Phaladeepika 7.24–30",
                archetype=f"Deficit Overcome without Worldly Command: Debility of {planet} in House {p_house} is cancelled, neutralizing acute distress without granting wide institutional authority.",
                manifestation_effects=[
                    f"Overcomes health, litigation, or psychological burdens associated with fallen {planet} in House {p_house}.",
                    "Brings internal peace and recovery from hardship, but lacks the angular foundation to confer public sovereignty."
                ],
                positive_factors=positive_factors,
                breakers=breakers
            ))
        else:
            capped_score = min(70.0, net_score)
            status = YogaStatus.RESCUED if capped_score >= 30.0 else YogaStatus.BROKEN
            detected.append(YogaInstance(
                id=f"neecha_bhanga_simple_{planet.lower()}",
                name=f"Nīca Bhaṅga Cancellation ({planet})",
                category=YogaCategory.NEECHABHANGA,
                status=status,
                plausibility_score=round(capped_score, 1),
                participating_planets=participating_planets,
                participating_houses=participating_houses,
                scripture_ref="Phaladeepika 7.24–30",
                archetype=f"Deficit Overcome without Worldly Command: Debility of {planet} in House {p_house} is cancelled through consistent personal effort.",
                manifestation_effects=[
                    f"Steadily resolves deficits of fallen {planet} in House {p_house} into practical competence.",
                    "Provides financial or communicative recovery through persistent discipline."
                ],
                positive_factors=positive_factors,
                breakers=breakers
            ))

    return detected

"""
jyotish/yogas/pancha_mahapurusha.py
Pancha Mahapurusha Yogas (Five Great Person Combinations).
Scripture: Brihat Parashara Hora Shastra Ch. 75, Phaladeepika Ch. 6, Verses 1-4.
"""

from typing import List, Dict, Any
from jyotish.yogas.models import (
    YogaInstance, YogaCategory, YogaStatus, YogaBreakerDetail
)
from jyotish.yogas.breakers import (
    EXALTATION_SIGNS, OWN_SIGNS,
    get_house_of_planet, get_sign_of_planet, get_planets_data, get_house_rulers,
    audit_trishadaya_interference, audit_combustion,
    audit_dispositor_vitality, audit_shadbala_muscle, audit_dusthana_placement
)

MAHAPURUSHA_CONFIGS = {
    "Mars": {
        "id": "ruchaka_yoga",
        "name": "Rucaka Mahāpuruṣa Yoga",
        "scripture_ref": "BPHS 75.1-4, Phaladeepika 6.1",
        "archetype": "The Fearless Warrior & Commander: Glows with radiant fire, executive boldness, and the power to destroy obstacles.",
        "effects": [
            "Fearless, courageous demeanor; naturally confronts and eliminates life's obstacles.",
            "Strong physical constitution, well-defined jawline/bone structure, and military/athletic capability.",
            "Commands authority, leads projects decisively, and emerges victorious in competitive struggles."
        ]
    },
    "Mercury": {
        "id": "bhadra_yoga",
        "name": "Bhadra Mahāpuruṣa Yoga",
        "scripture_ref": "BPHS 75.5-8, Phaladeepika 6.2",
        "archetype": "The Auspicious Scholar & Organizer: Blessed with razor-sharp analytical intellect, hygienic cleanliness, and scholarly eloquence.",
        "effects": [
            "Extraordinary intelligence, vast retention of technical details, facts, and oratory eloquence.",
            "Praised by scholars and educated minds; master of commerce, calculation, writing, and counseling.",
            "Long life, disciplined hygienic habits, and exceptional organizational efficiency."
        ]
    },
    "Jupiter": {
        "id": "hamsa_yoga",
        "name": "Haṃsa Mahāpuruṣa Yoga",
        "scripture_ref": "BPHS 75.9-12, Phaladeepika 6.3",
        "archetype": "The Spiritual Swan & Preceptor: Revered by the virtuous, embodying moral clarity, spiritual wisdom, and righteous fortune.",
        "effects": [
            "Deep moral integrity (Dharma), broad-minded philosophical wisdom, and devotion to sacred ideals.",
            "Revered by teachers and spiritual seekers; natural preceptor, mentor, and protector of community resources.",
            "Peaceful disposition, graceful speech, and continuous divine fortune throughout life."
        ]
    },
    "Venus": {
        "id": "malavya_yoga",
        "name": "Mālavya Mahāpuruṣa Yoga",
        "scripture_ref": "BPHS 75.13-16, Phaladeepika 6.3-4",
        "archetype": "The Cultured Creator & Diplomat: Endowed with aesthetic refinement, luxurious comfort, deep patience, and serene senses.",
        "effects": [
            "Refined aesthetic discernment, love of fine arts, music, and luxurious surroundings.",
            "Deep patience (Dhriti), graceful magnetic presence, and tranquility of the senses (Prasannendriya).",
            "Fortunate in marriage, partnerships, wealth accumulation, and diplomatic relations."
        ]
    },
    "Saturn": {
        "id": "sasa_yoga",
        "name": "Śaśa Mahāpuruṣa Yoga",
        "scripture_ref": "BPHS 75.17-20, Phaladeepika 6.4",
        "archetype": "The Master of Discipline & Endurance: Respected by all social classes for peerless work ethic, surviving grueling trials where others fail.",
        "effects": [
            "Unshakeable discipline, emotional self-mastery, and willingness to tackle grueling, unglamorous responsibilities.",
            "Commands the loyalty of workers and subordinates; authority recognized across all walks of society (Sarvajanai).",
            "Exceptional longevity, resilience in adversity, and enduring institutional legacy built through patience."
        ]
    }
}

def detect_pancha_mahapurusha_yogas(chart: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[YogaInstance]:
    """Scans chart for Pancha Mahapurusha Yogas from Lagna and Chandra."""
    detected = []
    planets_data = get_planets_data(chart)
    moon_house = get_house_of_planet(chart, "Moon")
    house_rulers = get_house_rulers(chart)
    lagna_rulers = house_rulers.get(1) or []
    lagna_lord = lagna_rulers[0] if lagna_rulers else ""
    
    for p, config in MAHAPURUSHA_CONFIGS.items():
        if p not in planets_data:
            continue
            
        p_house = get_house_of_planet(chart, p)
        p_sign = get_sign_of_planet(chart, p)
        
        is_exalted = (p_sign == EXALTATION_SIGNS.get(p))
        is_own = (p_sign in OWN_SIGNS.get(p, []))
        
        if not (is_exalted or is_own):
            continue
            
        # Check angularity from Lagna (1, 4, 7, 10)
        is_kendra_lagna = p_house in (1, 4, 7, 10)
        
        # Check angularity from Moon
        moon_diff = 0
        is_kendra_moon = False
        if moon_house > 0 and p_house > 0:
            moon_diff = (p_house - moon_house) % 12 + 1
            is_kendra_moon = moon_diff in (1, 4, 7, 10)
            
        if not (is_kendra_lagna or is_kendra_moon):
            continue
            
        # Base plausibility: 80.0 for primary Lagna Kendra, 70.0 for secondary Chandra-only Kendra (indāvapi)
        score = 80.0 if is_kendra_lagna else 70.0
        positive_factors = []
        
        if is_exalted:
            positive_factors.append(f"{p} is exalted in {p_sign}.")
            score += 10.0
        elif is_own:
            positive_factors.append(f"{p} is in its own home sign {p_sign} (acts as its own dispositor).")
            score += 10.0
            
        if is_kendra_lagna:
            positive_factors.append(f"Angular prominence: Situated in Kendra House {p_house} from the Ascendant.")
            if p_house in (1, 10):
                score += 5.0  # Extra prominence on Lagna/Midheaven
            if is_kendra_moon:
                positive_factors.append(
                    f"Confluence: Also angular from the Moon (Kendra H{moon_diff} from Chandra), reinforcing experiential manifestation."
                )
                score += 5.0
        else:
            positive_factors.append(
                f"Lunar angularity (indāvapi): Situated in Kendra H{moon_diff} from the Moon (occupies House {p_house} from Lagna)."
            )
            
        if p == lagna_lord:
            positive_factors.append(f"Ascendant Lord merger: {p} rules the Lagna, identifying the native's core vitality with the archetype.")
            score += 10.0
            
        # Run Breaker Audits
        breakers = []
        
        # 1. Trishadaya intrusion
        breakers.extend(audit_trishadaya_interference([p], chart))
        
        # 2. Combustion
        breakers.extend(audit_combustion([p], chart))
        
        # 3. Dispositor condition (if exalted, check dispositor)
        if is_exalted:
            breakers.extend(audit_dispositor_vitality([p], chart))
            
        # 4. Dusthana placement from Lagna (applies when formed only from Chandra Kendra in H6, H8, or H12)
        dusthana_breaker = audit_dusthana_placement([p_house], YogaCategory.MAHAPURUSHA.value)
        if dusthana_breaker is not None:
            breakers.append(dusthana_breaker)

        # 5. Shadbala muscle
        shad_breakers, shad_pos = audit_shadbala_muscle([p], chart)
        breakers.extend(shad_breakers)
        positive_factors.extend(shad_pos)
        
        # Cap positive confluence score at 100.0 before deducting breakers
        score = min(100.0, score)
        for b in breakers:
            score -= b.penalty
            
        score = max(5.0, min(100.0, score))
        
        has_major_breaker = any(b.penalty >= 20.0 for b in breakers)
        if score >= 75.0 and not has_major_breaker:
            status = YogaStatus.PURE
        elif score >= 50.0:
            status = YogaStatus.STAINED
        elif score >= 30.0:
            status = YogaStatus.RESCUED
        else:
            status = YogaStatus.BROKEN
            
        detected.append(YogaInstance(
            id=config["id"],
            name=config["name"],
            category=YogaCategory.MAHAPURUSHA,
            status=status,
            plausibility_score=round(score, 1),
            participating_planets=[p],
            participating_houses=[p_house],
            scripture_ref=config["scripture_ref"],
            archetype=config["archetype"],
            manifestation_effects=config["effects"],
            positive_factors=positive_factors,
            breakers=breakers
        ))
        
    return detected

"""
jyotish/yogas/parivartana.py
Parivartana Yogas (Mutual House Sign Exchanges).
Scripture: Phaladeepika Ch. 6, Verses 32-34.
Classifies all 66 mutual sign exchanges into:
- 28 Mahā Parivartana (among 1, 2, 4, 5, 7, 9, 10, 11)
- 8 Khala Parivartana (3rd house with 1, 2, 4, 5, 7, 9, 10, 11)
- 30 Dainya Parivartana (involving 6, 8, or 12; including 3 Viparīta crossovers among 6, 8, 12)
"""

from typing import List, Dict, Any
from jyotish.yogas.models import (
    YogaInstance, YogaCategory, YogaStatus, YogaBreakerDetail
)
from jyotish.yogas.breakers import (
    get_house_of_planet, get_sign_of_planet, get_planets_data,
    audit_trishadaya_interference, audit_combustion, audit_shadbala_muscle,
    resolve_yoga_score_and_status, SIGN_LORDS
)

AUSPICIOUS_HOUSES = {1, 2, 4, 5, 7, 9, 10, 11}
DUSTHANA_HOUSES = {6, 8, 12}
CLASSICAL_PLANETS = ["Sun", "Moon", "Mars", "Mercury", "Jupiter", "Venus", "Saturn"]


def detect_parivartana_yogas(chart: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[YogaInstance]:
    """Scans for mutual sign exchanges among all 7 physical planets."""
    detected: List[YogaInstance] = []
    planets_data = get_planets_data(chart)

    for i in range(len(CLASSICAL_PLANETS)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(CLASSICAL_PLANETS)):
            p1, p2 = CLASSICAL_PLANETS[i], CLASSICAL_PLANETS[j]
            if p1 not in planets_data or p2 not in planets_data:
                continue

            s1 = get_sign_of_planet(chart, p1)
            s2 = get_sign_of_planet(chart, p2)
            if not s1 or not s2 or s1 == s2:
                continue

            # Mutual exchange (anyonyam): p1 in p2's sign AND p2 in p1's sign
            lord_s1 = SIGN_LORDS.get(s1)
            lord_s2 = SIGN_LORDS.get(s2)

            if lord_s1 == p2 and lord_s2 == p1:
                # Dual-sign rulership disambiguation: use actual houses occupied by p1 and p2
                h1 = get_house_of_planet(chart, p1)
                h2 = get_house_of_planet(chart, p2)
                if h1 not in range(1, 13) or h2 not in range(1, 13) or h1 == h2:
                    continue

                exchanged_houses = sorted([h1, h2])
                positive_factors = [
                    f"{p1} (in {s1} / H{h1}) and {p2} (in {s2} / H{h2}) exchange signs (H{exchanged_houses[0]} ⇄ H{exchanged_houses[1]})."
                ]
                breakers: List[YogaBreakerDetail] = []

                # Precedence per Phaladeepika 6.32-34:
                # 1. Any involvement of 6, 8, 12 -> Dainya (30 pairs)
                # 2. Otherwise involvement of 3 -> Khala (8 pairs)
                # 3. Otherwise among {1, 2, 4, 5, 7, 9, 10, 11} -> Mahā (28 pairs)
                has_dusthana = bool({h1, h2} & DUSTHANA_HOUSES)
                both_dusthana = (h1 in DUSTHANA_HOUSES and h2 in DUSTHANA_HOUSES)
                has_third = (h1 == 3 or h2 == 3)

                # Layer 2 secondary auditors (Combustion & Shadbala across all Parivartanas)
                comb_brk = audit_combustion([p1, p2], chart)
                shad_brk, shad_pos = audit_shadbala_muscle([p1, p2], chart)
                positive_factors.extend(shad_pos)

                if has_dusthana:
                    y_id = f"dainya_parivartana_{p1.lower()}_{p2.lower()}"
                    y_name = f"Dainya Parivartana Yoga ({p1} in {s1} ⇄ {p2} in {s2})"

                    if both_dusthana:
                        # 3 Viparita Crossover Pairs: {6,8}, {6,12}, {8,12}
                        base_score = 68.0
                        positive_factors.append(
                            f"Viparīta Crossover: Both exchanged houses (H{exchanged_houses[0]} & H{exchanged_houses[1]}) are Duṣṭhānas, turning mutual crisis into protective resilience."
                        )
                        archetype = "The Reversal Exchange (Dainya-Viparīta Crossover): Two Duṣṭhāna lords swap difficult houses (6, 8, 12), neutralizing external rivals and containing loss."
                        effects = [
                            "Converts adversity, debt, or sudden crisis into unexpected resilience and protection.",
                            "Classically grouped among the 30 Dainya exchanges, yet functions as a mutual Viparīta reversal."
                        ]
                        breakers.extend(comb_brk)
                        breakers.extend(shad_brk)
                        score = base_score - sum(b.penalty for b in breakers)
                        score = round(max(5.0, min(74.0, score)), 1)
                        y_status = YogaStatus.RESCUED if score >= 30.0 else YogaStatus.BROKEN
                    else:
                        # 27 Contaminating Dainya Pairs: one Dusthana + one non-Dusthana
                        base_score = 80.0
                        archetype = "The Trial of Adversity: Sign exchange involving Duṣṭhāna houses (6, 8, 12), producing unexpected upheaval or karmic friction."
                        effects = [
                            "Periodic cycles of sudden expenditure, debt, health vulnerability, or institutional friction.",
                            "Directs energy into problem-solving, service, or spiritual detachment through crisis."
                        ]
                        breakers.append(YogaBreakerDetail(
                            factor="Dusthana Exchange (Dainya)",
                            culprit_planet=f"{p1}/{p2}",
                            description=f"Exchange links House {exchanged_houses[0]} and House {exchanged_houses[1]}. Significations of the auspicious house absorb Duṣṭhāna trials.",
                            penalty=35.0
                        ))
                        breakers.extend(comb_brk)
                        breakers.extend(shad_brk)
                        score = base_score - sum(b.penalty for b in breakers)
                        score = round(max(5.0, min(70.0, score)), 1)
                        # Affliction yoga invariant: Dainya is NEVER YogaStatus.PURE
                        y_status = YogaStatus.STAINED if score >= 30.0 else YogaStatus.BROKEN

                elif has_third:
                    # 8 Khala Pairs: 3rd house with {1, 2, 4, 5, 7, 9, 10, 11}
                    y_id = f"khala_parivartana_{p1.lower()}_{p2.lower()}"
                    y_name = f"Khala Parivartana Yoga ({p1} in {s1} ⇄ {p2} in {s2})"
                    base_score = 80.0
                    archetype = "The Cycle of Effort: Sign exchange involving the 3rd house of hustle, alternating between struggle and self-made triumph."
                    effects = [
                        "Alternating phases of bold initiative, intense hustle, setbacks, and sudden self-made victories.",
                        "Grants enterprise and courage, though results fluctuate according to personal persistence."
                    ]
                    breakers.append(YogaBreakerDetail(
                        factor="3rd House Hustle Intrusion (Khala)",
                        culprit_planet=f"{p1}/{p2}",
                        description=f"Exchange links House 3 with House {h1 if h2 == 3 else h2}; fortunes fluctuate with personal effort.",
                        penalty=15.0
                    ))
                    breakers.extend(comb_brk)
                    breakers.extend(shad_brk)
                    score = base_score - sum(b.penalty for b in breakers)
                    score = round(max(5.0, min(74.0, score)), 1)
                    y_status = YogaStatus.STAINED if score >= 30.0 else YogaStatus.BROKEN

                else:
                    # 28 Mahā Pairs: among {1, 2, 4, 5, 7, 9, 10, 11}
                    y_id = f"maha_parivartana_{p1.lower()}_{p2.lower()}"
                    y_name = f"Mahā Parivartana Yoga ({p1} in {s1} ⇄ {p2} in {s2})"
                    base_score = 90.0
                    is_supreme_exchange = ({h1, h2} == {9, 10})
                    if is_supreme_exchange:
                        base_score += 5.0
                        positive_factors.append("Supreme Dharma-Karma Exchange (H9 ⇄ H10).")

                    archetype = "The Royal Exchange: Mutual reception between auspicious Kendra/Trikoṇa/Dhana houses, multiplying mutual prosperity."
                    effects = [
                        "Endows sovereign authority, wealth, vehicles, fame, and enduring societal respect.",
                        "Both planets behave as honored guests in each other's homes, mutually fortifying their promises."
                    ]

                    # Apply Layer 2 breakers for Mahā Parivartana per YOGA_ARCHITECTURE.md
                    exclude_trishadaya = (11,) if 11 in (h1, h2) else ()
                    breakers.extend(audit_trishadaya_interference(
                        [p1, p2], chart,
                        is_supreme_pairing=is_supreme_exchange,
                        exclude_houses=exclude_trishadaya
                    ))
                    breakers.extend(comb_brk)
                    breakers.extend(shad_brk)

                    score, y_status = resolve_yoga_score_and_status(base_score, breakers)

                detected.append(YogaInstance(
                    id=y_id,
                    name=y_name,
                    category=YogaCategory.PARIVARTANA,
                    status=y_status,
                    plausibility_score=score,
                    participating_planets=[p1, p2],
                    participating_houses=exchanged_houses,
                    scripture_ref="Phaladeepika 6.32-34",
                    archetype=archetype,
                    manifestation_effects=effects,
                    positive_factors=positive_factors,
                    breakers=breakers
                ))

    return detected

FILE: power_yogas.py
